atha dharmasaṃhitā prārabhyate


prathameśrīparaśivānugrahādupamanyunā |
prāptaṃ traikālikaṃjñānaṃsaṃkṣepāditivarṇyate || 1 ||

|| 2 ||

|| 2 ||

|| 3 ||

yodhattebhuvanānisatva guṇavānsraṣṭārajaḥsaṃśrayaḥ saṃhatītamasānvitoguṇamayīṃmāyāmatītyasthitaḥ |
satyānaṃtamanaṃtabodhama malaṃbrahmādisaṃjñāspadaṃnityaṃ tattvasamanvayādadhigataṃ pūrṇaśivaṃdhīmahi || 3 ||

sūtauvāca

ekadādevakīputraściṃtayanhasisthitaḥ |
pūjayāmāsavidhivadupamanyuṃ samāgatam || 4 ||

pracchasukhamāsīnaṃ kaṃpūjayasinityaśaḥ |

cirakālaṃmayātaptaṃ pūrvamevamahattapaḥ |
śaṃkaraḥkṛpayāviṣṭodevodarśanamāgataḥ || 6 ||

tribhiraṃśaiḥ śobhamānamajasrasukhamavyayam |
ekapādaṃmahādaṃṣṭrasajvālakava lairmukhaiḥ || 7 ||

dvisahasramayūkhānāṃ jyotiṣātivirājitam |
sarvāstrapravaraṃcādyamanekākṣaṃ sahasrapāt || 8 ||

yattatkalpāṃ tasamayeviśvaṃ saṃharatidhruvam |
nāvadhyoyasyacabhavedṛtedevājjanārdanāt || 9 ||


tapasthorudrapārśvasthaṃ dṛṣṭavānahamavyayam |
gulyamatraṃ paraṃ nānyattattulyama dhikaṃhivā || 11 ||



darpāviṣṭaśvageheyanniḥkṣipyalava ṇāsuraḥ |
śatrughnaṃnṛpatiṃ yuddhe samāhūyakṣayaṃgataḥ || 14 ||



sarpahastamanirdeśyaṃpāśahastamivāṃtakam |
paraśutīkṣṇa dhāraṃcasarpādyaiśvavibhūṣitam ||17||

kalpāṃtadahanākāratathāpuruṣavigraham |
yattadbhārgavarāmāyakṣatriyāṃtakaraṃraṇe || 18 ||

rāmoyaddalamāśrityapūrvedattaṃ kapardinā |
paraśuṃtaṃgṛhītvāsauśivadattaṃmahāmanāḥ || 19 ||

triḥsamakṛtvoyaḥ sacaṃddāharuṣito muniḥ |
sudarśanaṃtathācakraṃ sahasravadanaṃvibhum || 20 ||

dvisahasrabhujaṃ devaṃ dadarśapuruṣākṛtim |

vajraṃśakticatūṇīraṃkhapāśaṃyamaṃkuśam |
gadāṃcalokapālānāmastrāṇyetāni yānica || 22 ||

dadarśa tāni sarvāṇibhagavadrudrapārśvataḥ |
savyadeśetudevasyabrahmālokapitāmahaḥ || 23 ||

vāmapārśvasthitaścaivaśaṃkhacakragadādharaḥ || 24 ||

skaṃdaḥśaktiṃsamā dāyamayūrasthaḥ saghaṃṭakaḥ || devyāḥ samīpe saṃtasthaudvitīyaiva pāvakaḥ || 25 ||

naṃdīśūlaṃsamādāyabhavāgre samavasthitaḥ |
sarvabhūtagaṇāścaivamātarovividhāḥsthitāḥ || 26 ||

astuvanvividhaiḥ stotrairmahādevaṃtadāsurāḥ || 27 ||

yatkiṃciccajagatyasmindṛśyate śrūyate thavā |
tatsarvaṃbha gavatpārśvanirīkṣyāhaṃsuvismitaḥ || 28 ||

sumahadvairya mālaṃvyaprāṃjalivividhaiḥ stavaiḥ |

bhagavānatha suprītomāmāhaprahasanniva || 30 ||

navicālayituṃśa kyomayāviprapunaḥpunaḥ |
parīkṣitāṃsi bhadraṃte bhavānbhaktyāmadīyayā || 31 ||

tasmādvaraya bhadraṃ te varaṃ deveṣudurlabham |
sacataṃ prāṃjalirbhūtvāprāhabhaktyānukaṃpitam || 32||

bhagavanyadituṣṭosiyadibhaktiḥsthirāmayi |
tenasatyenamejñānaṃ trikālaviṣayaṃ vibho || 33||

prayacchabhaktiṃvipulatvayicāvyabhicāriṇīm |
sānvayasyāpinityaṃcabhūrikṣīraudanaṃvibho || 34 ||

mamā stutavasānnidhyaṃnityaṃcaivāśramevibho |
evamuktaḥ sataṃprāhavarjitastvaṃbhaviṣyasi || 35 ||

jarāmaraṇajaidapaiḥ sarvakāmapra doharaḥ |
munīnāṃ pūjanīya śvayaśodhanasamanvitaḥ || 36||

śīlarūpaguṇaiśvaryamatprasādātpadepade |
kṣīrodasāgarasyaiva sānnidhyaṃpayasāṃnidheḥ || 37||

tatratebhavitānityaṃyatrayatreccha semune |
amṛtātmatutatkṣīraṃyāvatsaṃyāmyatetataḥ || 38 ||

imaṃvaivasvataṃkalpaṃ paśyasevaṃdhubhiḥ saha |
tadgotraṃcākṣayaṃtvastupradā syāmisadaivahi ||39||

sānnidhyamāśrametubhyaṃmayibhaktiśvasusthirā |
dāsyāmidarśanaṃvatsasmṛtaścabhavatāsukham || 40 ||


mameśānovarāndatvāsūryakoṭisa maprabhaḥ |
evaṃdṛṣṭomayākṛṣṇayaduktaṃtena dhīmatā || 42 ||

tadavāmaṃca me sarvadevadevasamādhinā |

paśyavṛkṣānmanoramyānsnigdhapatrān sugaṃdhinaḥ || 44 ||

sarvartukusumairyuktān sadāpuṣpaphalānvitān |

prasādāddevadevasya viśvabhāvasamanvitam || 46 ||


|| 5 ||

upamanyuruvāca

mamāstitvakhilaṃjñānaṃprasādācchūlapāṇinaḥ |

tamahaṃdṛṣṭavāndevamapidevāsureśvaram |
yenapaśyaṃtyanārādhyaṃkonyodhanyataromayā || 2 ||

etatdhyāyaṃtividvāṃsoyattatparamamakṣaram || 3 ||

sarvatattvavidhānajñaḥ sarvatattvārthadarśanaḥ |


satena saṃharetsatsaṃjagatsthāvarajaṃgamam ||6||

yugatisarvabhūtānisaṃvartakaivānalaḥ |
kālobhūtvāmahādevograsamānaḥ satiṣṭhati || 7 ||

sarvagaḥ sarvabhūtātmāsarvabhūtodbhavo dbhavaḥ |
āstesarvagatādevoadṛśyaḥ sarvadaivataiḥ || 8 ||

atastvaṃputralābhāyasamārādhayaśaṃkaram |
yairbhavārādhanātprānaṃda tkāmaṃtacchṛṇuṣvaha || 9 ||

śarvātsarvāmaraiśvaryahiraṇyakaśipuḥ purā |
varṣāṇāṃdaśalakṣāṇisalabhaccaṃdraśekharāt || 10 ||

ta syāthaputrapravarovarṣāṃyutamayodhayat |
sacaśarvavarādiṃrdranaṃdanonāmaviśrutaḥ || 11 ||


naśastrāṇivi hatyaṃgevaradasta syadhīmataḥ |
grahasyātibalasyājaucakaṃ vajramukhānyapi || 13 ||


tuṣṭovi dyutprabhasyāpitrailokyeśvaratāmadāt |
śatavarṣasahasrāṇisarvalokeśvarobhavat || 15 ||


dhātrāssṛṣṭaḥśatamakhodai tyovarṣaśataṃpurā || 17 ||

tapaḥ kṛtvā sahasaṃtupurāṇamalabhadbhavāt |
yājñavalkyaitikhyātogītovedepurākila ||18||

ārādhyasamahādevaṃ prāptavānajñānamuttamam |
vedavyāsastuyonāmnāprāptavānatulaṃyaśaḥ || 19 ||

iṃdreṇavālakhilyāstu parībhūtāstuśaṃkarāt || 20 ||

lebhiresomahartāraṃgaruḍaṃsarvadurjayam |
āpaḥ pranaṣṭāḥ sarvāścapūrvaṃ roṣātkapardinaḥ || 21 ||

atrerbhāryācānasūyātrīṇivarṣaśatāni ca || 22||

musalemunirāhārāsumāśarvāttataḥsutān |
dattātreyaṃ muniṃlebhe caṃdra durvāsasaṃtathā || 23 ||

vikarṇaśvamahādevaṃ tathābhaktasukhāvaham |
prasādya bhagavāntsiddhiprāptavānmadhusūdana || 24 ||


tutoṣa bhagavānāhagraṃthakartābhaviṣyasi |
vatsākṣayācate kīrtitrailokye prabhaviṣyati ||26||

bhaviṣyasiṛṣiśreṣṭhasūtrakartātatastataḥ || 27 ||


graṃtharallokavi khyātobhavitāsyajarāmaraḥ || 29 ||

upamanyuruvāca

evaṃvidhomahādevaḥ puṇyaḥ pūrvataraiḥstutaḥ |

yesaṃtitānnaśaknomityapivarṣaśatairapi || 31 ||

sanatkumāra uvāca ||

etacchrutvāva castasyasobravīttaṃ mahāmunim |

vāsudeva uvāca ||

dhanyastvamasi vipreṃdra kastvattostīhapuṇyakṛt |
yasyadevātidevastesānnidhyaṃkuruteśrame || 33 ||

darśanaṃmuniśārdūladadyātsa bhagavānśivaḥ |
a pitāvanmamāpyevaṃprasādaṃvākarotvasau || 34 ||

upamanyuruvāca ||

tasyaivarupayā tvaṃvaidrakṣyasepuruṣottamam || 35 ||

ṣoḍaśāṣṭauvarāṃścaiva prāpsyasi tvaṃ maheśvarāt |
sapatnīkātkathaṃnādāttvaṃhidevo janārdanaḥ || 36 ||

pūjyosidaivataiḥsarvaiḥślāghanīyaḥ sadāguṇaiḥ |
japyaṃ catepradāsyāmiśraddadhānāyacācyuta || 37 ||

brahmaṇyaḥ śraddadhāna śvasatyaṃdrakṣyasiśaṃkaram |
ātmatulyabalaṃputraṃlabhiṣyasi maheśvarāt || 38 ||

devajñātosidṛṣṭosiputrakāmobhavānmama |
vyāsauvāca || evaṃkathayatastasyamahādevāśritāḥ kathāḥ || 39 ||

dinānyaṣṭauprayātāni muhūrtamiva tāpasāḥ |
naṃva me tu dine prāptemunināsacadīkṣitaḥ || 40 ||

maṃtramadhyāpitaḥśārvamatharvaśirasaṃ mahat |
jaṭī muṃḍīca saṃghīcababhūvaniyamānvitaḥ || 41 ||

muninādīkṣitaḥ kṛṣṇoyogaka kṣīcamekhalī |
māsamekaṃnirāhārodvitīyaṃ jalamātra bhuk || 42 ||

māsatrayaṃvāyubhakṣobabhūva susamāhitaḥ |
padāṃguṣṭho dhṛtatanuḥṣaṣṭhe cordhvabhujastathā || 43 ||

saṃprāneṣoḍaśemāsisaṃtuṣṭādarśanaṃdadau |
harau maheṃdracāpānyesarvaloke payodhare || 44 ||

brahmādyaiḥ stūyamānaṃtupūjitaṃ siddha koṭibhiḥ || 45 ||

divyamālyāṃbaradharaṃ bhaktinamaiḥ su rāsuraiḥ |
taṃdṛṣṭvāprāṃjaliḥ ruṣṇodevaṃta majamavyayam || 46 ||

sarvāścaryamayaṃ devaṃ vismayotphullalocanaḥ |
nānāvidhaiḥ stutipa daivaṅmayenārcayattadā || 47 ||

sahasranāmnādeveśaḥstutaḥ kṛṣṇena dhīmatā |

mumucuḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃtusādhuvārānmanogatān |
nikṣiptāpuṣpabṛṣṭistu vāsudevasya mūrdhani || 49 ||

siddhaiḥ samunibhicaiva apsa robhiḥ samaṃtataḥ |

uvāca

keśavaṃ tuṣṭo rudraścāthaviḍaujasaḥ |
kṛṣṇajānā mibhaktaṃtvāṃmayinityaṃdṛḍhavrataḥ || 51 ||

vṛṇīṣvāṣṭauvarānmattaḥ puṇyāṃtrailokyadurlabhān |

prāhadharmema tirnityaṃyaśaścāgryaṃbalaṃ mahat |
tvatsāmīpyaṃsthirābhaktistvayinityaṃ mamāstviti || 53 ||

putrāṇicadaśādyānāṃputrāṇāṃmamasaṃtuvai |
vadhyāśvaripavaḥ sarve saṃgrāmebaladarpitāḥ ||54||

yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ bhabeyamativa labhaḥ |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvātamāhabhagavānubhavaḥ || 55 ||

sarvabhaviṣyatītyevaṃ punaḥ saprāhaśūladhṛk |
sāṃbonāmamahāvīryaḥ putrastebhavitāvalī || 56 ||

ghoraṃsaṃvartakādityaḥśaptomunibhirevaca |
mānuṣobhavitāsītisateputrobhaviṣyati ||57||

yada nyatprārthitaṃkiṃcittattatsarvalabhasvaca |
satulabdhavaraḥ sarvān stutibhiḥsamatoṣayat || 58 ||

nānāvidhābhirvavhībhistāṃ praṇamyāthapārvatīṃ |
tamāhapārvatītuṣṭāmanojñānbhuvidurlabhān || 59 ||

gṛhāṇamattaśvavarānūkṛṣṇatuṣṭāsmitenagha |
de vitvaṃyadituṣṭāsivāsudevaṃ kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 60 ||

tadvadevāthatāṃprāhaceddadā sijaganmaye |
tapasātena satyena brāhmaṇānpratimā smabhūt ||61||

dveṣaḥkadāciṃdbharbratupūjayeyedvijānsadā |
tuṣṭaucamātāpitaraunityaṃ mamababhūvatuḥ || 62 ||

sarvabhūteṣvānukū lyaṃbhajeyaṃyatratatradaḥ |
kulaprasūtirucitāmamāstutabadśarnāt || 63 ||

tarpayeyaṃ sureṃdrādīn devānyajñaśatenatu |
yatīnāma tithīnāṃcasahasrāṇyathasaptaca || 64 ||

bhojayeyaṃsada gehe śraddhāpūtaṃtubhojanam |
bāṃdhavaiḥ sahaprītistunityamastusunirvṛtiḥ ||65||

devibhāryāsahasrāṇāṃ bhaveyaṃtrāṇavallabhaḥ |
akṣīṇaretāḥ kāmyaṃ catāstathāpāśrayaṃvapuḥ || 66 ||

annātpriyatarolo kebhaveyaṃsatyavādini |

evamastvitibhardrateśāśvatīsarvakāmadā |
tasmiṃstāṃścavarāndattvāpārvatīparameśvarau || 68 ||

tatraivāṃtardadhatustau kṛṣṇovai svāśramaṃyayau |

tathāvatsaṃcatasmaitatsamācaṣṭopa manyave |
satataṃprāhakonyaḥ syātśarvāddevājjanārdana || 70 ||

mahādānapatirlokeko dhenāpi suduḥsahaḥ |
jñānetapasiśauryevāsthairyevāpadbhyaeva ca || 71 ||

śṛṇuśaṃbhostugoviṃdadevāśvaryamahadyaśaḥ |
tacchrutvā śraddhayāyukto bhavacchaṃbhostubhaktimān || 72 ||

bhagavāna śaṃkaraḥ pūrvatra jhalokemahātmanā |

sāṃkhyāḥpaṭhaṃtitat stotraṃ vistīrṇacanighaṃṭumat |
durjñā naṃmānuṣāṇāṃtustotraṃkecitkacidbhuvi || 74 ||

stotreṇārādhitaḥśaṃbhuḥ prādāttasmaitrilocanaḥ |
varānyathepsitānpūrvanāma bhiramṛtopamaiḥ || 75||

labhiṣyasyuttamānkāmān gacchakṛṣṇagṛhaṃ sukhī |
ityuktastannamaskṛtya keśavodvārakāṃ yayau || 76 ||

anyeṣāmapiyaddattaṃpārvatyāśvayathepsitam |
śarvādyolabdhavān kṛṣṇamunīnāṃtacchṛṇuṣvame || 77 ||

lebheputraśataṃśarvātkīrti caivatuśāśvatīm ||78||


sīgnihetorvivādenagṛhītobrahmahatyayā |
brahmahātvamiti proktomunibhiḥ parivarjitaḥ || 80 ||

mokṣitaḥśaṃkareṇaivatejasvījagataḥ kṛtaḥ |
vedecapaṭhyatenityamadhyātmanirataḥśuciḥ || 81 ||

tathācabhārgavorāmotyārādhyatapasāvibhum |
nirīkṣyaduḥkhitaḥ śarvapitaraṃkṣatriyairhatam || 82 ||

tīkṣṇaṃsaparaśuṃ lebhenirdadāhacatenasaḥ |
triḥ saptakṛtvaḥkṣatraṃcaprasannātsamaheśvarāt || 83 ||

maheṃdraparvaterāmoghoraṃ kṛtvopapātakam |
ajeyaśvāmaraścaivasogrāpitapasāṃnidhiḥ ||84||

liṃgārcanaratonityaṃ dṛśyate siddhacāraṇaiḥ |
kalpatipunarevāsāvṛṣiḥ sthānamavāpsyati ||85||

asitasyānujaḥpūrvapīḍayākṛtavāṃstapaḥ |
mūḍhagrāheṇaviśvasyadevalonāmatāpasaḥ || 86 ||

puraṃdareṇaśatastuyaśaśvāyuśca susthiram |
adharmyadharmamalabhaliṃgamārādhyakāmadam || 87 ||

cākṣuṣaśvamanoḥ putraḥ sāmagānāṃcasaṃsadi |
krodhādbhṛtsa mar̤hanāmavasiṣṭhaḥ śaptavānmunim || 88 ||

ayajñayajanaṃsāmagṛṇāsigatacetanaḥ |
tasmāttiṣṭhamahāśāpaṃkiṃgāyasirathaṃtara m || 89 ||

varṣāṇadaṃḍakāraṇyemṛgaścaikovasiṣyasi || 90 ||

naṣṭapānīyaya vasesaṃvasatvaṃvanecara |
prajñayārahitoduḥkhīrurusaṃghanivāsite || 91 ||

sacaśāpābhibhūtaścadāruṇecamarusthale |
ayajñi yadrumadeśetatkṣaṇādabhavanmṛgaḥ || 92 ||

namaskārānnasaṃyuktaṃmṛgogṛtsamadogataḥ |
hṛdaye saṃsmaranubhaktyāpraṇavenayutaṃśi vam ||93||

tasmānmṛgamukhākārogaṇomṭagamukhaḥ kṛtaḥ |
ajarāmaratāṃnīta stīrtvāśāpaṃ punaśvasaḥ || 94 ||

śaṃkareṇamahābhāgonityaṃlaṃbodarānugaḥ |
liṃgārcanaratonityaṃ jaigīṣavyomahātapāḥ || 95 ||

bārāṇasyāṃmuniḥ pūrvaṃsthito bhogaparāṅmukhaḥ |
vihāyaca phalaṃ sarvaśubhāśubhasyakarmaṇaḥ || 96 ||

datvāṣṭaguṇamaiśvaryaṃśaṃkareṇavisarjitaḥ |
gārgyāyaprada dauśavamokṣadānbhuvidurlabhān || 97 ||

kāmānucārīmahākṣetrakālajñānaṃmahadhimat |
catuṣpādaṃsarasvatyāḥpāragatvaṃcaśā śvatam || 98 ||

tattulyaṃtusahasraṃtuputrāṇāṃpradadauśivaḥ |
vedavyāsaṃtuyogīṃdraṃ putraṃ tuṣṭaḥ pinākadhṛk || 99 ||

parāśarāyacadadau jarāmṛtyuvivarjitam |
māṃḍavyaḥśaṃkareṇaivajīvaṃ datvāvisarjitaḥ || 100 ||

varṣāṇāṃdaśalakṣāṇiśūlāgrādavaropitaḥ |
ādhibhirvyādhibhiḥśūlairvajitaśvatathārutaḥ || 1 ||

dhyāyannamaḥśivāyetiśivenaparamātmanā |
daridrobrāhmaṇaḥ kaśvinni kṣipyaguruveśmani || 2 ||

putraṃtugālavaṃ bālye pūrva māsīdgṛhāśrame |
guptāyāmagniśālāyāṃbhikṣurāyātitadgṛham || 3 ||

bhā ryāmuvāca

yaḥkaścidavaśyaṃnirdhanoyataḥ |
satuvācyobhavatyācanadṛśyataitipriyaḥ || 4 ||

kadācidatithiḥ kaścitkṣuttṛṣāśāmakarśitaḥ || 5 ||

tāmuvāca

bhartātekaga tacetitaṃcasā |
prāhabhartāmadīyastusāṃprataṃnacadṛśyate || 6 ||

gṛhasthaśvāthasaṃcha nastatraivasammṛtodvijaḥ || 7||

gṛhamāgamya mātuḥ saśrutvāśāpaṃ sudāruṇam. ||8||

ārādhyaśaṃkaradevamiṣṭikatvātuśāṃbhavīm |
gṛhādasau viniṣkrāṃtaḥ pitātaṃprāhasāṃjaliṃ || 9 ||

dhanavānputravāṃścaiva gṭatohaṃjīvitaḥ punaḥ || 10 ||

itivaḥ kathitamaśeṣānnāhaṃśaktaḥ samāsatovāpi |
vaktuṃ śaṃbhośvaguṇānśeṣasyevehamemukhānisyuḥ || 1.11 ||


||5||

munayaūcuḥ

sūtatvaṃbrūhinobhūyoyaduktamṭaṣisaṃsadi |
vyāsenatripura prastubuvatātacavaiguṇān || 1 ||

śrutvāhitānpriyānbrūhiyatphalaṃ prāpyateśive |
samarcitesamāsenaśrotumicchāmahevayam || 2 ||

sūtaputrāsurāstehika syānvayasamudbhavāḥ |
kathaṃ dadāha bhagavānnagarāṇi suradviṣām || 3||

trīṇyekena cabāṇenayugapattenavīryavān |

sūtauvāca ||

tārakākṣastutajjyeṣṭhovidyunmālīcamadhyamaḥ || 5 ||

kamalākṣaḥ kanīyāṃśrvasarve tulyabalāḥ sadā |
jiteṃdriyāḥsusannaddhāḥ saṃyatāḥ satyavādinaḥ || 6 ||

tetumeruguhāṃgatvātapaścakurmahādbhutam |
trayaḥpuṣpāṇibhogāṃścavihāyasumanoharān || 7 ||

vasaṃtesarvakāmāṃśvagītavādicaniḥsvanam |
grīṣmesūryaprabhāṃ jitvādi kṣu prajvālyapāvakam ||8||

tanmadhya saṃsthāḥsitdhyarthajuḍuvurhavyamādarāt |
mahāpratāpapatitāḥ sarve pyāsansamutthitāḥ || 9 ||

varṣā sugata saṃtrāsānadīṃmūrdhanyadhārayan |
śaratkāleprabhūtaṃtubhojanaṃtububhukṣitā || 10 ||

ramyaṃsnigdhaṃsthiraṃtyaṃ phalamūlamanu ttamam |
saṃyamātkṣuttṛṣaujitvāpānānyuccāvacānyapi || 11 ||

bubhukṣitebhyodatvātu babhūvurupalāiva |
saṃsthitāste mahātmā nonirādhārāścaturdiśam || 12 ||

hemaṃtegirimāśritya dhairyeṇaparameṇaca |
tuṣāradehasaṃchannājalaklinnenavāsasā || 13 ||

ācchādyadehaṃkṣaumeṇaśiśiretoyamadhyagāḥ |
anirviṇṇāstataḥ sarve kramaśovardhayaṃstapaḥ || 14 ||

evaṃtesaṃsthitādaityādivā rātramataṃdritāḥ |
muhūrtena samastatradaśavarṣasahasrakam || 15 ||

evaṃteṣāṃ gataḥ kālodharmeṇetimatirmama |
prādurāsīttatobrahmā surāsuragururmahān || 16 ||

taṃ tuṣṭaḥ saṃdarśaneteṣāṃ varaṃdātuṃmahāyaśāḥ |

kimarthaṃ tadaityeṃdrāḥ sarva kartāsmi sarvadā || 18 ||

sarvadāsyāmiyuṣmabhyaṃ kathayadhvaṃyadīpsitam |
tasyatadvacanaṃ śrutvāśanairyadyanmanogatam || 19||

ūcuḥ

prāṃjalayaḥ sarve praṇipatyapitāmaham |
bhagavannāstinoveśmaparākramavatāmapi || 22 ||

adhṛṣyāḥ śātravāṇāṃtuyatravatsyāmahe sukham |
tārakākṣa stataḥ prāha abhedyaṃyatsurairapi || 21 ||

karotuviśvakarmāyanmamahemamayaṃ puṃram |
yayāṃcekamalākṣa sturājataṃsumahatpuram || 22 ||

vidyunmālīcasaṃdṛṣṭovajrāyasamayaṃ mahat |
etadukātatodaityājajalpuramitaujasaḥ || 23 ||

hedeva divinānyatra veśmānyaccavṛṇīmahe |
yathābhimatameteṣāṃviśvakarmāṇamuktavān || 24 ||

satathetyevartateṣāṃ puraṃ kurupṛthakpṛthak |
purāṇitrīṇiramyāṇiviśvakarmācakāraha || 25 ||

evaṃ kurutvetyukto sau kāmacārīnitatkṣaṇāt |
kimuktenāpi bahunāphalamūlayutānica || 26 ||

udyānavanavṛkṣānnasvargācchataguṇānica |
nadīnadasaristrotaḥ puṣkaraiḥ śobhitānyapi || 27 ||

sarvakāmaphalāvṛkṣāḥsaṃtitatratyanekaśaḥ |
kṛtaiḥ puraiḥ prāhatuṣṭastahādaityānūpitāmahaḥ || 28 ||

tripureṣucadaityeṃdrāpraviśadhvaṃyathākramam |
sarvakāmasamṛddhīnidṛṣṭātānipurāṇyapi || 29 ||

parituṣṭaṃtukaṃ matvājīvitaṃvīkṣyavāsthiram |
tetamūcukhidhāmānaṃjīvitaṃvīkṣya caṃcalam || 30 ||

bhagavankipurairebhirvāṣpagadgadayā girā |
sthirīkurujagannāthapāṃtunaḥparipaṃthinaḥ ||31||

jarārogādayaḥ sarve māsmānmṛtyūra gātkvacit |
ajarāśvāmarāḥsarvebhavāmaitinomatam || 32 ||

sammṛtyavaḥ kariṣyāmaḥ kiṃkāryahipurottamaiḥ |
lakṣmyākiṃvā vipulayāsthānaiśvaryeṇavāpunaḥ || 33 ||

yatraivamṭatyunāgrastoniyataṃpaṃcabhirdinaiḥ |
tacchrutvāvacanaṃprāhanasajātojaniṣyate ||34||

ajaraścāmarolokebhaviṣyati mahītale |
ṛtetukhaṃḍaparaśārdevānnārāyaṇātkvacit || 35 ||

taudharmādharmaparamāva vyaktauvyaktarūpiṇau |
saṃpīḍanāyajagatoya disaṃkriyatetapaḥ || 36 ||

saphalaṃtadgataṃvidyāttasmātsuvihitaṃnayam |
tadvicārya svayaṃ butdhyānaśakyaṃyatsurāsuraiḥ || 37 ||

durlabhaṃvāsuduḥsādhyaṃ mṛtyuṃvaṃcayatānaghāḥ |
tatkicinmaraṇehetuṃvaṇīdhvaṃsatvamāśri tāḥ || 38 ||

yenamṛtyurna bhavatarikṣaṃtastatpṛthakpṛthak |

procuḥsaṃcitayi tvāthasarvalokapitāmaham |
pureṣutriṣucaiteṣuekasthānasthiteṣuca || 40 ||

madhyāhnābhijitekāle sītāṃśaupuṣyasaṃsthite |
varṣatsukālamegheṣupuṣkarāvartanāmasuṃ || 41 ||

sarvadevamayodevaḥ sarveṣāmeka helayā |
asaṃbhāvyeratheniṣṭhan sarvopaskaraṇānvite || 42 ||

asaṃbhāvyaikakāṃḍenabhinattunagarāṇinaḥ |
nirvairaḥ kṛttivāsā stuyosmākamitinityaśaḥ || 43 ||

vaṃdyaḥ pūjyobhivādyaśvasosmākaṃnirdahetkatham |
iticetasisaṃdhāyatādṛśobhuvidurlabhaḥ || 44 ||

brahmaṇācavarodattoyācitastairmahāvaraḥ |
tatolabdhavarāstetunivasaṃtismatatraha || 45 ||

sarvatrailokyamutsādya pravi śyanagarāṇite |
tato mahān gataḥ kālova satāṃ puṇyakarmaṇāṃ || 46 ||

yathāsukhaṃyathājopaṃteṣāṃ saṃkhyāvivarjitaḥ |
tataḥ kadā citrailokyadevāḥ śaraṇamabhyayuḥ || 47 ||

paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṃvidhvaste daityadānavaiḥ |

stotrairnānāvidhairdevaṃśūlinaṃkṛttivāsasam |
ūcurapratimaprajñābhyātṛbhyāṃsahitenatu || 49 ||

trailokyaṃsvavaśenītaṃtathācamunisattamāḥ || 50 ||

vidhvastāḥ sarvaśaḥsiddhāḥ sarvamutsāditaṃ jagat |
yajñakarmasamagraṃtu svayaṃ budhvācadāruṇaḥ || 51 ||

pravartitotyadharmastairṛṣīṇāṃcanivartitam |
avadhyesarvabhūtānāṃnīyatāṃcā surāsurāḥ || 52 ||

yāvannavaśagādaityairghoraikhipuravāsibhiḥ |
tāvadvidhīyatāṃnītiryayāsaṃrakṣyatejagat || 53 ||

teṣāṃ saṃbhā ṣamāṇānāṃprativākyamuvāca

saḥ |
jānāmidaityānvibudhā aśakyāstusurāsuraiḥ || 54 ||

duḥsādhastuvadhasteṣāṃ sarvaiṣāṃpuravāsinām |

suhṛddohe mahatpāpaṃ pūrvamuktaṃ svayaṃbhuvā |
brahmapprecasurāpecasteye bhagnavratetathā || 56 ||


nakiṃcidvidyatepāpaṃyasmāttvayogavittamaḥ |

rājñastasyanaṃtatpāpaṃvidyate dharmaevaca |
tasmādrakṣedvijān sādhūnkaṃṭakānvaiviśodhayet ||59||

evamicchedihāmutra rājācedrājyamātmanaḥ |
prabhustvaṃ sarvalokānāṃ tasmādrakṣasvamāciram ||60||

munīṃdreśāṃstathāyajñānvedānnāmnāsiśaṃkaraḥ |
etacchrutvāhataṃnāstirathaḥ sārathināsaha || 61 ||

yamāsthāyadhanurvāṇānnihaniṣyāmyahaṃvarān |
brahmācaitadvacaḥ śrutvānīpso haitumuvāca ha || 62 ||

saṃgrāmaṃgacchatastubhyaṃ sārathyaṃ karavāṇyaham |
tatastaṃ pṛthivīprāharathaṃmāṃkuru mānada || saparvatadvīpavatīṃ caturjaladhimekhalām ||63||

caṃdrārkāvūcaturmāṃtauratha cakretunau kuru |
bhagavanyoddhumāgacchaste patripuravāsinām ||64||

ābhāṣetāṃmahādevaṃśaṃbhuṃkulagirīvacaḥ |
gaṃdhamādanaviṃdhyaucavaṃśāvāvāṃkuruprabho ||65||

asmindivyeratheśaṃbhośaṃkaraṃprāhabuddhimān |
tatastvanaṃtonāgeṃdra māmakṣaṃ kurusāṃpratam || 66 ||

elāpatrobhujaṃgastupuṣpadaṃ taścatāvubhau |
agreaudhruvakāvāvabhivāvastava gauravāt || 67||

pūrva māṃ kurudurbhedyamuvāca

mala yogiriḥ |
devadeva jagannā thabhagavaṃśrcaṃdraśekhara || 68 ||

tataḥ saṃdhyāśca varṇastaṃtakṣakaḥ karburotravīt |
avināhaṃmahādeva kuru māṃsudṛḍharathe || 69 ||

vedava tānicatvāripuṇyānyūcurvaṣadhvajam |
yotrāṇi kurunastatra vedāṃgasahitānyapi || 70 ||

vedāḥ prāhurmahādevaṃ turagānnaḥ kuru prabho |
svāyaṃbhuvānmahāvegāndurbheyāṃścakutārkikaiḥ || 71 ||

upavedāstatodevaṃ jajalpuraparājitāḥ |
asmānkhalīnānbaṃdhe putatrasaṃyamanekuru || 72 ||

sāvitrīsahitāprāhagāyatrī kṛttivāsasam |
pragrahaṃtvanayāsārdhaṃdurādharṣaṃkuruṣvamām || 73 ||

praṇavastvabravīccha va pratodaṃkuruśāśvatam |

uddhartuyadiśakropikarasthaṃ maṃdarācalam ||75||

vāsukiḥpraṇataḥ prādurbheyaṃdevadānavaiḥ |
guṇaṃmāṃ kurudeveśata kaṃṭhastho pināgarāt || 76 ||

bhūtādibhūtanidhanaḥ paramātmājanārdanaḥ || 77 ||

asminyuddhemahādaityaiḥ śaraṃmāṃkurudāruṇam || 78 ||

kruddhaḥkāmaripuṃprāhacaṃḍadordaiḍamaṃḍitam |

kālovaiśvānarorudrolelihānobhayānakaḥ |

hasatastasya ghorasya ghorāniśveruraciṣaḥ |

tatobhūtapiśācāśvaruvaṃtismamahāravam |
vyādhayaśvayamaṃjitvāpurānītātyanekaśaḥ || 83 ||

rākṣasāvividhākārāḥ karmajāśvābhiyonijāḥ |
ratigrahnāśvavividhāstathākuhakaṭattayaḥ || 84 ||

daityānāṃvaralabdhānāṃteṣāṃyebādyasaṃyutāḥ |
sarvenimeṣatodagdhāstadātripuravāsinām ||85||

dadyamānasyatasyāthaviṣṇustaṃpunarabravīt |
alaṃyadasti tekiṃcitkāraṇaṃtattvayākṛtam || 86 ||

nirdahiṣyasitatsainyaṃbhavakāṃḍāgrataḥsthitaḥ |

tatovāyurmahādevamuvāca

catathāśaśī |
pakṣayormāṃtathāpuṃkheniyojayaśarevibho || 88 ||

vyomālikajjalaḥ śyāmaḥ kuruṣva yadi manyase |
niyojayāgradhārāyāṃyamaḥ prāhamaheśvaram || 89 ||

uvāca

bhīmaṃsaṃhasyapradhānātuśatadā |
athavidyutsaha srāṇāāṃkuruśalyaviśodhinīm || 90 ||

tataḥ saṃvartakāmeghāḥ procuḥ śaṃbhokuruṣvanaḥ |
dhvajemerau śubhestaṃbhedevānāmālayevibho || 91 ||

etacchrutvātu sarveṣāṃtataḥ sarvacakāra saḥ |
saṃmaṃdhyadaivataiḥ sārdhaśaraṇyobhaktavatsalaḥ || 92 ||

rathaṃrutvātatastvaṣṭāsaṃ pādayatabrahmaṇe |
śaṃbhavesaunivedyātharopayāmāsaśūlinam || 93 ||

tatastasminrathedivyedivyagaṃdharvapannagaiḥ |
stūyamā nomunigaṇaiḥ samārūḍha triśūladhṛk || 94||

śārvasthānakamāsthāyasaṃdhāyacaśarottamam |
sajjaṃtatkārmukaṃkṛtvāpratyālīḍhaṃ mahādbhutam || 95 ||

niveśyadṛṣṭi muṣṭaucamuṣṭidṛṣṭauniveśyaca |
atiṣṭhanniścalastatraśataṃvarṣasahasrakam || 96 ||

tatoṃguṣṭhega ṇādhyakṣaḥsatudannaniśaṃsthitaḥ |
nalakṣyaṃviviśustānipurāṇyasyacapūjitaḥ || 97 ||

tatoṃtarikṣādaśṭaṇoddhanurbāṇadharoharaḥ |
muṃjakeśovirūpākṣovācaṃparamaśobhanām || 98 ||

bhobhonayāvadbhagavānarcito sauddivigrahaḥ |
purāṇajagadīśeśasāṃprataṃnadahiṣyasi || 99 ||

etacchrutvātuvacanaṃgajavakramapūjayat |
bhadrakālīṃsamāhūya tatoṃdhakaniṣūdanaḥ || 100 ||

tasmintsaṃpūjitetsṛṣṭeparituṣṭapuraḥsare |
vināyaketatovyomnidadarśabhagavānharaḥ || 101 ||

purā ṇitrīṇidaityānāṃyuktānicayayātatham |
abhīlākhyemuhūrtetudhanurniṣkṛṣyasodbhutam || 2 ||

kṛtvājyātalanirghoṣaṃnādamatyaṃ tadustaram |
ātmanonāmaviśrāvyasamābhāṣyamahāsurān || 3||

mārtaṃḍakoṭivapuṣaṃkāṃḍamugromumocaha |
dadāhatripurasthāṃ stāntrī ndaityānvimalāpahā ||4||

tataḥpurāṇibhagnānicaturjaladhimekhalām |
gatāniyugapadbhūmiṃ trīṇidagdhānibhasmaśaḥ || 5 ||

daityāśvaśataśodagdhāstasyavāṇāgravanhinā |
tārakākṣastvanirdagdhobhrātṛbhyāṃsahito bhavat ||6||

mahādevaṃ samuddiśya tamūcurmanasāśanaiḥ |
bhaktyāparamayāyuktaḥpralapanvividhagiram || 7||

bhavajñātosituṣṭosiyadasmākaṃ manogatam |
karaṇī yaṃkṛtaṃtvadyadṛṣṭastavaparākramaḥ ||8||

bhagavanyadituṣṭosiyajvāsmāntsa habaṃdhubhiḥ |
tena satyena bhūyopikadātvaṃpradahiṣyasi || 9 ||

durlabhaṃlabhyamasmābhiryadaprāpyaṃ surāsuraiḥ |
tvadbhāvabhāvitābuddhirjātājātaṃbhavatviti || 10 ||

ityevaṃvibuvaṃtastedānavāstena vahninā |
anyepibālavṛddhāśvanirdagdhābhasmasātkṛtāḥ || 1.1 ||

striyovāpuruṣovāpivāhanānicatatraye |
sarvetenāgnināda gdhāḥ kalpāṃta jagadyathā || 12||

bhartṛkaṃṭhagatāṃkṛtvākāściddagdhavarastriyaḥ |
kāścitputrānavacchādyabāhubhyāṃ parigṛtyaca || 13||

dagdhāḥpalāyamānāśvanirdagdhāḥ śataśoparāḥ |
kāśvitsuptāḥ pramattāścaratiśrāṃtāśvayoṣitaḥ || 14 ||

ardhadagdhāvibuddhā śvababhramumahamūrcchitāḥ |
tenanāsītsusūkṣmopighorastripuravanhinā || 15||

avidagdhovinirmuktaḥ sthāvarojaṃgamopi vā |
varjayitvābhayadaityaṃviśvakarmāṇamavyayam || 16 ||

aviruddhaṃtu devānāṃrakṣitaṃviṣṇutejasā |
saṃnipāto hiyeṣāṃ novi yatenāśakārakaḥ || 17||

daivāttadvatpadārthānābhāvābhāvekṛtākṛte |

nasaṃyogaḥkadāyeṣāṃbhūyācipuravāsinām || 19||

matametaddhisarveṣāṃ daivādyarhiya tobhavet |
tataḥ sa bhagavāndrodagdhvātripuravāsinaḥ || 20 ||

kṛtakṛtyomahāyogībrahmādyairabhipūjitaḥ |

tasmiṃścāṃtarhite devemunigaṃdharva kiṃnarāḥ |
nāgāḥ sarpāśrvāpsarasaḥ saṃdṛṣṭāścāyamānuṣāḥ || 22 ||

svasvasthānamanuprāpyanirvṛtiṃpara māṃyayuḥ |
etadvaḥ kathitaṃsaṃ vaidhanyaṃśatrukṣayaṃkaram || 23 ||



māhātmyametadṛṣabhadhvajasya śrutvāmunergadhavatīsutasya |
vacomahārthemu nayaśvasarvepapracchurugraṃpraṇipatyasūtam || 1 ||

munayaūcuḥ ||

kimaṃdhakārirbhagavānṛpinākīkasyānvayevīryavataḥ pṛthivyā m |
jātomahātmābalavānpradhānekaśvāṃdhakaḥ kasya sutaḥ kathaṃvā || 2 ||

etatsamagraṃ sarahasya madyabravīhisūta bhagavatprasādāt |
vyāsāttvayāvaividitaṃhisamyakyuddhaṃ yathābhūdanayorhataḥ saḥ || 3 ||

teṣāṃtadāsauvacanaṃniśamyaprovāca sūtastumunīṃstadānī m |
pūrvaṃ dvijāmaṃdaraśailasaṃsthāḥ kapardina khaṃḍa parākramasya || 4 ||

cakretatonetranimīlanaṃtusāpārvatīna rmayutaṃ salīlam |
pravālahemāja dhṛtaprabhābhyāṃkarāṃbujābhyāṃnimimīlanetre || 5 ||

harasya netreṣunimīliteṣukṣaṇenajātaḥ sumahāṃdhakāraḥ |
ta tsparśayogācca maheśvarasyakarāccatasyāskhalitaṃmadāṃbhaḥ || 6 ||

śaṃbhorlalāṭeśaṇava nhitamovinirgato bhūrijalasyabiṃduḥ |
garbhobabhūvāthakarānanasyabhayaṃkaraḥ krodhaparaḥ kṛtaghnaḥ || 7 ||

anyovirūpījaṭilaḥśmaśrulaśvakṛṣṇonarovaivikṛtaḥ suromā |
gāyanahasanprarudannṛtyamānovi lelihānoghanaghoraghoṣaḥ ||8||

jātenatenādbhutadarśanena gaurīṃbhavosausmitapūrvamāha |
nimīlyanetrāṇikṛtaṃcanamavikṣepa se māṃdayite kathaṃtvam || 9 ||

gaurīharāttadvacanaṃ niśamyavihasyamānāpramumocanece |
jāteprakāśesatighorarūpojātoṃdhakārādapinetrahīnaḥ || 10 ||

papraccha koyaṃ bhagavanvirūpastādṛgvidhaṃtacca nirīkṣyabhūtam |
vadasvasatyaṃmamakiṃnimattaṃsṛṣṭothavā krenacakasyaputraḥ || 11 ||

śrutvāha rastadvacanaṃpriyāyāutpannaeṣo'dbhuta caṃḍavīryaḥ |
nimīlitecakṣuṣīmebhava tyāsa svedajaśyāṃdhakanāmadheyaḥ || 12 ||

tato'sya ka rtāsmiyathānurūpaṃ tvayāsa sakhyā sadayaṃgaṇebhyaḥ |
sarakṣitavyastvayanaṃhicaikaṃ vicāryabuddhyākaraṇīyamārye || 13 ||

gaurītato bhartṛvaconiśamyakāruṇyabhāvātsahitāsakhībhiḥ |
nānāprakārairbahubhirdyupāyaiśvakārarakṣasvasutasyayadvat || 14 ||

kāle 'thayasminbhuvicāṃdhakastuhiraṇyane trastvathaputrakāmaḥ |
araṇyamāśritya tapaścakāra jātastadākaśyapajaḥ sutārthaṃ || 15 ||

tuṣṭaḥ pinākītapasā'syasamyagvarapradānāyayayaudvijeṃdrāḥ || 16 ||

tuṣṭastamāhākuliteṃdriyaṃtaṃkimarthametadvatamāśrirtate |
prabrūhikāmaṃ varadobhavohaṃyadicchasi tvaṃ sakalaṃ dadāmi || 17 ||

putrastu maicaṃdralalāṭanāsti suvīryavāndaityakulānurūpī |
tadarthametadvratamāsthito'haṃ taṃ dehi deveśasuvīryavaṃtam || 18||

yasmācca madbhā turanaṃtavīryāḥprahrādapūrvāapipaṃcaputrāḥ |
mamehanāstītiniranvayo'haṃyomāmakaṃrājyamidaṃbubhūṣet || 19 ||

rāṣṭraṃ parasya svabalenahṛtvā bhuṃkte'thavāsvaṃ piturevadiṣṭam |
saprocyateputraihāpyamutraputrīsa tenāpibhavetpitāsau || 20 ||

urddhagatiḥ putra vatāṃniruktāmanīṣibhirdharmavatāṃ variṣṭhaiḥ |
sarvāṇi bhūtānitadarthameva mṛtau pravartatihaṭhātsvabhāvataḥ || 21 ||

niranvayasyāpina saṃtilokāstadarthamincchaṃtisutān svabhūmre |
krītādayaḥ saṃtisutāstathā'ṣṭauicchāmitasmātsutamekameva || 22 ||

etadbhavastadvacanaṃniśamyakṛpākaroṃ daitya nṛpasya tuṣṭaḥ |
tamāhadaityādhipanāstiputrastvadvīryajaḥ kiṃtudadāmiputram || 23||

mamā tmajaṃtvaṃdhakanāmadheyaṃtvattulyavīryaparājitaṃca |
vṛṇīṣvaputraṃ sakalaṃvihāya duḥkhaṃpratīcchasva sukhaṃ hicemam || 24 ||

ityeva muktāpraddausatasmai hiraṇyane trāyasutaṃprasannaḥ |
harastugauryāsahitomahātmābhūtādhināthatripurāriruyaḥ || 25||

tatoharāyā pyasutaṃsadetyaḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyayathākrameṇa |
stotrairanekairabhipūjya rudradṛṣṭaḥ svarājyaṃgatavānmahātmā || 26 ||

tatastuputraṃgiri śādavāpyarasātalaṃcaṃḍa parākramastu |
imāṃdharitrīmanayatsvadeśaṃ daityovijityatridaśānaśeṣān || 27 ||

tatastudevarmunibhi śvasarvaiḥsarvātmakaṃyajñamayaṃkarālam |
vārāhamāśrityavapuḥpradhānamārādhitoviṣṇuranaṃtavīryaḥ || 28 ||

ghoṇā prahārairvividhai rdharitrīṃvidāryapātālatalaṃpraviśya |
tuṃḍena daityān śataśovicūrṇyadaṃṣṭrābhirayābhirakhaṃḍitābhiḥ || 29 ||

pādaprahārairaśanipra kāśairunmathya senyāniniśācarāṇām |
mārtaṃḍa koṭipratimenapaścātsudarśanenā'dbhutacaṃḍatejāḥ || 30 ||

hiraṇyanetrasyaśi rojvalaṃtaṃcicchedadaityāṃ śradadāhaduṣṭān |
tataḥ prahṛṣṭoditijeṃdrarājye tamaṃdhakaṃtatrasacā'bhyaṣicat || 33 ||

svasthānamāsādya tato dharitrīṃdaṃṣṭrāṃkureṇādadharatprahṛṣṭaḥ |
bhūmicapātālatalānmahātmāpupoṣabhāgaṃtvatha pūrva kaṃtu || 32 ||

pratāpyalokānakhilāṃ stato'sausaṃbhāgataḥpadmayoniṃdadarśa |
varaṃ pradātuṃ pravadaṃtamevavaraṃvṛṇīṣvetisamūḍhabuddhiḥ || 33 ||

mṛtyorbhayaṃ me bhagavansadaivapitāmahā'bhūnna kadācidevam |
śastrāstrapāśāśaniśuṣka vṛkṣa girīṃdratāyāgniripuprahāraiḥ || 34 ||

svargedharaṇyāṃ divaseniśāyāṃdaivāhataḥ prāhana sopisaṃdhau |
tasyaitadīdṛgvacanaṃ niśamya daityeṃdratuṣṭo'smilabhasvasarvaṃ || 35 ||

praṇa myaviṣṇuṃmanasātamāhabhayānvitosāvapipadmayoniḥ |
tataḥ sa daityaḥ paripūrṇa kāmastvamaṣṭakoṭyastvathaṣaṇṇavatyaḥ ||36||

utti ṣṭharājyaṃ kurudānavānāṃśrutvāgiraṃtāṃsasukhībabhūva |
rājyebhiṣiktaḥ prapitāmahenatrailokyanāśāya matiṃcakāra ||37||

utsāya dharmān sakalānpramattojitvāhave so 'pisurānsamastān |
tatobhayādiṃdramukhāśvadevāḥ kṣīrodadhauyatraharistuśete || 38 ||

ārādhayāmāsuratīva gatvātatastu tdṛṣṭaḥ pradadauvarāṃstu |
utthāyatasmācchayanādupeṃdroyathānurūpairvividhairvacobhiḥ || 39 ||

ā śvāsyadevānakhilānmunīṃdrāravīṃduvaiśvānaratulyatejāḥ |
āśrityarūpaṃjaṭilaṃkarālaṃdaṃṣṭrāyudhaṃtīkṣṇanakhaṃ sunāsaṃ || 40 ||

mṛgeṃdratulyaṃcavidāritāsyaṃ mārtaṃḍakoṭipratimaṃsughoram |
yugāṃtakālāgnisamaprabhāvaṃjaganmayaṃ kiṃva hubhirvacobhiḥ ||41||

astaṃravausopihigacchamānegatosurāṇāṃnagarīmahātmā |
hatvācayuddhaṃ pravalaiḥ sahāyairhatvāthatāndaityagaṇāngrahītum ||42||

baśrāmatatrādbhutavikramaśvabhaṃjanvanaṃtānasurāṃścasiṃhaḥ |
dṛṣṭaḥ sadaityairatula prabhāva staṃ menirete hitathaiva sarve || 43 ||

siṃhaṃcataṃ sarvamayaṃ nirīkṣya prahār̤hanāmāditijeṃdraputraḥ |
uvācarājānamayaṃmṛgeṃdrā jaganmayaḥ kiṃcidalaṃraṇena || 44 ||

praviṣṭaeṣobhagavānanaṃtonnṛsiṃhamātrona garaṃtvidaṃte |
nivartyayuddhāccharaṇaṃprayāhipaśyāmisiṃhasya karālamūrtim || 45 ||

ya smānnayoddhābhuvanatraye'pikuruṣvarājyaṃtunamanmṛgeṃdram |
śrutvāsaputrasya vacodurātmāsaṃgṛtyatāṃsiṃhamimaṃbhavadbhiḥ ||46||

ta māhabhīto'sikimatracakrevīraivirūpaṃ bhṛkuṭī mukhaṃ tu |
tasyājñayādaityava rāstataste grahītukāmāviviśurmṛgeṃdram ||47||

kṣaṇe nadagdhāḥ śalabhāivāgnirūpābhilāṣātpravivikṣavovai |
daityeṣudagdheṣvapidaityarājaśvakārayuddhasammṛgādhipena || 48 ||

śastraiḥ samagrairakhilaistathāstraiḥśantyṛṣṭipāśāṃkuśapāvakādyaiḥ |
saṃyudhyatoreva tayorjagāmabrāhmaṃdinaṃśastranakhāstrapāṇyoḥ || 49||

ta taḥ sadaityaḥ sahasāvahūṃścarutvā bhujānaśastrayutānnirīkṣya |
nṛsiṃharūpaṃ prayayaumṭageṃdrasaṃyudhyamānaṃtvaharatsamaṃtāt ||50||

tataḥ saḥ yuddhaṃvvatiduḥsahaṃtuśastraiḥ samastaiśvatathāmahāstraiḥ |
kṣayaṃgataiḥ śūladharopyupāyāttatogṛhīta. sammṛgādhipena || 51 ||

bhujairanekaigi risāravadbhirnidhāya kaṃṭhasa bhujāṃtareṣu |
nakhāṃkurairdāraṇa marmavidbhirnakhākhattpadmamasṛgvimiśram ||52||

utpāṭyajīvādviga taḥkṣaṇenatyaktastadānīṃsatukāṣṭhabhūtaḥ |
pādaprahārairatitāḍito'sau punaḥ punaścūrṇitasarvagātraḥ || 53 ||

tasminhatedeva ripautra sannaḥprakrādamāmaṃtryakṛtapraṇāmam |
rājyebhiṣicyā'dbhutavīryaviṣṇustataḥ prayātogatimapratayam ||54||

tato hiraṇyākṣasu taḥ kadācitsaṃśrāvitonarmayutaṃkadācit |
tairbhrātṛbhiḥ saṃprayutovihāreki maṃdharājyenatavāgyakāryam || 55 ||

hiraṇyane trastu babhūvamūḍhaḥkalipriyanetravihīnameva |
yolabdhavāṃstvavikṛtaṃ virūpāṃghāraistapobhirgiriśaṃprasāya || 56 ||

sa tvaṃnabhāgīditirājarājñaḥkimanyajātopilabhetarājyaṃ |
teṣāṃ vākyāniniśamya tāninivāryabuddhyāsvayameva dīnaḥ ||57||

tāṃśāṃtayitvāvividhairvacobhirgatastvaraṇyaṃniśinirjanaṃtu |
varṣāyutaṃtatratapaścakārajajāpa japyaṃvidhṛtaikapādaḥ || 58 ||

ā hārahīnoṃniyatordhvabāhuḥ kartuṃnaśakyaṃhisurāsurairyat |
prajvālya vanhiprajuhotimātraṃ samāṃsa raktasyacavarṣamātram || 59 ||

tīkṣṇenaśastreṇanikṛttadehāt samaṃtrakaṃ pratyahamevahutvā |
snāyvasthiśeṣaṃkuṇapaṃtadāsaukṣayaṃgataṃśoṇitameva sarvaṃ || 60 ||

yadā syamāṃsāni na saṃtidehe prakṣe mukāmastu hutāśanāya |
tataḥsadṛṣṭastridaśālayairjanai nivāritovākyaśatairnayoktaiḥ || 61 ||

nivā rayitvāprapitāmahastaṃprovācahṛdyaṃpravaraṃvṛṇīṣva |
yacchāmikāmāṃstava sarvaloke sudurlabhāndānavatāngṛhāṇa || 62||

sa padmayone stuvaconiśamyaprovāca dīnaḥ praṇatastu daityaḥ |
yairniṣṭuremaipaddhataṃturājyaṃ pralhāda mukhyāmamasaṃtubhṛtyāḥ || 63 ||

aṃdha syadivyaṃhitathāstu cakṣuriṃdrādayaste karadā bhavatu |
mṛtyustu mābhūnmama deva daityagaṃdharvayakṣoragamānuṣebhyaḥ || 64 ||

nārāyaṇā- dvāditijeṃdraśatroḥsarvātmakātsarvamayācca sarvaḥ |
śrutvā vacastasyasudāruṇaṃ tat suśaṃkitaḥ padmabhavasta māha || 65 ||

daityeṃdrasarva bhavitātadetatvināśahetuṃcagṛhāṇakiṃcit |
yasmānnajātonajaniṣyate vāyonapraviṣṭo mukhamaṃtakasya || 66 ||

atyaṃta dī khalujīvitaṃtubhavādṛśāḥ satpuruṣāstyajaṃti |
etadvacaḥ sānunayaṃniśamyapitāmahāvyāhapunaḥ sadaityaḥ || 67||

kālatrayeyāśvabhavaṃtināryaḥśreṣṭhāśvamadhyāśvatathākanīyāḥ |
tāsāṃmadhyeratnabhūtātuyāsyānmamāpi nityaṃjananīvakācit ||68||

kāyenavācāmanasāpyagamyānārīnṛlokasyacadurlabhāca |
tāṃkāmayānasya mamāstunāśodaityeṃdra bhāvāt bhagavantsva yaṃbhūḥ || 69 ||

vākyaṃ tadākarṇyasapadmayoniḥ suvismitaḥ prāhatatodhakasya |
yatkāṃkṣa sedaityavarāstu te vai sarvaṃ bhavatyeṣavacaḥ sakā maḥ || 70 ||

uttiṣṭhadaityeṃdralabhasvakāmaṃ sadaiva vīrastu kuruṣvayuddham |
śrutvātadedṛgvacanaṃsaṃdaityaḥ snāyvasthiśeṣastutamāhadevam ||71||

kathaṃvibhovairibalaṃpraviśyatyanena dehena karomiyuddham |
snāyvasthiśeṣaguṇamāṃsapuṣṭaṃkareṇapuṇyena bhaveyathā || 72 ||

śrutvā vacastasyacapadmayoniḥ kareṇasaṃspṛśyacataccharīram |
gataḥ sureṃdraiḥ sahitaḥ svadhāmasaṃpūjyamānomunisiddhasaṃghaiḥ || 73 ||

saṃspṛṣṭamātraḥ sacadaityarājaḥ saṃpūrṇadehobalavānbabhūva |
saṃjātanetraḥ subhagobabhūvatdṛṣṭaḥ svamevaṃ nagaraṃviveśa || 74 ||

utsṛjya rājyaṃ sakalaṃ catasmai pralhāda mukhyāstvathadānaveṃdrāḥ |
tamāgataṃ labdhavaraṃca matvābhṛtyābabhūvurvaśagāścatasya ||75||

tatodhakaḥ svargamagāt vijetuṃ samādhiyuktaḥ sahabhṛtyavagaiḥ |
yatrākhiletatra surānsa mastāna kara pradevajradharaṃcakāra || 76 ||

nāgānsu parṇānvararākṣasāṃśvagaṃdharvayakṣānapi mānuṣāṃstu |
girīṃdravṛkṣāṃstṛṇajātisarvāṃścatuṣpadānsiṃhamukhānvijitya || 77 ||

trailokyametaddhicarācaraṃvaivaśyaṃcakārātmanisaṃniyojya |
tato'nukūlāni sudarśanāninārīsahasrāṇibahūnihṛtvā || 78 ||

rasātalādvṛtyatathādharāyāstriviṣṭapādyāḥ suvaśāḥ surūpāḥ |
tābhiryutoyyeṣusaparvateṣurarāma ramyeṣunadītaṭeṣu || 79 ||

||21||

krīḍāyamānaḥsatumadhyavartītāsa praharṣādayadānaveṃdraḥ |
tatpītaśiṣṭānipibanpramattodivyānipeyāni sumānuṣāṇi ||80||

anyāni divyānituṣaḍrasāni phalānimūlāni sugaṃdhavaṃti |
saṃprāpyayānānisuvāhanānimayenassṛṣṭānigṛhottamāni ||81||

puṣpārdhadhūpānna vilepanaiśvasuśobhitānyadbhutadarśanaiśva |
saṃkrīḍamānasya gatānitasyavarṣāyutānīhatathā'dhakasya ||82||

jānātikiṃcinnaśubhaṃparatrayadātmanaḥsaukhyakaraṃ bhaveddhi |
tataḥ pramattastusutānpradhānān kutarkavādairabhibhūyasarvān || 83 ||

cacāradaityaḥ sahitomahātmāvināśayan vaidikasarvadharmān |
vedadvijānvittamadābhibhūtonamanyateso'pyamarāngurūṃśva || 84||

namanyate daivahatogatāyuḥ svalpairahobhirgamayanvayaśca |
tataḥ kadācidgatavānsa sainyābahuprayātāpṛthivītale'smin || 85 ||

anekasaṃkhyāapivarṣakoṭyaḥ praharṣito maṃdaraparvataṃtu |
svargepamāṃtatra nirīkṣyaśobhāṃbabhrāma sainyaiḥsahapānamattaḥ || 86 ||

krīḍārthamāsādya cataṃ girīṃdramatisavāsāyacakāra mohāt |
śuṃbhaṃdṛḍhaṃtatrapuraṃ sa kṛtvātatrasthitodaityapatiḥ kadācit || 87||

niveśayāmāsapunaḥkrameṇaatyadbhute maṃdaraśailasānau |
duryodhanauvaigha sahasti saṃjñautanmaṃtriṇaudānavasattamasya ||88||

nārīṃsurūpāṃdadṛśutrayopiteśīghragādaityavaraṃsametya |
ūcuryathādṛṣṭamatīva sarva guhāṃtaredhyānanimīlitākṣaḥ || 89 ||

daityeṃdra kaśvinmuniratradṛṣṭorūpānvitaścaṃdrakalārdhajūṭaḥ |
kaṭisthalavyāghrakṛttiryuvā'saunāgeṃdra bhogāvatasarvagātraḥ || 90 ||

kapālamālābharaṇojaṭālaḥsaśūlahastaḥśaratūṇadhārī |
mahādhanuṣmānvaṭatākṣasūtraḥkhaḍītriśūlīlakuṭīkapardī || 91 ||

tasyāpidūrepuruṣaśvadṛṣṭaḥ sa vānaroghoramukhaḥ karālaḥ |
sarvāyudhaḥ sūkṣmakaraśvarakṣasthitojaragovṛṣabhastuśuklaḥ || 92 ||

nārīśubhāpārśvagatāhitasyadṛṣṭācakācidbhuviratnabhūtā || 93 ||

pravālamuktāmaṇihemaratnavastrāvṛtāmālyaśubhopagūḍhā |
sāyenadṛṣṭāsa cadṛṣṭimān syāddṛṣṭena cā'nyenakimatrakāryam ||94||

manyāmahetasya ca divyanārībhāryāmuneḥ puṇyamataḥ priyāca |
etāni śrutvātuvacāṃsi teṣāṃ kāmatvarā varṇita sarvagātraḥ || 95||

vi sarjayāmāsamuneḥ sakāśaṃduryodhanādīnsahasājajalpuḥ |
āsādyatetaṃmunimaprameyaṃ yaddaivataṃtasya caṃdaityarājaḥ || 96 ||

hiraṇya netrasya suto mahātmāta māhakasyāsiihopaviṣṭaḥ |
kasyeyamīdṛktaruṇī surūpānārīśubhādaityapatiṃ munīṃdra || 97 ||

vedaṃśarī raṃtavabhasmadigdhaṃkapālamālābharaṇaṃ virūpam |
tūṇīrasatkārmukavāṇakhaḍgabhṛśuṃḍiśūlāśanitomarāṇi || 98 ||

vajānhavīpu ṇyatamājaṭāgrekvā'yaṃśaśīvā kuṇapāsthikhaṃḍam |
viṣānalodīrṇamukhaḥkvasarpaḥkvasaṃgamaḥ pīnapayodharāyāḥ || 99 ||

jaradgavā rohaṇamapraśastaṃkṣaṇekṣaṇetasyanadarśanaṃca |
saṃdhyāpraṇāmaḥ kvacideṣadharmaḥvabhojanaṃ lokaviruddhametat || 100||

prayacchanārīṃ mamasāṃtvapūrvaṃ sarva viruddhaṃ kuruṣevimūḍha |
ayuktametattvayinānurūpaṃyasmādahaṃratnapatitriloke || 1 ||

vimuṃcaśastrāṇi mayā'dya coktaḥ kuruṣvapaśvārāpaevaśuddham |
ullaṃghyamacchāsanamapradhṛṣyaṃvimokṣa se sarvamidaṃśarīram || 2 ||

gatvāṃ'dhakaṃbūdhvamiti pradhānaḥ provāca daityaṃsmitapūrvameva |
ākarṇya sarvatvayadūtavākyaṃ yadyasti rudrastvatha kiṃmayāsyāt || 3 ||

nāhaṃkvacitsvaṃpitaraṃ smarāmiguhāṃtareghoramananyacīrṇam |

amūlametanmayisuprasiddhaṃsudustyajaṃ sarvamidaṃ mamāsti |
bhāryāmameyaṃtaruṇīsurūpāsarvaṃsahā sarva gatasyasiddhiḥ || 5 ||

evaṃ hiyayaducitaṃtavāstigṛhāṇa tadvaikhalurākṣasatvam |
gaṃbhīra metadvacanaṃniśamyatedānavāstaṃpraṇipatyamūrdhnā || 6 ||

jagmustato daityavarasya sūnuṃ trailokyanāśāyanatapratijñam |
babhāṣiraṃdaityapatiṃpramattaṃpraṇamya rājānamadīnasatvāḥ || 7||

tetatra sarvajaya śabdapūrva rudreṇa yattatsmitapūrvamuktam |
niśācaraścaṃcalaśauryadhairyaḥ kadānavaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ satvahīnaḥ || 8 ||

rājaṃstvamuktomuninā vihasyakrūraḥ kṛtaghnaśvasadaivapāpī |
kvadānavaḥ sūryasutādvibhetikkame bhujāḥ parvatarājasārāḥ || 9 ||

kā'haṃ śastrāṇicadāruṇā nimmṛtyoścasaṃtrāsakaraṃkvayuddham |
kvavīra kovānaravaccatulyoniśācarojarasājarjarāṃgaḥ ||10 ||

veyaṃsurūpākvacamaṃdabhāgyo balaṃtvadīyaṃkvacavīvadhovā |
śaknoṣicettvaṃ prayatasva yuddhaṃ kartuṃ tadātyehikuruṣvakiṃcit || 11 ||

vajrāśanestulyamihāstiśastraṃ kvateśarīraṃmṛdupadmatulyam |
ityevamādīnivacāṃsi bhadratapasvinādānavasaṃyatena || 12||

yuktaṃnatetenasahā'trayuddhaṃ tvāmāha rājansmayamānaeva |
kiṃvastuśūnyairbahubhiḥ pralāpairasmābhiruktairyadibuddhyasetvam || 13 ||

tapobhiyuktenatapasvināvaismartā 'si paścānmunivākyametat |
tataḥsateṣāṃva canaṃ niśamyajajvālaroṣeṇasamaṃdabuddhiḥ || 14 ||

ājyāvasiktaiva kṛṣṇavarmā satyaṃhitetatkuṭilaṃsutīkṣṇam |
gṛhītakhagovaradāna mattaḥ pracaṃḍavātānukṛtīśvakurvan || 15 ||

gatastatomattagajeṃdragāmīpī tvāsurāṃghūrṇitalocanaśca |
guhāmukhaṃprāpyadadarśadaityo gaurīṃtrilokasya casārabhūtām || 16 ||

śastrāṇiharturdṛṣabhadhvajasya taudaṃpatīgovṛṣavīrakaṃca |
tāṃhartukāmaḥ pravivikṣuragretato'dhakaḥ krodhasarāganetraḥ ||117||

nirīkṣya gaurīṃsmarabāṇaviddho guhāṃtatovīrakaruddhamārgām |
trigdhāṃyayādīpaśikhānirīkṣyapataṃgasaṃjñaḥ kramirabhyupetya || 18 ||

tathādadhāvāśupunaḥ punaśvasaṃpīḍyamānopisavīrakeṇa |
pāṣāṇavṛkṣāśanitoyavanhibhujaṃgaśastrāstra vibhīṣikā bhiḥ || 19 ||

saṃpīḍito'sautupunaḥ prapīḍyapṛṣṭaśvakastvaṃ kimihāgatosi |
niśamyataddvāṃsvamataṃ satasmai cakārayuddhaṃ sahavīra ke. ṇa || 20 ||

muhūrtamāśvaryavadaprameyaṃsaṃkhyejitovīratareṇadaityaḥ |
tasmiṃstusaṃgrāmaśirovihāya kṣutsāmakaṃṭhastṛṣitogato 'bhūt ||21||

cūrṇīkṛtekhagavarecakhinne palāyamānevighasādayaste |
cakruḥ suyuddhaṃ sahavīrakeṇa prahāda mukhyāditijapradhānāḥ || 22 ||

lajjāṃkuśākaṣṭadhiyovabhūvuḥ sudāruṇaṃśastraśatairanekaiḥ |
virocanastatracakārayuddhaṃbaliśvavāṇaśvasahasrabāhuḥ || 23 ||

bhaṃjaḥ subhaṃjastvathaśaṃbaraśvavatrādayaśvāpyathavīryavaṃtaḥ |
teyuddhyamānāvijitāḥ samaṃtātkṣiprahatāvaigaṇavīrakeṇa || 24 ||

saṃ khyehatānāṃ bahudānavānāmuktaṃjayetyeva hisiddhasaṃdhaiḥ |
bheruṃḍanṛttābhinayapravṛtte medovasāmāṃsasupūtimadhye || 25 ||

kravyā dasaṃghātasamākuletu bhayaṃkareśoṇitakardametu |
bhagnaistu daityairbhagavānpinākīvrataṃ mahāpāśupataṃsughoram || 26 ||

priyemayā yatkṛtapūrvamāsīddākṣāyaṇīṃprāhasusāṃtvayitvā |
tasmātphalaṃ yanmamatatpranaṣṭaṃmartyairamartyasyayataḥ praghātaḥ || 27 ||

puṇyakṣa yādvigrahaeva jātodivāniśaṃdevitava prasaṃgāt |
utpādyadivyaṃ paramādbhutaṃtupunarvanaṃghorataraṃcagatvā || 28 ||

tasmādvataṃghorata raṃcarāmimahāvrataṃ nāma sudāruṇaṃyat |
saṃrakṣitātvaṃsutavīra keṇa guhāṃtaretiṣṭhasusaṃyatā'tha || 129 ||

yāvattvihāyāmivrataṃsucīrtvāsunirbhayāsuṃdarivaiviśokā |
etāvaduktvā vacanaṃ mahātmāutpādya ghoṣaṃśana kaiśvacāra || 30 ||

satatragatvāvratamugradīptirgatovanaṃpuṇyatamaṃ sughoram |
cartuthyaśakyaṃtusurāsurairyattattādṛśavarṣasahasramātram ||31||

sāpārva tīmaṃdaraparvatasthāpratīkṣamāṇāgamanaṃ bhavasya |
pativratāśīlaguṇopapannāekākinīnityamathāpibhītā || 32||

guhāṃtare duḥkhaparābabhūva saṃrakṣitāsāsutavīra keṇa |
tataḥsadaityovaradānamattastairyodhamukhyaiḥ sahitoguhāṃtam || 33 ||

nirbhinnadhairyaḥ punarājagāmaśilīmukhairbhārasamudbhavaiśca |
atyadbhutaṃtatraca kārayuddhaṃ teṣāmṭate bhojanapānavelām || 34 ||

rātriṃdinaṃvarṣa śatānipaṃcakruddhaḥ sasainyaḥ sahavīrakeṇa |
khaṅgaiḥ sakuṃtaiḥ sahabhidipālairgadābhṛśuṃḍī bhirayeṣukuḍiḥ || 35 ||

śilīmukhairarddhaśaśī bhirugrairvitastibhiḥkūrmamukhairjvaladbhiḥ |
nārācamukhyairniśitaiśvaśūlaiḥ paraśvadhaistomaramudgaraiśca || 36 ||

khurairguḍaiḥ parvatapāda paiśvadivyairathāstrairapidaityasaṃghaiḥ |
naṃdīvinibhinnatanuḥ papātadvāraṃguhāyāḥ pihitaṃsamagram || 37 ||

tairāyudhairdaityabhujaprayuktai guhāmukhe mṛchitaevapaścāt |
ācchāditaṃvīra kaśastrajālairdaityeṣu sarveṣumuhūrtamātram || 38 ||

apāvṛtaṃ karttumaśakyamā sīnnirīkṣyadevīditijānsughorān |
bhayenasasmārapitāmahaṃtudebīsakhībhiḥ sahitācaviṣṇum || 39 ||

sainyaṃcatadvīravara sya sarvaṃ saṃsmārayāmāsaguhāṃtarasthā |
brahmātathāsaṃsmṛtamātra eva strīrūpadhārī bhagavāṃśvaviṣṇuḥ || 40 ||

iṃdraśvasarvaiḥ sahasaini kaiśrvastrīrūpamāsyāyasamāgatāste |
bhūtvāstriyasteviviśustadānīṃ munīṃdrasaṃghāśva mahānubhāvāḥ || 141 ||

yasmātsurājñāmanavasthitānāmataḥ puresaṃgamanaṃviruddham || 42 ||

tataḥsahasrāṇinitaṃbinīnāmanaṃtasaṃkhyānyapidarśayeyuḥ |
rūpāṇidivyāni mahādbhutānigauryaiguhāyāṃtusavīra kāyai || 43 ||

striyo'thadṛṣṭāgirirājakanyā guhāṃtaraṃ pūritapūrvamāsīt |
strībhiḥśatairnādaśatairanekairnanādakalpāṃtasameghaghoṣā || 44 ||

bhe ryaśvasaṃgrāmajayapradāsudhmātāsuśaṃkhāsunitaṃvinībhiḥ |
mūrcchāṃvihāyādbhutacaṃḍavīryaḥ savīrakovaipunarutthitastu || 45 ||

pragṛtyaśastrāṇi mahārathānāṃtairevaśakhaiditijān jaghāna |
brāhmītato daṃḍahastāviruddhāguhāṃtarākrodhaparītacetāḥ || 46 ||

nā rāyaṇīśaṃkhagadāna cakradhanurdharāpūritabāhudaṃḍā |
viniryayaulāṃgalakhaḍgahastāvyomālikākāṃcanatulyavarṇā ||47||

dhārā sahasrākulamugravegaṃbaiḍaujasīgṛtya kareṇavajam |
sahasranetrāyudhisusthirācasudurjayādaityaśatairadhṛṣyā || 48 ||

vaiśvāna rīśaktikarāsuva kāyāmyācadaṃḍoddhṛtapāṇirugrā |
sutīkṣṇakhaḍgodyatapāṇiragyānaiśācarīnairṛtacāpaghorā || 49 ||

toyā likāvāruṇacāpahastāvinirgatāyuddhamabhīpsamānā |
pracaṃḍavātaprabhavāca devīsubdhātatastvaṃkuśapāṇireva || 50 ||

kalpāṃ tavanhipratimāṃgadāṃcapāṇaugṛhītvādhanadodbhavāca |
yākṣeśvarītīkṣṇamukhāvirūpānakhāyudhānāgabhayaṃkarīca || 51 ||

etā stathānyāḥśataśohidevyaḥsunirgatāḥsaṃkulayuddhabhūmim |
dṛṣṭvācatatsainyamanaṃtapāraṃvivarṇavarṇāśrvasuvismitāśca || 152 ||

samākulāḥsaṃcakitābhayādvaisenāpīṃtavīrakaṃghoravīryam |
cakurmahāyuddhamabhūtapūrvanidhāyabuddhauditijāḥ pradhānāḥ ||53||

nivartanaṃsmṛtyumathātmanaśvanārībhiraṃtarvaradīnasatvāḥ |
atyadbhutaṃ tatracakārayuddhaṃ gaurītadānīṃsahitāsakhībhiḥ ||54||

kṛtvāra ṇecā'dbhutabuddhiśauṃḍasenāpatiṃvīrakaṃghoravīryam |
hiraṇyanetrātmajaeva bhūyaścakre mahāvyūhamudārakarmā || 55 ||

saṃbhāva yiṣṇuṃcanirīkṣyayāmyāṃsudāruṇaṃtagilanāmadheyam |
mukhaṃkarālaṃvighasevayasyatasminkṛte bhagavānājagāma ||56||

kalpāṃ taghorārkasahasrakāṃtiśvīrṇeya te kupitaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ |
gatetateāvarṣasahasramātretamāgataṃprekṣya maheśvaraṃca || 57 ||

cakrurmahāyu ddhamatīvamātraṃnāryaḥprahṛṣṭāḥsahavīrakeṇa |
praṇamyagaurīṃgiriśaṃcamūrdhnāsaṃdarśayanbharturatīvaśauryam ||58||

gaurīcayuddhaṃcaca kāradṛṣṭāharastataḥ parvatarājaputrīm |
kaṃṭhe gṛhītvātuguhāṃpraviṣṭorāmāsahasrāṇivisarjitāni || 59 ||

gaurīṃcasaṃmānaśataiḥ prapūjyaguhāmukhevīrakaeva tasthau |
tataḥ prahārairapijarjarāṃgastasmin raṇe daityavareṇadūtaḥ || 60||

jagādavākyaṃ tu sagarbhamuyaṃtra viśyaśaṃbhuṃpraṇipatyamūrdhnā |
saṃpreṣitastāṃvighasoguhāṃtuepoṃ'dha kastvāmapicādyavākyam || 61 ||

nāryācakāryaṃta va kiṃcidaṃ stivimuṃcanārīṃtaruṇīṃsurūpām |
prayoktavāṃstāpasatajjuṣasvakrāṃtaṃmayāyatkamanīyamāśram || 62 ||

muniviroddhavyaiti pracityanatvaṃ munistāpasakiṃtuśatruḥ |
atīva daityeṣumahāvirodhīyudhyasvavadhyo'si mayāpramathya || 63 ||

neyāmayātelala nānurūpāetadvacodūtamukhānniśamya |
kapālamālītamuvācakopājjvalanviṣādena mahāṃstrinetraḥ || 64 ||

vyaktaṃ vacastadbhava tāsamagraṃproktaṃhitattetvaritaḥprayāhi |
yaḥ syādaśaktobhuvitasyako'rthodārairdhanaiva sumanoharaiśca || 165 ||

āyāṃtudaityāścabalena mattāvicāryamevaṃ tukṛtaṃmayaitat |
śarīrayātrāpikutastvaśakteḥ kurvaṃtuyagradvihitaṃtuteṣām || 66 ||

māpiyadyatkaraṇīyamastitattatkariṣyāminasaṃśayo'tra |
etadvacastadvighasopiyasmācchutvāharānnirgataevathṛṣṭaḥ ||67||

āgānnadannārjita huṃrutāni kurvan gatadaityapateḥ sakāśam |
tasyeṃgitajñaśvasadaityarājogadāṃgṛhītvātvaritaḥ sasainyaḥ || 68 ||

kṛtvārdhasaiṃhaṃgilanāmadheyaṃsudāruṇaṃ devavarairabhedyam |
guhāmukhaṃ prāpyamaheśvarasya vibhedaśastrairaśaniprakāśaiḥ || 69 ||

anyetovīrakamevaśastrairavākirannadrisutāmathā'nye |
dvāraṃhikecidrucitaṃbabhaṃjuḥpuṣpāṇisarvāṇicanāśayeyuḥ || 70 ||

phalānimūlānijalaṃcatyamudyānamārgānapikhaṃḍayeyuḥ |
viloḍayeyurmuditāśca kecicchaṃgāṇiśailasyacabhānumati || 73 ||

tatoharaḥ saṃsmṛtavānkha sainyaṃ samāvhayatkupitaḥśūlapāṇiḥ |
bhūtānicānyāni sudāruṇāni devān sasainyānsahaviṣṇumukhyān || 72 ||

āhūtamātrāstugaṇāḥ sasainyārathairdhvajairvājiṣaiśvagobhiḥ |
uṣṭraiḥ kharaiḥ pakṣivaraiśva siṃhaiḥ sarve cadevāḥ sahabhūtasaṃdhaiḥ || 73 ||

vyāghrairmṛgaiḥśūkaraśārabhaiśvasamīna matsyaiḥ śiśumāramukhyaiḥ |
anyaiśvanānāvidhajīvasaṃ dhairgrahaiviśīrṇasphuṭitaiḥ śmaśānaiḥ ||74||

bhujaṃgamaiḥ pretaśataiḥpiśācairdivyairvimānaiḥ kamalākaraiśrva |
nadīnadaiḥ parvatavāhanaivasamāgatāḥ prāṃjalayaḥ praṇamya ||75||

kapardinaṃtasthuradīnasatvāḥ senāpatiṃvīrakameva kṛtvā |
visarjayāmāsaraṇāyadevānviśrāṃtavāhānathatatpinākī || 76 ||

yuddhaṃsthiraṃ labdhajayaṃpradhānaṃsaṃpreṣitāstetumaheśvareṇa |
cakruryugāṃtapratimaṃcayuddhaṃmaryādahīnatvaritaṃgilena || 77 ||

daityeṃdra senyena sahaiva ghoraṃ krodhānnigīrṇāstridaśāstvasaṃkhyāḥ |
tasminkṣaṇe yudhyamānāśca sarvavaleṃdra viṣṇvarkaśaśāṃka mukhyāḥ ||178||

tathaiva bhūtānigaṇaiśvasārdhaṃkṣudhānvitenādbhutavikrameṇa |
daityeṃdravākyādvighasenatena sainyenigīrṇesativīra kastu ||79||

vihāyasaṃgrāmaśiroguhāṃtāṃpraviśyasarvapraṇipatya mūrdhnā provāca duḥkhābhihataḥ smarāriṃsavīrabhadrovihitāṃjaliḥ san ||80||

nigīrṇamesainyaṃvighasaditijenādya bhagavannigīrṇo'sauviṣṇu tribhuvana gururdaityadalanaḥ |
nigīrṇaucaṃdrārakamalajamaghonaucavaradaunigīrṇāstesarveyamavaruṇavātāśvaghanadaḥ || 81 ||

sthito'smyekaḥ pravhaḥ kimihakaraṇīyaṃ bhavatime ajeyodaityeṃdraḥ pramuditamanādaityasahitaḥ |
ajeyatvāmāyātpratijava manomārutagatiḥ svayaṃ buddhyādṛṣṭvā yadiha karaṇīyaṃ kuruvibho || 82 ||

nasāmānyodaityovighasaditijastvapyayama sausvayaṃ viṣṇurdevaḥ kanakakaśipuṃkaśyapasutam |
nakhaistīkṣṇairbhacvātadapi bhagavānoṣavaśagaḥ pravṛtto vai viśvaviṣasitumalaṃvyāttavadanaḥ || 83 ||

vasiṣṭhāyaiḥ śamo bhuvanapatibhiḥ sammunibhistathābhūtobhūyastvamitisuciraṃdaitya sahitaḥ |
tatastenoktāste praṇayavacanairātmani hitaiḥ kadāśāpāttasmādbhavatimamamokṣo munivarāḥ || 84 ||

yataḥkrodhairuktovighasaharaṇāyuddhasamayetate |
ghorairbāṇairvidalitamukhomuṣṭibhiralam || baṃdaryākhyāraṇye narahari mahāpuṇyavasatau nirastābhyāṃtābhyāṃvigatakaluṣoyāsyasiparam || 85 ||

tatasteṣāṃvākyātpratidina masaudaitya gilanaḥ kṣudhārtaḥ saṃgrāmeśramatipunarāmodamuditaḥ |
tamaśvedaṃghoraṃjagatihitayoḥ sūryaśaninostathāśukrastubhyaṃparama ripuratyaṃtavidhuraḥ || 86 ||

hatāndevaidaityānpunaramaravidyāstutipadaiḥsavīryān saṃdṛṣṭāntraṇaśataviyuktānprakurute |
vaprāṇāstyājyāstavatamanusaṃgrāmasamayebhavānyāṃnītāyāṃkṣaṇamapitaḥ kāryakaraṇaḥ || 87 ||

idānīṃsatputrātpramathapatirākarṇyakupitaśviraṃdhyātvā cakre tribhuvanamitiprāganupamam |
agāyatsomākhyaṃdina karakarākāravapuṣaṃ prahāsāttannāmnātadanu nihṛtaṃtenacatamaḥ || 88 ||

bhayādityaṃtaṃ prakāśesmiṃllokepunarapimahāyuddhamakarodraṇedaityaiḥ sārddhaṃ vikṛtavadanairvīra kamuniḥ |
śilā cūrṇa bhuktvāpravaramunināyastujani tastapaḥkṛtvātoyeyamamapipurāyaśvajitavān || 89 ||

tatorudraḥ sadyaḥ sakhalu ditijenātibalinānigīrṇosaunaṃdīniśita śaraśūlāsisahitaḥ |
pradhānoyodhānāṃ munivaraśatānāmapimahānnivāsovidyānāṃśamadamamahā dhairya sahitaḥ || 90 ||

nirī kṣyaivaṃ paśvājjarahṛṣabhamārutya bhagavānkapardī yuddhārthīviṣasaditijaṃ saṃmukhamukhaḥ |
japandivyaṃmaṃtraṃnigilanavidhātodgila nakaṃsthitaḥ sajjaṃrutvādhanuraśanikalpānapiśarān || 91 ||

tatonito'sauvighasavadanādvīra kamunigṛhītvā tatsarvasvava lamatulaṃviṣṇusahitaḥ |
tamudgīrṇāḥ sarve kamala jamaghoneṃdudinapāḥprahṛṣṭaṃtatsainyaṃpunarapimahāyuddhamakarot ||92||

jitetasmi naśukrastadanuditijānyuddhanihatānyadāvidyāśauryātpunarapisajīvānprakurute |
tadācadhvādattaḥ paśumivagaṇairbhūta gīrṇastenāsautripuraripuṇādānavaguruḥ || 93 ||

vinaṣṭeśukākhye suraripubalaṃ tattadakhilaṃjitaṃdhvastaṃbhagnaṃsmṛtamapi suraiśvāpi dalitam |
prabhūtairbhūtaughairditijakuṇapagrāsaramikaiḥ saruṃḍairnṛtyadbhirniśitaśaraśaktyuddhata karaiḥ || 94 ||

pramattairvetālaiḥ sudṛḍhaka ratuṃḍerapikhagairṭakairnānābhedaiḥśava kuṇapapūrṇasya kavalaiḥ |
vikīrṇesaṃgrāme kanakakaśiporvaṃśajanakaśviraṃ yuddhaṃ kṛtvāhariharama ghonaiśvavijitaḥ || 95 ||

praviṣṭaḥpātālegirijaladhiraṃdhāṇyapibhayāsataḥ sainyekṣīṇeditijavṛṣabhastvaṃdhakavaraḥ |
prakope devānāṃ kadanamakarodvīra kaba legadāghātairghorairvidalitatanuśvāpihariṇā || 96 ||

nadhairyātsaṃgrāmaṃtyajativaralabdhaḥ kilayata stadākāṃḍairghoraikhidaśapatināpīḍitatanuḥ |
tataḥśastrāstraughaistarugirijalaistāṃ śvavibudhājigāyoccairgarjanpramathapatimā hūyaśanakaiḥ || 97 ||

sthito yuddhaṃ kurvanraṇa patitaśastrairbahuvidhaiḥ parikṣīṇaiḥ sarvaistadanugirijāṃrudramahanat |
tathāvṛkṣaiḥ sarvai raśaninivahairagnijaladairvirūpairmāyābhiḥ kapaṭaracanāśaṃvaraśataiḥ || 198 ||

śaileṃdranaṃdinaṃ |
kuhakaṃkapaṭaṃ || 99 ||

viṣpadaiḥspaṃdamānaiḥ |
pacādyac || 200 ||
