dakṣiṇabhāvāt śivadakṣiṇāṃgāt || 2 ||

3 |
giriśaṃyuyudhe || vijitvāśaileṃdraṃkuhakamaparaṃtatrakṛtavānmahāsatvovīrakhipurariputulyaśvamatimān || avadhyodevānāṃvaraśatamahonmādaviva śaḥprabhūtaiḥśastrāstraiḥsapadiditijojarjaratanuḥ || 99 ||

svadehādvippadaiḥ kṣititalagatairaṃdhakagaṇairasaṃkhyātaṃ ghoraṃvikṛtavadanaṃskhā tmasadṛśam |
sphuratkalpāṃtāgnipratimavapuṣābhūtapatinātriśūlenodbhinna tripuraripuṇādāruṇataram || 200 ||

yadāsainyātse nyaṃpaśupatihatādanyadabhavadraṇotye ratyuṣṇaiḥ piśitanissṛtai biṃdubhiralam |
tadāviṣṇoryogātyamayapatimāhūyamatimāncakā rogaṃrūpaṃvikṛtavadanaṃstraiṇamajitam || 1 ||

karālaṃsaṃśuṣkaṃ vahubhujalatākrāṃta kakubhaṃ dvinetraṃtvākīrṇabhujagavadanairlomaniva haiḥ |
satābhyāṃsarpābhyāṃviṣadahanapuṃjaugha massṛjacchrarahyo mākāraṃ tadnu kupitodakṣiṇabhavāt || 2||

viniṣkrāṃtā karṇādraṇaśi rasiśaṃbhorbhagavataḥ saṃgrāmasthādevīcaraṇayugalālaṃkṛtamahī |
stutādevaiḥ sarvaistadanubhagava preritamatiḥkṣudhārtātatsainyaṃditija nissṛtaṃtaścarudhiram ||3||

jaghāsānunnaṃtadraṇaśirasisatkardama ma laṃtatastve ko daityastadapiyuyudheśuṣkarudhiraḥ |
talāghātairvī rairaśanisadṛśairjānucaraṇairnakhairvajñākārairmukhabhujaśirobhiśvagiriśam || 4 ||

smaran kṣātradharmasvakulavihitaṃśāśvatamajaṃ raṇeśāṃtaḥpaścātpramathapatinābhinnahṛdayaḥ |
triśulesaṃprotonabhasividhṛtaśchatrasadṛśo adhaḥ kāyaḥ śuṣkastapanakiraṇaija rṇatanumān || 5||

jalāsārairmedhaiḥ pavana sahitaiḥkle ditavapurviśīrṇastigmāṃśustuhina sakalākāraśakalaḥ |
tathābhūtaḥ prāṇāṃ stadapina jahaudaityavṛṣabhastatoyuddhasyāṃtebhuvanapatayojagmuramarāḥ || 6 ||

stavairnānābhedaiḥ pramathapatimabhyarcyavidhivadgatāḥ svaṃ svadhāmatridaśapatayaḥprītamanasaḥ |
haraḥ svīyaiḥ sārddhaṃ girivaraguhāyāṃpramuditovisṛjyaikovaṃśānvividhabalibhiḥ pūjyasubhagaḥ ||7||

cakārāgryāṃkrīḍāṃgirivarasutāṃprāpyamuditāṃtathāputraghorādvighasavadanānmuktamanagham || 208||


|| 5 ||

sūta uvāca ||

itihāsam iti śrutvā punas te paramarṣayaḥ |
vyāsaṃ paramasaṃdṛṣṭāḥ papracchur munisattamam || 1 ||

munaya ūcuḥ ||

śukro daityapatir vidvān bhakṣitas tripurāriṇā || 2 ||

kalpāntadahanaḥ kālo jaṭharasthaḥ pinākinaḥ |
kiṃ cakāra mahāyogī rudrasya jaṭharānalaḥ || 3 ||

tapasyantaṃ tu taṃ bhūyo bhārgavaṃ dīptatejasam |
na dadāha kathaṃ śukraṃ bhojanāṃ tharapañjarāt || 4 ||

viniṣkrāntaḥ punar dhīmān bhagavantam umāpatim |
katham ārādhayām āsa kiyatkālaṃ sa bhārgavaḥ || 5 ||

lebhe 'dhako gāṇapatyaṃ kathaṃ śūlādi nirgataḥ |

bhagavān kālikāputro yo yātāttham uvāca saḥ |
teṣām etad vacaḥ śrutvā vāsa tatre 'pārīkṣitas tu || 7 ||

vyāsa uvāca ||

bhārgavo vāruṇīṃ vibhrad rakṣitas tripurāriṇā || 8 ||

mahāśalyupraśamanīm sarvasiddhipradāṃ śivām |
śṛṇuta mṛtyuṃjayāṃ vidyāṃ pāraṃparyakramāgatām || 9 ||

oṃ namas te deveśāya surāsuranamaskṛtāya bhūtabhavyamahādevāya haripiṅgalalocanāya balāya buddhirūpiṇe vyāghravasanacchadāyāraṇyāya trailokyaprabhava īśvarāya || 10 ||

harāya haritanetrāya yugāntakaraṇāyānalāya gaṇeśāya lokaśambhave lokapālāya mahābhujāya mahāhastāya śūline mahādaṃṣṭriṇe || 20 ||

maheśvarāya kālāya kālarūpiṇe nīlaśrīvāya mahodarāya gaṇādhyakṣāya sarvātmane sarvabhāvanāya sarvagāya || 30 ||

mṛtyave mṛtyudūtāya pāriyātrasuvratāya brahmacāriṇe vedāntagāya paśupataye avyayāya śūlapāṇine ṭaṣaketave haraye jaṭine 40 muṇḍine śikhaṇḍine lakuṭine mahāyaśase bhūteśvarāya guhāvāsine vīṇāpaṇavatūṇavate amarāya darśanīyāya bālasūryanibhāya 50 śmaśānacāriṇe bhagavate umāpataye arindamāya bhasmākṣipātine pūṣṇodaśanaśanāya kūrkarṇe pāśahastāya pralayakālāyolkāmukhāya 60 agniketave munaye dīptāya viśāṃpataye unmadāya vepanakāya caturthakāya lokasattamāya vāmanāya vāmadevāya 70 vāgdākṣiṇyāya vāmato bhikṣave bhikṣurūpiṇe jaṭine svayaṃjaṭilāya śakrahastapratiṣṭambhakāya vasūnāṃ stambhakāya ṛtave ṛtukārāya kālāya medhāvine 80 madhukarāyācalāya vānaspatyāya vājasane nityamāśrampūjitāya jagaddhātre jagatkartre puruṣāya śāśvatāya dhruvāya 90 dharmādhyakṣāya trivartmane bhūtabhāvanāya trinetrāya baṭurūpāya sūryāyutasamaprabhāya devāya devātidevāya candrāṅkitajaṭāya nartakāya 100 lāsakāya pūrṇenduṣadṛśānanāya brahmaṇyāya śaraṇyāya sarvadevamayāya sarvatūryaninādine sarvabandhamocanāya bandhanāya sarpadhāriṇe dharmottamāya 110 puṣpadantāya apibhāgāya mukhyāya sarvaharāya hiraṇyaśravase dvāriṇe bhīmāya bhīmaparākramāya 118 oṃ namo namaḥ || 10 ||

vyāsa uvāca ||

imaṃ mantravaraṃ jambvāśuko jaṭharapañjarāt |
niṣkrānto liṅgamārgeṇa śambhoḥ śukramivolkaṭam || 11 ||

gauryāṅgṛhītaḥ putrārthaṃ tato vighneśvaraḥ sutaḥ |

tribhir varṣasahasraistu sumatīrtaṃ mahītale |
maheśvarāt punar jātaḥ śuko vedanidhir muniḥ || 13 ||

vyāsa uvāca ||

dadarśa śūlasaṃśuṣkadhyāyantaṃ parameśvaram |
andhakaṃ dhairyasaṃpannaṃ dānavāntaṃ tapasvinam || 14 ||

acyutaṃ śāśvataṃ sthāṇuṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ pinākinam || 15 ||

vṛṣabhākṣaṃ mahājñeyaṃ 10 puruṣaṃ sarvakāmadam |

virūpaṃ giriśaṃ bhīmaṃ sagviṇaṃ 20 raktavāsasam |
yoginaṃ kāladahanaṃ tripurahnaṃ kapālinam || 17 ||

gūḍhavrataṃ guptamantraṃ gambhīraṃ bhāvagocaram |
aṇimādiguṇādhāraṃ 30 trailokaiśvaryadāyakam || 18 ||

vīraṃ vīrahaṇaṃ ghoraṃ virūpaṃ māṃsalaṃ paṭum |
mahāmāṃsavasonmattabhairavaṃ 40 parameśvaram || 19 ||

trailokyaḍāmaraṃ lubdhyaṃ lubdhyakaṃ yajñasūdanam |
kṛttikānāṃ sute raktaṃ unmattaṃ kṛttivāsasam || 20 ||

gajaruttiparīdhānaṃ kṣubdhaṃ bhujagabhūṣaṇam |
dattālaṃbaṃ cavetālaṃ ghoraśākinipūjitam || 21 ||

aghoraṃ ghoradaityaghnaṃ ghoraghoṣaṃ vanaspatim |
bhasmāṃgaṃ jaṭilaṃ śuddhaṃ bheruṃḍaśatasevitam ||22||

bhūteśvaraṃ bhūtanāthaṃ paṃcabhūtāśritaṃ khagam |
ko dhinaṃ niṣṭhuraṃ caṃḍaṃ caṃḍīśaṃ caṃḍikāpriyam || 23||

caṃḍatuṃḍaṃ garutmaṃtaṃ nistriṃśaṃśavabhojanam |
lelihānaṃ mahāraudraṃtyuṃ mṛtyo ragocaram || 24||

mṛtyormṛtyuṃ mahāsenaṃ śmaśānāraṇyavāsinam |
rāgaṃ virāgaṃrāgāṃdhaṃ vītarāgaṃ śatārciṣam || 25 ||

satvaṃ rajastamo dharmamadharmaṃ vāsavānujam |
satyaṃ tvasatyaṃ sadrūpa masadrūpa mahetukam || 26 ||

ardhanārīśvaraṃ bhānuṃ bhānukoṭiśata prabham |
yajñaṃ yajñapatiṃ rudramīśānaṃ varadaṃ śivam ||27||

aṣṭottaraśataṃtvetanmūrtīnāṃ paramātmanaḥ |
śivasyadānavodhyāyanmuktastasmānmahābhayāt || 28 ||

divyenā'gṭatavarṣeṇasiṃcitaścakapardinā |
tuṣṭenamokṣitastasmā cchūlāgrādavaropitaḥ || 29 ||

uktaśvasāṃtvapūrvaṃ yatkṛtaṃ sarvamahātmanā |
īśvarauvāca || bhobhotuṣṭo'smidaityeṃdra damenaniya menaca || 30 ||

śauryeṇatavadhairyeṇavaraṃvaraya suvrata |
ārādhitastvayānityaṃsarvanirdhūtakilbiṣaḥ || 31 ||

varado'haṃvarārha stvaṃmahādaityeṃdrasattama |
prāṇasaṃdhāraṇādastiyaccapuṇyaphalaṃtava || 32 ||

tribhirvarṣasahasraistu tenā'stutavanirvṛtiḥ |

bhūmaujānuddayaṃ kṛtvā bhagavaṃ tamumāpatim |
bhagavanyayadukto'si dīnodīna padākṣaraḥ || 34 ||

harṣagadgadayāvācāmayāpūrvaraṇājire |
yadyatkṛtaṃvimūḍhatvā tkarmalokeṣugarhitam ||35||

ajānatātvāṃtatsarva kāmadoṣātkṛtaṃ mayā |
pārvatīṃpratiduṣṭaṃyattattanmanasimākṛthāḥ || 36 ||

duḥkhitasya dayākāryārūpaṇasyaviśeṣataḥ |
dīnasyabhaktiyuktasyabhavitānityamevahi || 37 ||

kṣamyatāṃmemahādevarūpaṇo duḥkhitobhṛśam |
so'haṃdīnobhaktiyuktoracitoyaṃmamāṃjaliḥ || 38 ||

iyaṃdevījaganmātāparituṣṭāmamopari |
krodhaṃvihā yasakalaṃprasannāmāṃnirīkṣatu || 39 ||

kā'syāḥ krodhaḥkvarūpaṇodaityo'haṃ caṃdraśekharaḥ |
kvabhavānparamodāraḥ kāmenaviva śīkṛtaḥ || 40 ||

kāmakrodhādibhirdoṣairjarasāmṛtyunātathā |
ayaṃtevīrakaḥ putroyuddhaśauṃḍomahāvala || 41 ||

kṛpaṇaṃ māṃ samālakṣyamāmanyuvaśamanvagāḥ |
bhaveyaṃmāpunardevinirlajjodharmavarjitaḥ || 42 ||

syāmahaṃvīra keṇāpi tulyaste vallabhaḥ sutaḥ |
tuṣārahāraśītāṃśuśaṃkhakuṃdeṃduvarṇabhāk || 43 ||

paśyeyaṃ pārvatīṃnityaṃ mātaraṃ gurugauravāt |
nityaṃbhavadbhyāṃ bhaktastunirvairodaiva taiḥ saha || 44 ||

nivasaṃyaṃgaṇaiḥ sārdhaśāṃtātmā yogaciṃtakaḥ |
māsmareyaṃpunarjātiviruddhadānavodbhavām || 45 ||

etāva duktvāvacanaṃ daityeṃdro maunamāsthitaḥ |
dhyāyaṃstrilocanaṃdevaṃpārvatīṃprekṣya mātaram || 46 ||

tatodṛṣṭastu rudreṇaprasannenaiva cakṣuṣā |
smṛtavānpūrvavṛttāṃtamātmanojanmacā'dbhutam ||47||

tasminsmṛtecavṛttāṃtetataḥ pūrṇamanorathaḥ |
praṇamya mātāpitaraukṛta kṛtyo'bhavattataḥ || 48 ||

||29||

kṛtāṃ pārvatyāmūyupāghrātaḥśaṃkareṇaca dhīmatā |
yathābhilaṣitaṃle bhetuṣṭādvāleṃduśekharāt ||49||

etadvaḥ sarvamākhyātamaṃdhakasyapurā tanam |
mṛtyuṃjayaṃcakathitaṃ maṃtraṃmṛtyuvināśanaṃ || paṭhitavyaṃprayatnenasarvakāmaphalapradam || 50 ||


6 |
munayaūcuḥ pativratānityamaśeṣadhātrī satyavratāsāpita ponvitāca|| kimātmanorakṣayituṃtyaśaktāgaurīṃparaṃ pālayatisvayaṃbhūḥ ||1||

ācakṣvasarvasvamanā mataṃtatkālī satīgauravatāṃ prapannā |
prabhāvamasyāḥśrutaeva yatnālliṃgaṃ yathāpūjyatamaṃhiśaṃbhoḥ || 2 ||

sūtauvāca

yasmāccayenacayadācayayācayaccayāva ccayatracaśubhāśubhamātmakarma |
tasmāccatenacatadācatathācataccatāvacca tatra ca kṛtāṃtavaśādupaiti || 3 ||

sucārurūpāṃstanabhā ranamrāṃsurūpiṇīṃmattagajeṃdrayānām |
dākṣāyaṇīṃcaṃdramukhīṃnirīkṣya ruruḥ kadācinmalaye kṛtaghnaḥ || 4 ||

daityastatomāraśilī mukhaistuprataptagātro'bhihatāṃtarātmā |
varṣāyutaṃsūryamarīcikoṭinibhinnamarmātapaeva cakre || 5 ||

pāṣāṇabhūtaścasuniśvaye naāhārahīnomamatāvihīnaḥ |
tataḥ kadācittamuvācadevaḥ padmodbhavaḥ padmapalāśakāṃtiḥ ||6||

kimarthametattapa āśritaṃtedaityeṃ draghoraṃ kathayasva sarvaṃ |
niśamyatattadvacanaṃ hitasya daityo'bravīḍyānanimīlitākṣaḥ || 7||

dākṣāyaṇī me bhavatu pradṛṣṭābhāryābha vovaihimamāstuvaśyaḥ |
māmestummṛtyuruṣitāttudevātstrīṇāṃsakāśādvibhiyāṃnajātu ||8||

mugdhāḥ striyaḥstvabalāḥsyairyadhairyāḥ parākrameśaurya suvīryahīnāḥ |
evaṃ rurorvācamimāṃniśamyasa jātahāsobhagavānuvāca || 9 ||

śaṃbhaukalyāṇapradebrahmaṇigate satitapaśva kenaitatsatyaṃtriṣu lokeṣudaityasudurlabhāśaṃkareṇāpinityam |
jaganmātāpārvatīlokapūjyākālatra yekutracidapyanārya || 10 ||

susaṃprāpyācetsābhaviṣyatsuśaṃbhornaśaṃkaro'nyāṃlalanāṃgamiṣyan |
najānhavīṃśirasādhārayiṣyanna ceṃdulekhāmathavāpisaṃ dhyāṃ| || 11||

nānāstriyaśvāduśatairanekairnārādhayiṣyanvividhairupāyaiḥ |
tasmādalaṃgacchavarāpsarobhiḥ krīḍāṃcasacejjīvituṃte manojñam || 12||

gacchā'thavāpārvatīṃpaśyadevīṃtāṃ prārthayānastu yathehakaścit |
tatraivavāsthīyatāṃkālarātrikapardinaṃvātri purārimugram ||13||

etāvaduktvāvacanaṃ mahātmāprajāpatirvyomacarobabhūva |
sadānaveṃdro 'pigato'thaśaṃbhaucakre rurustatratapaḥ suraudram || 14 ||

tapogninātanmalayaṃdadāhadaṃdatyamānobhagavānpinākī |
gato'nyadeśaṃtarasāsadārobhayāttapogneḥ parataḥ paraṃ tu ||15||

gaurī patiṃprāhapalāya se kiṃnivāryatāṃvanhirayaṃ prabhutvāt |
agādagaṃyāsibhayātkimarthaṃ prabrūhi satyaṃvacanaṃ tathoktaḥ || 16 ||

niśamyagāmīdṛśamiṃdu maulistasyāḥ satāmāhaśṭaṇupriyevai |
tvāṃprārthayānorurureṣa daityastapaścacārogramatistu tena ||17||

trailokyametatsacarācaraṃvai nirdagdhumī ṣṭetapasāpareṇa |
nāhaṃsamarthastvayitatkuruṣvayacchakyase yatnamatoyakiṃcit || 18 ||

eṣo'jaliḥśīladhanemayātetrailokyametatsacarācaraṃ ca |
rakṣasvamātmānamihopayasya kṣamasva manyaṃmayi mānuthāstvam ||19||

taṃtāpasaṃgaccharuruṃvanasthaṃvimohayaṃtītamapīhaśaktyā |
tattādṛśaṃsāvacanaṃ niśamyaśaṃbhostugaurīcakitātadābhūtu || 20 ||

kruddhātataḥsāpivanaṃjagāmaviṃdhyācalenāgamaheṃdrakalpam |
saṃyudhyamānaṃgurumuṣṭighātaistaccarmaṇāveṣṭitagātrayaṣṭiḥ || 21||

gajeṃdraraktenatulitagātraṃ dadarśa siṃhaṃsuvidāritāsyam |
sālīlayātaṃnijaghānaghoraṃ taccarmaṇāchinna kaṭisthalāca || 22||

raktenasiṃhasyacasiktakeśīlaṃbodarīvaikṛtikābabhūva |
pracaṃḍa huṃkāraśatānikṛtvā prāptāguhāṃdānavasattamasya || 23||

pāda prahārairatha muṣṭighātairhatvākapāṭārgalamevatasya |
kṛtvādṛhāsaṃpralayāśraghoṣāuvācataṃmīlitalocanaṃtu || 24 ||

prātā'smi yadyatkaraṇīyamastieṣāsmigaurītava kiṃkaromi |
alaṃmahādaityatava śrameṇa ācakṣva sarvaṃ manasepsitaṃte || 25||

daityeṃdra kiṃ tvaṃtapasākṛtenatattatkuruṣvādyamanorathaṃtu |
daṃṣṭrākarālāmayatāṃsadṛṣvāsaṃdṛṣṭaromāpatitodhariṇyām ||26||

dhairyagṛhītvāpya thatāmuvāca

gaurī catattvaṃ vrajakālarātri |
gaurīsugātrāśaśipūrṇava kāsucārurūpāstanabhāranamrā || 27||

tapognināhaṃtapa raṃpareṇatrailokyametatpradahāmigaccha |
nagacchasevānihatāmayātvaṃgamiṣya se pretapateḥ sakāśam || 28 ||

etāvaduktvāgaढ़yā jaghānatāṃyogasiddhāṃgirirājaputrīṃ |
tayācamuṣṭyānihataḥ sadaityaḥ śastrāstrasaṃghairapisācatena || 29 ||

pāṣāṇavarṣaiśvatayā sadaityovakṣairanekairgiribhistathaiva |
jalāgnivarṇairvividhairanekaiḥ sapairmahāghoraviṣaṃva madbhiḥ || 30||

daṃtairnakhairbhakṣita krūrakarmāmāyāṃcakārātha sudurnivāryāṃ |
śuśoṇitasrāvasamudbhavāṃścaśrīvīryatulyāṃstu rurūnsasarja || 31 ||

anaṃtarūpānmalinovirūpānkarāladaṃṣṭrānatulaprabhāvān |
tatomahāsainyamanaṃtasaṃkhyaṃ dṛṣṭvāpyumāsvāttanutaścakāra || 32||

strī sainyamugraṃvikṛtaṃhiyogātsaṃbhakṣaṇārthaihiri porbalasya |
daityeṃdrasainyaṃtvatha caṃḍikābhiḥ saṃbhakṣitatatkṣaṇacaṃḍikāyāḥ || 33 ||

saṃbhakṣitaṃsakalaṃśoṇitaṃcasavanipītaṃ kṣatajaṃrurūṇāṃ |
pītvāpramattāditijasyamāyāṃgaurīcateṣāṃrudhiraṃcabhūri || 34 ||

e kākinīghorarūpākarālāpunaḥ samagraṃ hṛdayenidhāya |
vināśayitvāpravibhūtimāyāpracaṃḍadordaṃḍaśatairanekaiḥ || 35||

yuddhaṃcakā rādbhutadarśanena ekena māyāśatagūhitena |
tataḥ samāyīkupitaśvakurvanpracaṃḍavātānurūtiṃsadaityaḥ ||36||

tataśvacārāśugati strilokīmābrahmalokādisamastametat |
punarnabhaḥsvargamagātkṣaṇenatataḥsapātālatalaṃsamagram ||37||

kālānalāṃtaṃjagada prameyobabhbhrāmatasyāstvathavaṃcanārthaṃ |
daityastukhinnāgirirājakanyācakretataḥsthūlamatīvarūpam || 38 ||

kālānalārkadyu timapramāṇaṃrūpasya yasyādbhutavikramasya |
pramāṇamuktaṃtvavicaṃḍikāyāḥ kiṃtvasyaraṃdhrasyapitāmahena || 39 ||

cakrenajāto najaniṣyatevāsamagrarūpasyapramāṇavaktā |
brahmāṃḍametadvadarāṃḍa kalpaṃyatrāṃtarasthaṃkaṇavadvibhāti || 40 ||

anaṃtarudrādiśivā disaṃkhyejagatrayevadanāṃtarnilīne |
sthitvāgatiṃdaityavarasyatasyapadātpadaṃgaṃtumaśaktaāsīt || 41 ||

|| 31 ||

gauryātatodatyavarovimūḍhobhujairgṛhītvāśiraucca karta |
nakhāṃkurairvajramayaijrjvaladbhiḥ svadehamatyadbhutacaṃḍavīryaḥ || 42||

ācchādayāmāsaripośvakṛtyāvarmāsyakṛtsnaṃca nityadevī |
daityenaraktena sabudbudenavāṃtaṃśirājālaśataistuyattat || 43 ||

sabududaṃsoṣṇamatīvahṛdyaṃsaṃbhakṣyamāṃsānicatānigaurī |
gatāprahṛṣṭātukapālahastādṛṣṭvācatāṃtādṛśarūpadhārī || 44 ||

gaṇairasaṃkhyairadhigamyamānoyatrasthito'saubhagavānpinākī |
kapālamālī bhagavānprahṛṣṭautthāyacāliṃganamevacakre || 45 ||

aṃkecarutvāśanakairanaṃtaṃpapaumahāraktamapiprakāmam |
niveditaṃyagirirājaputryātāṃpāyayāmāsacapānamattaḥ || 46 ||

tatpītaśiṣṭaṃsutarāṃpapaucagātrāṇigātreṣunidhāyatasyāḥ |
gajeṃdrasiṃhoddhatadaityarakte tadgātraraktairanuliptagātraḥ || 47 ||

rurośvatatrāpicakṛttivāsāhimācalaḥ kiṃśukapuṣpakuṃjaḥ |
tatodadau parvatarājaputrīsiṃhasyacarmādbhutakhelanāya || 48 ||

mātaṃgarutiṃcamahāviśālāṃviśālalolāyatacārunetrā |
mṛgeṃdracarmāpyayatagṛhītvā cakārakaṭyāṃbhagavānpinākī || 49 ||

mātaṃgakṛtticakarairgṛhītvājugopagātrāṇitataḥsmarāriḥ |
devīruroścarmavigūhitāṃgīpramodamānāsahitāsakhībhiḥ || 50 ||

tatrastutāvaiṛṣibhiḥsasiddhaiḥsurāsurairmānuṣakiṃnaraiśva |
saṃprārthitāpūjitābrahmaṇācatrailokyarakṣārthamatīva ghoram || 51 ||

rūpaṃtutaccāṃḍikaṃgopayitvāpunaḥ śaraccāṃdramasaṃsurūpam |
kṛtvārarāmātha hareṇadevīharopitadvatprabhumodarāmayā || 52 ||


munayaūcuḥ||

śivaśaktyātmakelokemūlasargāditomune |
kona yorhyadhikaḥ sūtamāyayāsādhvasenatu || 1 ||

guṇenā'bhyadhi kaḥkaścidoṣeṇasūtanaṃdana |

sūtauvāca ||

pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate lokestrīṇāmatyadhikāḥ sadā |
guṇadoṣautathāmāyāyadrahasyaṃśṛṇuṣvatat || 3 ||

dvāsaptatisahasrāṇistrīṇāṃpuṃsāmathāpivā |
sarveṣāmevabhūtānāṃ nāḍīnāmitisaṃkhyayā ||4||

tāsāṃjaghanyānāḍīsyādvardhategarbhasaṃsthitā |
tadaṃtarenaroyasyāṃvikhyātāgarbhadhāriṇī ||5||

kathaṃtasyāguṇairdoṣairmāyayāvāpumānnaraḥ |
strībhāvenacapuṃbhāvaḥ strīpuṃsoḥ saṃgametathā ||6||

vānarānane nityamācchādyateloketasmādabhyadhikāḥ striyaḥ |
itihāsamimaṃ puṇyaṃstrīṇāṃmāyāvijṛṃbhitam || 7 ||

atrānurūpaṃśṛṇume puṇyaṃśrotrasukhāvaham |
vāṇāsurapurepūrva cakredevāsuraiḥ saha || 8 ||

nadītīreharaḥkrīḍāṃramyeśoṇasamāvhaye |
nanṛturjahasuvāpigaṃdharvāpsarasastathā ||9||

jepuḥ praṇemurānarcustuṣṭuvurmunayaśvatam |
vavalguḥpramathāḥ sarveṛṣayojuḍuvustathā || 10 ||

āyayuḥsiddhasaṃghāśrcaṃdadṛśuḥśāṃkarīṃratim |
kutārkikāvineśuśvamamluścaparipaṃthinaḥ || 11 ||

mātarobhimukhāstasthurvineśuśvavibhīṣakāḥ |
rudrasadbhāvabhaktānāṃ bhavadoṣāścapuṣṭuvuḥ || 12 ||

tasmindṛṣṭeprajāḥ sarvāḥ paramanirvṛtiṃyayuḥ |
carakhalurmunayaḥ siddhāḥstrīṇāṃdṛṣṭvāviceṣṭitam || 13||

pupuṣuścāpiṛtavaḥ svasvaṃ bhāvaṃtutatraca |
vavurvātāśvamṛdavaḥ puṣpakeśara dhūsarāḥ ||14||

cukūjubhṛṃgasaṃghāśca śākhināṃmṭadulaṃpayaḥ |
puṣpabhārāvanadvānāṃrāraṭīṣuśvakokilāḥ || 15 ||

madhuraṃ kāmajananaṃvaneṣūpavanesuca |
tataḥ krīḍāvihāretumattobāleṃduśekharaḥ || 16 ||

anirjitena kāmenahṛṣṭaḥ provācanaṃdinam |
śīghraṃmadvacanādgatvākṛtasnānāmihānaya || 17 ||

vānarānanagaurīṃme kailāsātkṛta maṃḍanām |

draṣṭumicchatidevitvadivorūparūtāṃśubhām |
tatastadvacanādgaurīmaṃḍanaṃ kartumārabhat || 19 ||

ājagāma tatonaṃdīrudrāṃtikaṃmanogatiḥ |
punarāhatatorudraḥkṣipramānayapārvatīm || 20 ||

bāḍhamukkaivatāṃgatvāgaurī māhasulocanāṃ |
draṣṭumicchatite bhartārutaveṣāṃmanoramām || 21 ||

tadvācastamathāpyanyadgataṃyoṣāgryapaṃcakam |
evaṃpate| sukāmārtagamyamāne'thanaṃdini || 22||

apsarobhiḥ samagrābhiranyo'nyamabhimaṃtritam |
labdhabhāvoyayāsadyaḥ pārvatyādarśanotsukaḥ || 23 ||

ayaṃpinākīkāmā riḥ kriyatesānitaṃvinī |
sarvāsadivyanārīṇāṃ rājñībhavaticottamā || 24 ||

mātṛṇāṃgaurīrūpeṇakrīḍayāmānmathairguṇaiḥ |
kāmo'haṃtikāmārimūcuranyonyamādṛtāḥ || 25 ||

spraṭuṃ śaknotiyākācidṛte dākṣāyaṇīṃstriyam |
kubhāṃḍaduhitātatraśaṃka raṃspraṣṭumutsahe || 26 ||

ahaṃgaurīsvarūpeṇacitralekhāvaco'bravīt |
citralekhovāca || yadināṃdīśvaraṃ rūpaṃ kācitkartṛkṣamā bhavet||devyāḥsakhīnāṃrūpaṃ tusukhasādhyaṃkariṣyatha || 27 ||

tatonāṃdīśvaraṃrūpaṃkeśavorusamutthayā |
kṛtaṃtadvaiṣṇavaṃ yogamāśri tyaparamārthataḥ || 28 ||

urvaśyāśvatato dṛṣṭvā rūpasya parivartanam |
kālīrūpaṃ ghṛtācītuviśvācī cāṃḍikaṃvapuḥ || 29 ||

sāvi cīrūpaṃ pramlocāgāyacaṃ menakātathā |
sahajanyājayārūpaṃvaijayaṃpuṃjikasthalī || 30 ||

vaināyakaṃmahārūpaṃ cakre sātukratustha lī |
mātṛṇāmapyanuktānāmanuktāśvā'psaropica || 31 ||

tatastāsāṃturūpāṇidṛṣṭvā kuṃbhāṃḍanaṃdinī |
vaiṣṇavādātmayogācca vijñānācca viḍaṃbanāt || 32 ||

mahāraktānnasaṃkāśaṃcaraṇaṃlalitāruti || 33 ||

mṛdumusnigdhasaṃpūrṇa divyapādopaśobhitam |
kramopacitasaubhāgyagulphāṃgulivirājitam || 34 ||

naveṃdu kiraṇajātavi ḍaṃbitaśaśiprabham |
divyanūpuraraṇatkārapūritāśeṣadiṅmukham || 35 ||

hasvavartulasaṃślakṣṇādivyajaṃghoṣaśobhitam |
jāta rūpasuvarṇābhaṃsaṃhatāṃgulibhūṣitam || 36 ||

vartulonnata saṃpūrṇa divyasphigupaśobhitam |
kanakādripratīkāśakaṭimaṃḍanamaṃḍi tam || 37 ||

kiṃcitkuṃcitasusnigdhasūkṣmaromāvalīyutam |
gaṃbhīrasāgarākāravarāhaśubhalakṣaṇam || 38 ||

mattabhramara nirṇādamekhalādāmabhūṣitam |
nānāvarṇamahāvakharamyacaṃdrāṃbikāṃkitam || 39 ||

īṣalajjāsuśobhāvyaṃśana kaidarśitāṃtaram |
vajrakalpitavedyāstu madhyasthaṃsuṃdaraprabham || 40 ||

kanakadroṇi saṃkāśadivyanābhivirājitam |
hemānadāmatulyābhapūrṇatrivaliśobhitam || 41 ||

śuddha jāṃbūnadaprakhyaṃkukṣodarasupīvaram |
jātarūpanibhākāravakṣasthala mahadhimat || 42 ||

atilāvaṇyaśobhāvyaṃ pūrṇaskaṃdhopaśobhitam |
śaśikoṭinibhākāraṃmuktādāma virājitam || 43 ||

saṃpūrṇammṛdusubhlakṣṇadivyadordaḍamaṃḍitam |
divyakeyūrakaṭakairbhūṣitaṃcāṃgulīyakaiḥ || 44 ||

khagdāmadarpaṇopetasuśobhita karāṃbujam |
pravālahemaraktānatulyapāṇitalaṃśubham || 45 ||

divyalāvaṇyaśobhāḍhyaṃrekhācitayaśobhitam |
kramopacitasuślakṣṇadīrghāgulimanoharam || 46 ||

puṣpopacitabhadrābhasuramyanakha caṃdrikam |
praśastadakṣiṇāvartakaṃbugrīvopaśobhitam ||47||

nānāvidhamahāratnagraiveyakaśatāvṛtam |
saṃpūrṇavartulākāradivyoṣṭhapuṭasūcitam || 48||

vidrumāṃkura saṃkāśamṛdu ślakṣṇava roddhataṃ |
satpadmarāgaraktābhakomalauṣṭhavibhāsitam || 49||

bāleṃdu lekhāsaṃghātatulyadaṃtāvalīyutam |
raktāna karṇikākārahasvajivhopaśobhitam ||50 ||

atyaṃta hemaśuddhābhasuspaṣṭaghrāṇamaṃḍitam |
vikārarahita snigdhanāsāpuṭavilomavat || 51 ||

caṃdramaṃḍalasaṃkāśasphuratpāṃḍukapolakam |
pūrṇāvikārahemābharamaṇīya mahāhanu || 52 ||

nimnasnigdhonnatābhogavikārojjhitakarṇavat |
hemamukkāmahāratnadivya saṃyukta kuṃḍalam || 53 ||

sitāsitamanāgraktalolalocanamaṃḍitam |
sadrājavartalekhābhabhbhrūlatālaṃkṛtānanam || 54 ||

gaurīśaliṃgasaṃkāśalalāṭataṭamaṃḍitam |
vikārarahitaṃsnigdhaṃrāgasaṃraktalocanam || 55 ||

lalāṭanayanābhogasavilāsavilokitam |
calatkuṃcitasusnigdhasūkṣmakṛṣṇālakāvatam || 56 ||

kuṃkumādimahāmālyatilakaṃdivibhūṣitam |
alakaprāṃta saṃlagna suvarṇamaṇividrumam || 57||

nānāratnasuvarṇāvyakirīṭoyotitānanam |
kokilāṃjanapuṃjābhadīrghaveṇīsuśobhitam || 58 ||

paṃcavarṇamahāpuṣpasragdāmacchannaveṇimat |
divyamālyāṃbaradharaṃ divyakhagdāmaśobhitam ||59||

viśvakarmakṛtairdivyairatiprāyairalaṃkṛtam |
evaṃrutetadānaṃdīpunastusagaṇogataḥ || 60 ||

gaurīmānayi tuhṛṣṭaḥ kailāsāttupunaḥśanaiḥ |
tataḥ sāvasaraṃprāpya harerūrusamudbhavā || 61 ||

dṛṣṭavādevamuvācāthadevaśailādihāgatā |
ahaṃgī rīcasaṃprāptāmātaraḥ sagaṇāstava || 62 ||

evamuktastayārudrastyaktvāśayyāṃtudṛṣṭavat |
purastānniryayaugauryāḥ śanaiḥ saptapadānitu ||63||


nānāvidha mahāstotraiḥ stuvaṃ tomuditāstathā |
tataḥ pāṇaugṛhītvātāṃrudraḥ śayyāmathā''ruhat || 65 ||

cakrenānāvidhāṃkrīḍāṃtayāsārdhapinākadhṛk |
rudraṃgāyaṃtinṛtyaṃtisarvāḥ kapaṭamātaraḥ || 66 ||

kāścidgāyatinṛtyaṃtiramaṃyaṃtihasaṃtica |
sukhanādsahasrāṇi kurvaṃtyanyāḥsahasraśaḥ ||67||

śivācarudrasahitārāvāṃścakre mahādbhutān |
athakiṃbahunoktenachidratāsāṃnavidyate || 68 ||

yena vijñāyate kiṃcitstrīṇāṃvyājavijṛṃbhitam |
kecidgāyaṃtinṛtyaṃtihasaṃticarutica || 69 ||

śaṃkarārādhanesaktāgaṇānaṃdīśvarādayaḥ |
kailāsānmātṛbhiḥ sārdhaṃgaṇaiḥ parivṛtāśanaiḥ ||70||

bhṛṃginaṃdimahākāṃladaṃḍilaṃbodarādibhiḥ || 71 ||

stūyamānātugaganātsaṃprānā bharturaṃtikam |
kimiyaṃpārvatīdevīkimiyamityaciṃtayan || 72 ||

tāṃdṛṣṭvācakitāḥsarvekimiyaṃbāsuśobhanā |
naitayordṛśyatebhedonānayornadinorapi || 73 ||

namātṛṇāṃgaṇānāṃtukopyayaṃsyādvyatikramaḥ |
tatoharasyapārśvasyāgaurī jñātvāvyatikramam || 74 ||

krīḍitaṃdivyanārīṇāṃprahāsaṃmumucetadā |
tāścaivāpsarasaḥsarvāścakruḥ kilikilāravān || 75 ||

tacchrutvātaccasaṃvīkṣyatatobāleṃduśekharaḥ || 76 ||

urvaśyādyātyapsarasaścitralekhācakāra || 77||

tatoratatisvaṃrūpaṃ cakruḥsvaṃsvaṃviceṣṭitam |
evaṃstrībhirmahādevaḥ pūrvavyājena toṣitaḥ ||78||

tataḥ sadbhāva gaurītupatināsahasaṃgatā |
atyadbhutāṃmahākrīḍāṃcakre devī nadītaṭe || 79 ||

toṣāvāṇaḍuhitākrīḍamānāṃtupārvatīm |
dṛṣṭvā manasi saṃdadhyau bhartṛsaṃgamakāṃkṣiṇī || 80 ||

anena bharcāsahitāyatheyamapipārvatī |
krīḍateyāḥ striyodhanyāramaṃtyevasamāgatāḥ || 81 ||

evaṃ saṃciṃtya manasāsoṣāsuniyame sthitā |
umāmārādhayāmāsa sopavāsā jiteṃdriyā || 82 ||

uvāca paramaprītānagarājasya naṃdinī ||83||

yathā'nenanadītīreramāmyahamaniṃdite |
tvamapibharṭasahitāramiṣyasyacirādiha || 84 ||

cakāra maṃdapuṇyāhabhatraraṃsye kadātviti || 85 ||

tathāpitasyāḥsaṃkalpaṃvijñāyagirijātataḥ |
uvācasamemāsisaṃprāpte satimānini || 86 ||

asmiṃstu kārtikemāsia tordhvamāsimādhave |
dvādaśyāṃśuklapakṣe tuyastu ghoreniśāṃgame || 87||

kṛtopavāsāṃtvāṃbhoktāsutāmaṃtaḥpurenaraḥ |
satebharttā kṛtodevaistenasārdhagamiṣyasi || 88 ||

āvālyādviṣṇubhaktāsiyato nityamataṃdvitā |
evamastviti sāprāhamanasālajjitā nanā ||89||

tatoratatibhagavān rudraśvādarśanaṃyayau |
sadāraḥ sagaṇaśvāpisa hitodeva mānavaiḥ || 90 ||

modamānāstriyaśvā thakrīḍāṃtāmanubhūyaca |
jagmuḥsvaṃ svaṃ gṛhaṃtsṛṣṭāsturagairvāraṇairathaiḥ || 91 ||

anyāśvasiddhayoginyājagmurākāśameva hi |
kasya cittvathakālasyabāṇorudrāṃtikaṃyayau || 92 ||

bhagavaṃtamumākāṃtaṃpūjayitvākṛtāṃjaliḥ |
uvācaduḥkhasaṃtapto daivadoṣāccaga vitaḥ || 93 ||

hedevakimanenāpisahasreṇakaromyaham |
bāhūnāṃgiritulyānāṃvināyuddhaṃ vṛṣadhvajaḥ || 94 ||

mayāyamaḥ kṛtayo ddhāvanhiśrvarutakomahān |
varuṇaścāpigopālogavāṃpālayitātathā || 95 ||

gajādhyakṣaḥ kubera stu sairaṃdhrī cāpinirṛtiḥ |
jitaśvā''khaṃḍaloyuddhekaradāyīsadākṛtaḥ || 96 ||

yuddhasyāgamanaṃbūhiyatreme bāhavomama |
śatruhastapramukteśvaśastrāstrairjarja rīkṛtāḥ || 97 ||

saptaṃtiśakrahastādvāpātayaṃtisahasradhā |
tacchrutvākupitorudrastvadṛhāsaṃmahādbhutam || 98 ||

kṛtvā'bravīnmahāmanyudhigdhigdai tyādhamāstuṃte |
darpasyā'sya mahāyuddhaṃlabhiṣyasi sudāruṇam || 99 ||

tatrategirivarmāṇābāhavonalakāṣṭhavat |
chinnābhū maupatiṣyaṃtiśastrāstraiḥkadalīkṛtāḥ || 100 ||

yadetanmānuṣaśiromayūrasahitaṃdhvaje |
vidyatetava duṣṭātmaṃstasya syātpatanaṃyadā || 101 ||

sthāpitasyāyudhāgārevināvātakṛtaṃbhayam |
tadāyuddhaṃ mahāghoraṃ saṃprāptamiticetasi || 2 ||

nidhāyaghora saṃgrāmaṃga cchethāḥsarvasainyavān |
sāṃprataṃgacchataddeśmayatratadddviyateśiraḥ || 3 ||

tathā'nyastu mahotpātāṃstatradraṣṭāsi durmate |
tacchrutvāru dramabhyarcya divyairaṃjali kuḍamalaiḥ ||4||

kuṃḍalāyayathāvṛttaṃpṛṣṭaḥ provācaharṣataḥ ||5||

svayaṃ bhagnadhvajaṃdṛṣvātatrayuddhāya niryayau |
sainyaṃ samagra māhūya saṃyuktasvāṣṭabhirguṇaiḥ || 6 ||

iṣṭisāṃgrāmikīkṛtvā dṛṣṭvā sāṃgrāmikaṃma dhu |
kakubhāṃmaṃḍalaṃ sarva saṃprekṣyasaṃsthito'bhavat || 7||

ititvatka malerutvākaḥ kasmādāgamiṣyati |

yastubā husahasraṃ mechinattinalakāṣṭhavat |
tathāśastrairmahātīkṣṇaichinaticchira stvi || 9 ||

yacasāvāṇaduhitā sunātākṛta maṃgalā || 10 ||

mādhavaṃ mādhave māsipūjayitvāma hāniśi |
suptācāṃtaḥpure guptestrībhāvamupalaṃbhitā || 11 ||

gauryāsaṃprekṣitanāpivyākṛṣṭādivyamāyayā |
kṛṣṇātmajātma jenātha rodamānāmanāthavat || 12 ||

sacāpitāṃbalādbhuktvāpārvatyāḥ sakhibhiḥpunaḥ |
nītastudivyayogena dvārakāṃnimiṣāṃ tarāt || 113 ||

mṛditāsātatotthāyarudatīvividhāġiraḥ |
sakhībhyaḥkathayitvātudehatyāgerutakṣaṇā || 14 ||

dauḥśīlyādopaśobhācasa- khyāsaṃsmāritāpunaḥ |
sarvatatpūrvavṛttāṃtaṃtatotsṛṣṭācasābhavat || 15||

abravīccitralekhāṃcaṃtatomadhurayā girā |
sakhiyadye pame bhartā pārvatyāvihitaḥ purā || 16 ||

kenopāyenateguptaḥ prāpyatevidhivanmayā |
kasminkulevājātosausama yenahṛtaṃmanaḥ || 17||

tayācoktātvayāsvapna yodṛṣṭodevitaṃ kathaṃ |
ahaṃsamānayiṣyāminavijñātastuyomama || 18 ||

daityakanyātadukteturā gāṃdhāraṇotsukā |
rakṣitāṃcatayāsakhyāsamamedivasetataḥ || 19 ||

rājñovastrapuṭerutvādevāndaityāṃśrvarākṣasān |
kṣatri yāṃśvatathāpyasyasāniruddhaṃdadarśaha || 20 ||

dṛṣṭāprovācacaurosaumayāprāptastu yena me |
cetoratnatvataṃsayaḥ saṃsparśādeva bhāmi ni ||21||

kasyāyamanvayejātonāmakiṃcāsyavidyate |
proktaṃtasyāstayāsarva sānvayaṃtasya dhīmataḥ || 22 ||

sarvamākarṇya sābhūyobabhāṣemattakāśinī |
upāyaṃ tasya mepaśya yenopāyena tatkṣaṇāt || 23 ||

labhāmiyaṃvinānā'haṃkṣaṇaṃjīvitumutsahe |
tataḥsakhīṃsamābhāṣyacitralekhāmanojavā || 24 ||

āprabhāvānmuhūrtetu saṃprāptādvāṃ rakāṃpurīm || 25||

ekena kṣaṇamātreṇanabhasādivyayoginī |
tataśvataḥ purodyāneprādyumniṃdadṛśesthitam || 26 ||

krīḍataṃstrī janaiḥ sārdhapibataṃmadhumādhavīṃ |
tataḥkhadvāsamārūḍhamaṃdhakārapaṭenasā || 27||

ācchādayitvāyogena tāmasenacamādhavaṃ |
ānayanmūrdhnitāṃkhadvāṃgṛhītvānimiṣāṃtarāt || 28 ||

saṃprāptāśoṇitapuraṃyatrasāvāṇanaṃdinī |
kāmārtāvividhānbhāvān cakāronmattavatkṣaṇāt ||29||

ānītamayataṃdṛṣṭvā tadābhītācasā'bhavat |
aṃtaḥpure sugupte canavetasminsamāgame || 130 ||

kālasama yāvatkrīḍitumārabdhaṃtāvajjñātaṃcatatkṣaṇāt |
aṃtaḥpuradvāragatairvaitrajarjarapāṇibhiḥ || 31 ||

iṃgitairanumānaiśvakanyādauḥ śīlyamevatat |
sacāpidṛṣṭastaistatranarodivyavapurdharaḥ || 32 ||

taruṇodarśanīyastusāhasīsamarapriyaḥ |
taṃdṛghvāsarvamācakhyu baṇāyabalisūnave ||33||

devakaścinnarastubhyaṃguptamaṃtaḥpure balāt |
śaktasyatavakanyāṃ tusvayaṃgrāhādadharṣayat || 34||

dānaveṃdra mahābāhopaśya paśyainamatraca |

divyanīlābjavapuṣaṃ prathamevayasisthitam |
taṃha svāvismitovākyaṃkiṃkarānapyathā'bravīt || 36 ||

bāṇaḥ krodhaparītātmāyuddhaśauḍohasanniva |
ahomanuṣyorūpādvyaḥ sā hasī dhairyavāniti || 37 ||

taṃmārayadhvaṃkupitāḥ śīghraṃśastraiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ || 38 ||

durācā raṃcataṃbadhvāghorekārāgṛhetataḥ |
rakṣadhvaṃkālasamayaṃkvaciddoraparākramāḥ || 39 ||

tatodaityena sainyaṃ tudaśasāhasrakaṃśanaiḥ |
va dhārthatasya vīrasyavyādiṣṭaṃpāpabuddhinā || 40 ||

śatrusainyaṃtatodṛṣṭvāgarjamānaṃsa yādavaḥ |

niṣkrāṃtobhavanāttasmādvajjahastaivāṃtakaḥ |
tenatānkiṃkarānhatvāpunaścāṃtaḥpuraṃyayau ||42||

jaghānaroṣaraktākṣodivyaraktānnalocanaḥ || 43 ||

lakṣahatethayodhānāṃtatobāṇāsuroruṣā |
kuṃbhāṃ ḍasaṃgṛhītāśvoyuddhaśauṃḍaḥ samāvhayat || 44||

aniruddhaṃ mahābāhudvaṃdvayuddha mahāhave |
tatodaṃśasahasrāṇituraṃgāṃśvarathottamān || 45 ||

yuddhaprāptena khaṅgena daityeṃdrasya jaghāna saḥ |
tadvadhāyatataḥ śaktiḥ kālavaiśvānaropamā || 46 ||

kṣipvābāṇenasaṃbhūyaḥśa saṃpreritāṃrupā |
aniruddho gṛhītvātāṃtayātaṃnijaghānasaḥ || 147||

rathopasthetatovāṇastenaśamyāhatodṛḍham |
patitomaṃtriṇātaṃtra hetubhiḥpratibodhitaḥ || 48 ||

tasmiṃstvaddarśanaṃprāpteprādyumniraparājitaḥ || 49 ||

ālokyakakubhaḥsarvāstasthaugiririvācalaḥ |
adṛśya mānastutadākūṭayodhīsadānavaḥ || 50 ||

nānāśastrasahasaiśvatāḍayitvāpunaḥpunaḥ |
chadmanānāgapāśaistaṃbaṃbaṃdhasumahābalaḥ ||51||

taṃbadhvāpaṃjarāṃtasthaṃkṛtvāyuddhādupāramat |
sūtaputraśiraśchidhiyena me dūṣitaṃkulam || 52 ||

chitvā sarvagātrāṇi rākṣasebhyaḥ prayacchabhoḥ |
athavānāsyamāṃsānikravyādā apibhuṃjate || 53 ||

agādhetṛṇasaṃkīrṇakūpepātakinaṃjahi |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dharmabuddhirniśācaraḥ || 54 ||

kuṃbhāṃḍatvatravīdvākyaṃdeva naitatkṣamobhavet |
asminhate hatotyātmābhavedi timatirmama ||55||

ayaṃtudṛśyatedevatulyoviṣṇoḥ parākramaiḥ |
athacaṃdralalāṭasya sāhasena samastvayam || 56 ||

imāmava syāṃprāpto'pipaurupesvevyavasthitaḥ |
asmāṃstṛṇopamānvettidaṣṭo'pi bhujagairbalāt || 57 ||

etadvākyaṃtubāṇasyakathayi tvāsadānavaḥ |
aniruddhamuvācedaṃ ko'sikasyā'si bhoriti || 58 ||

kenavātvamihānītodurācāranarādhameṃ |
daityeṃdrastu hi vīratvaṃnamaskurukṛtāṃjaliḥ || 59 ||

jito'smītivacodīnaṃkathayitvā punaḥpunaḥ |
evaṃrutetumokṣaḥ syādvedhanāttavanā'nya thā || 60||

tacchrutvāvacanaṃtasyaprativākyamuvāca

saḥ |
reredaityādhamasakheparapiṃḍopajīvakaḥ || 61 ||

niśācaradurācārakṣetra dharmana vetsibhoḥ |
dainyaṃpalāyanaṃvā'yaśūrasya maraṇādapi || 62 ||

viruddhaṃcā'yaśasyaṃcabhavediti matirmama |
kṣatriyasyaraṇe śreyomaraṇaṃ saṃmukhasya ca || 63 ||

natuprāpyatu bhūmitudīnasyaiva kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
kṛte 'piṃdainye maraṇaṃ navicālayituṃ kṣamaḥ || 164 ||

śatrurbhavasi maṃdātman jopamāsvāyamūkavat |

nityaṃkṛtāṃjaliryasyaso 'nyasyāṃ'jalimānkatham |
svayaṃtuvetsyaseyo'haṃchinne bāhuvanevane || 66 ||

etadvākyaṃ tu tasyotktvā yādavāmānamāśritaḥ |
bāṇaḥ kubhāṃḍavacanādaniruddhaṃnajaghnivān || 67 ||

kiṃtusvāṃtaḥpuraṃgatvāpapaupānamanuttamaṃ |
tato'niruddhoṣaddhastu nāgabho gairviṣolbaṇaiḥ || 68 ||

priyayāhṛtacetāstudurgāsasmāratatkṣaṇāt |
uvācadevibaddho'smidatyamānastupannagaiḥ || 69 ||

āgaccha me kurutrāṇaṃyaśodecaṃḍaroṣiṇi |
tenasātoṣitātatrakālībhinnāṃjanaprabhā || 70 ||

jyeṣṭhakṛṣṇa caturdaśyāṃ saṃprānā sumahāniśi |
gurubhirmuṣṭibhirghātairdārayāmāsapaṃjaram || 71 ||

śarāṃstān bhasmasātkṛtvāsarparūpānbhayānakān |
moca yitvāniruddhaṃtutatavāṃtaḥpuraṃtataḥ || 72 ||

praveśayitvādurgātunacaivā'darśanaṃ gatā |
satu labdhajayobhūtvāpriyāṃprāpyamumodaca || 73 ||

tatogate'niruddhetutatstrīṇāṃrodanasvanam |
śrutvācatvaritaḥ kṛṣṇoyuddhaṃ śrutvā canāradāt || 74 ||

jagāmaśoṇitapuraṃ tārkṣyamāhūyatatkṣaṇāt |
bhrātrārāmeṇasahitaḥ pradyumnena ca dhīmatā || 75 ||

tatrā'gninā'bhavadyuddhaṃyamena ca hareṇaca |
trimukhe natripādena jvareṇacaguhena ca || 76 ||

pramathairvividhākāraisteṣāmatyaṃtadāruṇam |
vibhīṣikābhirvavhībhiḥ koṭavībhiḥpade pade || 77 ||

nirlajjābhiśvanārībhirbhagnābhirapi sarvaśaḥ |
vīryasaṃstaṃbhayaṃtībhirvarāṃgasyacadarśanāt || 78 ||

teṣāṃcaturṇāsaṃ grāmaḥ paśvādvāṇenacā'bhavat |
māheśvarāśvayekecidvāṇasyā''san jvarādayaḥ || 79 ||

tāṃstān jaghāna goviṃdo ghāraiḥ svaisvairva rādibhiḥ |
kṛtvā sahasraṃ kāyānāṃpītvātoyaṃ mahārṇavāt || 80 ||

garuḍonāśayanhiṃvā tairmeghārṇavāṃbubhiḥ |
kumārasyamayūraṃ tutuṃḍaghātairvyapāṭayat || 183||

viṣṇunānirjiterudresaputresagaṇe sati |
bāṇovinirgatoyuddhadvaṃdvayuddhena viṣṇunā ||82||

kubhāṃḍasaṃgṛhītāśvovattesainyakṣayesati |
tataḥ sudarśanenā'tha viṣṇurvāṇabhujānbahūn || 83 ||

chitvāśiraśvaśatrostu yadāchettuṃ samudyataḥ |
stotraistatastvanekaiśvaśaṃkareṇa satoṣitaḥ ||84||

uktavordhva kareṇāthahareṇā'mitraghātinā |
bhagavandevakīputrakṛtaṃyakkriyatetvayā ||85||

māvāṇasyaśiraśchidhisaṃharasvasudarśanam |
dattaṃmayāpurātubhyamativīryaṃraṇetava || 86 ||

cakraṃ jayaśvagoviṃdanivartasvaraṇādataḥ |
tvaṃtuyogīśvaraḥ sākṣātparamātmājanārdanaḥ ||87||

varamasya mayādattaṃ namṛtyubhayamastite || 88 ||

tanmesatyaṃvacaścā'stuyadituṣṭo'si me prabho |

akṣatāṃgastuvijayīviṣṇuraṃtaḥpuraṃyayau |
aniruddhaṃ samāśvāsyasahitaṃ bhāryayāpunaḥ || 90 ||

gṛhītvāratnasaṃghātaṃcitralekhāṃcatatsakhīm |
punaḥsvabhavanaṃprāptojitvācavaruṇaṃpathi || 91 ||

visarjayitvāgaruḍaṃsakhivaccopahasyaca |
dvārakāyāṃtatodṛṣṭaḥ kāmacārīcacāraha || 92 ||

viṣṇaugateta tonaṃdībāṇaṃprovāca duḥkhitam |
daityaṃśoṇitadigdhāṃgaṃkurunṛtya mahotsavam || 93 ||

bhaktānukaṃpinaśvā'grerudrasyetipunaḥpunaḥ |
naṃdivākyāttatobāṇodvipācchīrṣaikamātrakaḥ || 94 ||

nanartatāṃḍavaṃ mukhyaṃ pratyālīḍhādiśobhitam sthānakairvividhākārairālīḍhapramukhairapi || 95 ||

mukhavādasahasrāṇiśśrūkṣepasahitānyapi |
śiraḥkaṃpasahasrāṇipratyānīkānsahasraśaḥ || 96 ||

cārīśvavividhākārādarśayitvāśanaiḥśanaiḥ |
tathāśoṇitadhārābhiḥ siṃcayitvāmahītalam || 197||

rudraṃprasādayāmāsaśūlinaṃ caṃdraśekharam |
tatonṛtyaṃ mahadṛṣṭvā bhagavānubhaktavatsalaḥ || 98 ||

uvāca

bāṇaṃ saṃtuṣṭonṛtyagītapri yoharaḥ |
varaṃgṛhāṇa daityeṃdrayatte manasivartate || 99 ||

bāṇenacagṛhīto'bhūddarastuvraṇaropaṇe |
bāhuyuddhasya cotpattirgāṇapa tyamathā'kṣayam || 200||

uṣāyāḥ putrarājyaṃtutasmin śoṇitakāvhaye |
nirvairatācavibudhairviṣṇaubhaktiranuttamā || 1 ||

vā sudevāttumaraṇaṃkalpatikupitādvaraḥ |
napunardaityatāduṣṭātāmasīrajasāyutā || 2 ||

harermaṃtrābhiyogastudivārātraṃcamitratā |
sarvalabhiṣyasītyuktvātatraivāṃtaradhīyata || 3 ||

rudrasyānucarobāṇomahākālatvamāgataḥ |
tataḥ kadācitputrastu tasyāṃjāto yadā'bhavat ||4||

tathākubhāṃḍamukhyaiśvaviśvajidrājasattamaḥ |
daityarājyebhiṣiktastu maṃtribhiḥ keśavājñayā || 5 ||

tasyānva vāyeye kecidākalpāṃtāstubhūbhujaḥ |
jāyamānājaniṣyāvāte'niruddhasya sūnavaḥ || 6 ||

uṣācacitralekhācasacarājācavi śvajit |
tasminkalpeparikṣīṇetāraḥśāṃkaraṃ padam || 7 ||

ititrinayananāmnaḥśaṃkarasyāpivṛttaṃsakalagurujanānāṃ sadguroḥ kathitamihamayāteśrotraramyairvacobhirmadanaśaravaṇāgnermanmathakṣobhitasya || 208 ||


munayaūcuḥ

koyaṃkāmohiyeneśovyāptapūrvamatirbhavaḥ |
nabhovāyvagnirāpovai pūrvibuddhirmano'pyaham || 1 ||

prakṛtiḥpuruṣo hṛtsthaḥ puruṣoṃ'guṣṭhamātrakaḥ |

sūtauvāca

kāmasyaiṣātuyāmūrtirbrahmaviṣṇvīśvarātmikā || 3 ||

sarvabhūtātmabhūtākhyātriliṃgāviśvarūpiṇī |
kvacitkā maḥkvacitkāmaḥkvacittukāma evahi || 4 ||

snānaṃdhyānaṃcayogaścayamaścaniyamojapaḥ |
nośaṃbhurnaivavāviṣṇurnobrahmāneṃ dubhāskarau || 5||

kartumicchaṃtiyaṃ muktvākāmaḥ saṃ kalpaevahi |
yajñaśvaśikṣāsvādhyāyojñānadāne śrutiḥsmṛtiḥ ||6||

aśuddhiḥparapīḍācasvargamokṣaparigrahaḥ |
snehavairāṇiye nehabhavaṃtisamanobhavaḥ ||7||

devānāṃyonayaśvā'ṣṭaumānuṣīnavamīcayā |
tiraśrāṃyonayaḥpaṃcabhavatyevaṃ caturdaśa || 8 ||

bhūtā vāvartamānāvājaniṣyāpicasarvaśaḥ |
kāmātsarvepravartate līyate vṛddhimāgatāḥ || 9||

sarvāsāmathayonīnāṃmithunānāṃ krame tu |
anyonyāsaktabuddhīnāṃkāmonenavibhāgaśaḥ || 10 ||

sthitojitajagajjanmadhāgnisaṃsāravartmani |
śabdādyairviṣaye ramyainiḥsāraiḥ kusumopamaiḥ || 11 ||

brahmedropeṃdrarudrāṇāṃ devadānavabhoginām |

baṃdhurmitramathācāryairakṣānetā'rthadoguruḥ |
kalpadrumo'thavāśyātāpitāmātāyamastathā || 13 ||

vaitariṇyāṃjalaṃmiṣṭaṃnarakonaṃdanaṃvanam |
kāmaḥ sarvamayaṃ puṃsāṃ svayaṃkalpasamudbhavaḥ || 14 ||

vaktuṃnaśakyateyacca paraṃ cānuparaṃca yat |
kevalaṃbuddhigamyaṃcasaṃsparśaprāṃtasaṃsthitam || 15||

ānaṃdamamṛtaṃdivyaṃ paraṃbrahmataducyate |
arkai duvanhiprabhavaṃ yene hacchi dyatetamaḥ || 16 ||

yasmādvikārāḥ sūryeduvakyaniladharāstvamī |
brahmedropeṃdramanujāḥ kṛmayaḥ paśavomṭagāḥ || 17 ||

utpadyate tathābrahmadivyamānaṃdamavyayam |
paramātmeticāpyuktaṃvikārāḥ kāmasaṃjñitāḥ || 18 ||

suptānāṃ jāgratāṃvātha sarveṣāṃ yothṛdi sthitaḥ |
nānāvidhānikarmāṇikurutebrahmatanmahat || 19 ||

kāmasaṃjñābhavetteṣāṃ tatkṛtānāṃtukarmaṇām |
evaṃsaṃsāracakaṃtu śaktiśaktimatoridaṃ || 20 ||

śabdapriyāpriyauyastutathāsparśopriyāpriyau |
priyāpriyāṇirūpāṇirasāngaṃdhānpriyā priyān || 21 ||

virūpaḥ satuvijñeyaḥ kartākarmacayogibhiḥ || 22 ||

tayorvinaśvaraṃ karmakartātunavinaśyati |
yathācitrasahasrāṇi kurutecitrakarmakṛt || 23 ||

tathākarmasahasrāṇi kurute parameśvaraḥ |
ekaiva pa ramātmātudvidhābhinnastu dṛśyate || 24 ||

bhoktābhojyaṃcaloke'sminnavinājñānacakṣuṣā |
bhoktaivasaṃsthitodevaḥ svecchayāpara movibhuḥ || 25 ||

yeyevikārādṛśyaṃtevyavahārāścabhūtale |
nateṣāṃsaṃśayaḥ kāryaḥ sarvatraparameśvaraḥ || 26||

nirākāraṃ mahāghoraṃ svasaṃvedyaṃ paraṃ dhruvam |
trivṛdbrahmatatoviśvaṃkāmaśvecchātrayaṃ kṛtam || 27 ||

sthūlaśvā''kāravānkā movidyatetrahmasaṃbhavaḥ |

caturvakomahāratnasuvarṇakusumāvṛtaḥ |
yuktolakṣmyāsarasvatyāratyāprītyāca sarvaśaḥ ||29||

kī yāṃśāṃvyātathāpuṣṭyātuṃṣṭyā pakṣīśvareṇatu |
āsīddevayugedevaḥ pradyumnobhagavānhariḥ || 30 ||

kāmaḥ kṛtayugecāthababhūvaja gatāṃpriyaḥ |
pūrṇeduvadanaḥ śyāmodiṣyalāvaṇyalocanaḥ ||31||

kuṃcitāsitakeśaścamaṇihemavibhūṣitaḥ |
pāśāṃkuśadhanurbā ṇoraktākṣaḥ kṣīrasāgare || 32 ||

sthitolakṣmyāsubhadrādyārukmiṇyābhadrayātathā |
raktoratyāsahāyastu kadācinmanmayoga taḥ || 33 ||

saṃpreṣitastuvibudhaiḥ purīṃcaṃdraprabhāṃkila |
tapacaṃdralalāṭasyapradharṣayitumeva hi || 34 ||

divyaiḥ śabdādibhirghorairbāṇaiḥ puṣpamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
unmīlyane tredṛṣṭaśvakailāsanilayenatu || 35 ||

pṛṣṭaśvako sikenāpisacoktovetsyasetumām |
kāmosī tihareṇoktobrahmaṇoktaḥ punaścasaḥ || 36 ||

rudramāṃvetsyasediṣṭyātenaṣpṛṣṭaḥ punaścasaḥ |
kimāgamanakṛtyaṃtetenoktastvāṃvimohi tum ||37||

kathamityevatenoktastenoktosaubalārditaḥ |
kiṃtebalaṃcatenoktaḥ paśyame kusumāniyat ||38||

ityuktvā puṣpabāṇai stuhṛdayaṃ bhettumudyataḥ |
kāmacaṃdralalāṭasyatataḥ kruddhaḥ sadāruṇaḥ || 39 ||

lalāṭanayanādagnirniṣkrāṃtoyenamanmathaḥ |
mahāraktā ruṇodagdhaḥsarvabhūtamanoharaḥ || 40 ||

tasmindagdhemahādevaṃstutvākāmāṃta karatiḥ |
prajāpatisutālebhevaraṃbāleṃduśekharāt ||41||

mayasyacagṛhejanmavaṃcanaṃśaṃbarasyaca |
bhartrāsamāgamobhūyodvāparasyaviparyaye ||42||

rudreṇayāparādagdhālalāṭanayanāgninā |
kāmasya mūrtirādyāsāpunarjātāharergṛhe || 43 ||

trailokyajayinoviṣṇoḥ prādyumnītai jasīśubhā |
haritaḥśyāmavarṇācasarvabhūtamanoharā || 44 ||

rukmiṇyāṃjātamātrastu śaṃbareṇadṛtaḥ purā |
tāmasaṃjñānamāśri tyamāyāvatyaisamarpitaḥ || 45 ||

tayāsaṃvardhitobhūyaḥ pūrvaratyāpatiścasaḥ |
yodhitastu tatastenaśaṃbaronihatoyudhi || 46 ||

sācakāmasyabhāryāsyādratirlabdhvāvaraṃharāt |
śaṃbaraṃvaṃcayitvātubhūyolabhepaticatam || 47 ||

sacāpikāmastāṃbhāryāla bdhvābhūyo'bhavatsudhīḥ |
śaṃbarasyadhanaṃdārāngṛhītvādvāra kāṃgataḥ || 48 ||

bhūyonārāyaṇagṛhaṃ prāpyasaṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ |
rājyaṃtu- śaṃbarapure'karoddiṣṇubalāśrayāt || 49 ||

krīḍataṃdvārakodyāne kadācicchubhadānavaḥ |
nītvāśvātredadau prītyānbhakṣayemaṃ paśuṃ tviti ||50||

tasmātsaṃgṛtyataṃdūranabhonītvājahautataḥ |
niśuṃbhovibudhasthānāddaśamādvātavartmanaḥ || 51 ||

tataḥ saṃvidhyaśai lāgreśuṃbhasyanagareśanaiḥ |
patitolabdhasaṃjñastu prayumnodhanvināṃvaraḥ || 52 ||

udyānedaityakanyāṃ ca labdhvālakṣmīṃjahāraca |
kṣī rodakanyāṃsannāmnīṃkṣatradharmeṇadharmavit || 53 ||

nāgapāśaistatobadhvāsa bhāryāviṃdhyaparvatāt |
vinirjitya mahābuddhinitvā saṃsthāpitogirau ||54||

himācalasyaśṛṃgetuvitastātīrajetataḥ |
vajrapaṃjaramadhyasthorakṣamāṇastudānavaiḥ || 55 ||

viṣamai nāgabhāgaistudatya mānoviṣolvaṇaiḥ |
durgāsaṃsmṛtavān bhaktyāviṣṇuśaktiṃmahābalāṃ || 56 ||

smṛtamātrāpisā'paśyadrakṣya māṇaṃcataṃtadā |
nānāvidhairupāyaistatpaṃjaraṃnaśaśākasā ||57||

yadāpraveṣṭuṃśastrāstraiḥsannaddhaṃcatadātusā |
sārikārūpamaka rotyaṃcamadhurasvaram ||58||

sārikāṃvāśanīṃdṛvālakṣmīḥ śuṃbhāsurātmajā |
cidaṃtaḥpurapatiya bhāṣemattakāminī || 59 ||

bharturvinodanārthatumamāpicamahāmate |
dīyatāṃsārika bidhvāyadijānāsi sauhṛdam || 60 ||

krīḍārthaitudadautasyainiḥśaṃkodānavaścasaḥ || 61 ||

vajrapaṃjara madhyaṃ tuprāpyasāsārikāpunaḥ || 62 ||

oṃnamaḥ śivāya navamevividhākhyānamukhena smaramukhyatā |
śivasya sarvotkarṣaścasaṃkṣepeṇanirūpyate || 1 ||

tatrokte ghoraṃtadrūpamāśrityavidadārāthapaṃjaram |
tasminsaṃcūrṇiteghoredurbheyadevadānavaiḥ || 63 ||

devyānīlānavapuṣāmahālāvaṇya yuktayā |
śrīmatpratyagratāruṇyayuktayāgopakanyayā || 64 ||

tataḥ śuṃbhagadāṃ prāpyapaṃjaradvārasaṃsthitām |
pradyumnaudaityasaṃghātaṃ nirjaghāna mahābalaḥ || 65 ||

tataḥśuṃbhaniśuṃbhautudṛṣṭvā sainya kṣayaṃ mahat |
pradyumnaṃpāśanirmuktaṃ cūrṇitaṃpaṃjaraṃ mahat || 66 ||

vāsudevasyabhaginīṃdṛṣṭvākāmavaśaṃgatau |
parasparamathābrūtāṃbhbhrātarauyuddhalālasau || 67 ||

mameyaṃtavaneyaṃsyādahamenāṃharāmi bhoḥ |
śatroriyaṃtrāṇabhūtāmamayenahṛtāsutā || 68 ||

evaṃtuvakṣyamāṇau proktaugopeṃdrakanyayā |
anenakṣatradharmeṇahṛte yaṃduhitātava || 69 ||

labhyātyahamanenaivavidhinādānavarṣabhau |
parākramaṃ bhavadbhyāṃtu paśyeyamitimemanaḥ || 70 ||

tasyāstadva canaṃśrutvātatoyuddhaṃpracakratuḥ |
paśyatāmathasarveṣāṃpradyumnanikaṭemahat || 71 ||

tatomahāgadāghātairanyonyenanipātitaiḥ |
tenopāyenatauhatvāmocayitvātuyādavam || 72 ||

sabhāryadvārakāṃnītvādevī cāṃ'tardadhepunaḥ |
tasyāṃputrastu yojātaḥ saśuṃbha nagare'bhavat || 73 ||

rājāparamadharmātmā viṣvaksenaitiśrutaḥ |
māyāvatyāstuyaḥ putroma yonāmnānṛkesarī || 74 ||

sarśa barapurerājābabhūvaripumardanaḥ || 75 ||

ititava kusumeṣodivyamāhātmyayuktaṃlalitapadavacobhiḥ śrotra peyairmanojñaiḥ |
tadanuṣṭa: ṇurahasyaṃ manmathasyaprabhāvaṃ sakalajanamanāṃsikṣobhituṃ yastujātaḥ || 76 ||



sūta uvāca ||


yogeṃdrāṇāṃ pradhānaśvasaṃsārabhayanāśanaḥ |
duṣṭanirharaṇaḥ śrīmānkṣīrodārṇavasaṃbhavā m || 2 ||

lakṣmīmurasisaṃdhattemanmathenavaśīkṛtaḥ |
purādevāsurāḥ sarvejarāmṛtyubhayārditāḥ || 3 ||

mamaṃthuramṭataṃ divyaṃkṣīrodesāgarottame |
amṛtemathyamānetuśrīśvalokamaheśvarī || 4 ||

dhanvaṃtariśaśāṃkaucapārijātomahādrumaḥ |
uccaiḥśravāścaturagogāvaḥ pīyūṣamevaca ||5||

punaśvamathyamāneca kālakūṭaṃmahāviṣam |
yugāṃtānalasaṃkāśaṃ surāsurabhayāvaham || 6 ||

pīyūṣajanmakāletuṃ biṃdavoyebahirgatāḥ |
tebhyaḥ kanyāḥ samudbhūtā anekādbhutadarśanāḥ || 7||

śaratpūrṇeduvadanāstaḍitsūryānalaprabhāḥ |
kāśvidutpalasaṃkāśāḥkāścitpulakasannibhāḥ || 8 ||

kāścitkanakasaṃkāśāḥ kāściddidrumasannibhāḥ |
masārasadṛśāḥ kāścitkācidrajatasaṃnibhāḥ || 9||

suprabhāḥpadmagarbhābhāḥ phullanīlājalocanāḥ |
lāvaṇyāmṛtatoyenasiṃ cayaṃtyodiśodaśa || 10 ||

duḥkhaśokajarāmṛtyurogāṇāmamṛtaprabhāḥ |

bhiṃdaṃtihṛdayānīvarasāyāmadhurasvanaiḥ |
jagadunmādayaṃtyevaśśrūbhaṃgāpāṃgavīkṣaṇaiḥ || 12 ||

koṭiśastāḥ samutpannāstvammṛtāḥ kāmamiśritāḥ |
tato'mṛtaṃsamutpannaṃjarāmṭatyunivāraṇam || 13||

kālakūṭaṃśaśāṃkaṃcaśarīredhṛtavānharaḥ |
jagrāhārkasturaṃgaṃtupārijātaṃśacīpatiḥ || 14 ||

kaustubhaṃcāśrayadviṣṇuḥ sarvedhanvaṃtariṃjanāḥ |
amṛtārthakṛtaṃyuddhadevairdaityaiḥparasparam || 15 ||

yugāṃtamivasaṃkṣubdhairanalārkasamaprabhaiḥ |
ṭataṃ somaṃcadaiteyairvalādviṣṇuśvamāyayā || 16 ||

striyāṃvilāsarūpiṇyāpunarvaiviṣṇunāhṛtam |
dṛṣṭvā'naṃdo'bhavatsvargepānastrībhojanādiṣu || 17||

ityukttāpradadustasmai viṣṇave chadmarūpiṇe || 18 ||

etasminnaṃtaredṛṣṭvāstriyodānavapuṃgavāḥ |
anayannammṛtodbhūtāyathāsthānaṃyathāsukham || 19||

tāsāṃpurāṇi divyāni svargācchataguṇānyapi |
ghorairyatraiḥsuguptānimayamāyākṛtānica || 20||

adṛvaiva striyādaityāḥ kṛtvāsamayameva hi || 21||

etāḥstriyonaspṛśāmoyadide vairvinirjitāḥ |

pūrayaṃtaivākāśaṃtarjayaṃtobalāhakān |

āyudhairvividhairatrairjighāṃsaṃtaḥ parasparam |
tatodaityāhatādevairviṣṇunācamahātmanā || 24||

hatāvaviṣṭāḥ pātālaṃviviśurvivarāṇica |
anuvavrājatānviṣṇuścakrapāṇirmahābalaḥ || 25 ||

pātālaṃ paramaṃgatvāsaṃsthi nābhītabhītavat |

kāṃta pūrve duvadanādivyalāvaṇyagarvitāḥ |
samohitaḥ kāsa bāṇairlabhetatraivanirvṛtim ||27||

tābhiśvavaranārībhiḥ krīḍamānobabhūvaha |

kaṃpayaṃtā mahīṃ sarvāṃnānāyuddhaviśāradān |
etasminnaṃtarebrahmādevadevaṃvyasarjayat || 29 ||

svargasyarakṣaṇārthāya viṣṇorāna yanāyaca |
tatovṛṣabharūpeṇagarjamānaḥ pinākadhṛk || 30||

praviṣṭovivaraṃ tatra vinadan bhairavānvān |
niṣpetustasyaninadaiḥ purāṇyaṃtaḥpurāṇica || 31 ||

tataḥ kruddhāhareḥ putrāḥ saṃgrāmāyavyanodayan |
tāndrodaṃta rūpeṇa khuraiḥ śṛṃgairvyadārayat || 32 ||

hate puteṣunikramyayayauviṣṇurharāṃtikam |
śaraiḥ saṃtāḍayāmāsadivyairakhaiśvakeśavaḥ || 33 ||

astrāṇitāniviṣṇośca jagrāsagi rigocaraḥ |
harirvijñāya gaurīśamāgataṃtaṃjagatpatim || 34 ||

prāhagaṃbhīrayāvācābhagavankṣamyatāmiti |

ātmānaṃkiṃnajānīṣetvaṃhiviśvasya kāraṇam |
tvayānā'caratiḥ kāryānivartasva mamājñayā || 36 ||

tacchrutvāvrīḍamānastuviṣṇuḥ prāhamaheśvaram |
mamātravidyate cakraṃtagrahīṣyāmisatvaraḥ || 37 ||

tamuvāca

mahādevaścakramatraivatiṣṭhatām |
duṣṭānadaiityasaṃghānāṃ china ttuvadanānyapi || 38 ||

ahaṃghoratamaṃtasmāccakramanyaddadā mite |
etaduktvāharolikhya ddivyaṃ kālānalaprabham || 39 ||

viṣṇave pradadau cakaṃ ghorārkāyutasuprabham |
labdhvāsudarśanaṃcā' nyatkeśihāvibudhāṃstataḥ || 40 ||

uvāca

nāryaḥ pātālevidyateyauvanaṃgatāḥ |
tābhiḥ sārddhamahākrīḍāṃyaḥkarotikarotusaḥ ||41||

tacchruchrutvākeśavādvākyaṃsarvāstridaśayonayaḥ |
praveṣṭukāmāḥ pātālaṃviṣṇunāsahitāstataḥ || 42 ||

etasminnaṃtare kā levijñāyabhagavānharaḥ |
krodhācchāpaṃdadaughoraṃ deva yonyaṃṣṭakasyaca || 43 ||

varjayitvāmurniśāṃtaṃdānavaṃ vāmadaṃśajam |
idaṃ yaḥpraviśetsthānaṃsayātunidhanaṃkṣaṇāt || 44 ||

śrutvāvākyamidaṃ ghoraṃ manuṣya hitavarddhanam |
pratyākhyātāstu rudreṇadevāḥsvagṛ hamabhyayuḥ ||45||

kasyacittvatha kālasyacitāviṣṭaṃmaheśvaram |
uvācapārvatīdevī kiṃcitayasiśaṃkara || 46 ||

evaṃ saṃcoditodevyāprāhavākyaṃtrilocanaḥ ||47||

vaivarāṇāṃcastrīṇāṃcarūpaṃ saṃcitayāmyaha m |
trailokyetādṛśīnārīyato'dyāpinavidyate || 48 ||

ahobata mahadduḥkhaṃyatratvama sivismitaḥ ||49||

gaṃtumicchāmyahaṃ draṣṭuṃyayanujñābhavenmama |
evaṃ kuruṣvetyuktvātu śivena paramātmanā || 50 ||

praviveśabi laṃ tūrṇa va kacchāyeva darpaṇam |
tāmabhyetyāṃganāḥsarvāḥsūryamityevacitayan || 51 ||

bhāskaro'yaṃsamuditaḥ kiṃsvitpātālaga vhare |
itisaṃciṃtayaṃtyaśvakanyārūpamadālasāḥ || 52 ||

pārvatītutatodṛṣṭvānārcayatīrvimohitāḥ |
tāsāṃrūpaṃ samālokyasaṃ. dṛṣṭāprāhapārvatī || 53 ||

yūyaṃviṣalatākārāvyārūpāṇiviśya |
śvaścepuṣpaphalānīvadurbodha kamalānica ||54||

yuṣmadīyaṃtathārūpamabhogyaṃ sarvadehinām |
striyaḥ puṃsopabhogārthaṃ prajāpatirathā'sṛjat || 55 ||

kanyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvākeyaṃ syādityaciṃtayan |
jñātvātupārvatīṃtāṃ tutobhūmaunipatyaca || 56 ||

stotrairnānāvidhākārairaṃbikāṃsamatopayan |
tatastu ṣṭācasātābhyovaraṃdattavatīpunaḥ || 57 ||

yuṣmākaṃpatayaḥ śamāḥ praviśataścaśaṃbhunā |
idānīṃmamaputrāśvamanuṣyāḥ śāṃkarāga ṇāḥ || 58 ||

jñānavijñānapūtāśca maṃtrapūtāścatāpasāḥ |

ramavaṃtairnaraiḥ sārdhami tyuktvāṃtaradhīyata |
evaṃstrīlaṃpaṭoviṣṇuḥ pūrvamāsītprajāpatiḥ || 60 ||

bhrātṛṇāṃdaityamukhyānāṃhatānāṃdāruṇeyudhi |
striyoṭa 'tvātupātālecikrīḍacamumodaca || 61 ||

tretāyugerāmarūpīviṣṇuḥ saṃprāpya jānakīṃ |
noṭamaḥ strīvilāsānāṃvittasyacasu tasyaca ||62||

retaḥsaṃpreṣaṇaścāpiproṣitasya striyāmapi |
tasmāt ‌kaliyuge bhūyogṛhītvā janmakeśavaḥ || 63 ||

vasudevasya devakyāṃmathurāyāṃ mahābalaḥ |
bālastugopakanyābhirvanekrīḍāṃcakārasaḥ ||64||

daśalakṣāṇiputrāṇāṃ gopālānāṃsa sarjaha |
tatastuyauvanākrāṃtorukmiṇīṃpradadarśa || 65 ||

vivāhayitvāputrāṃśca pradyumnāyāṃśvanirmame |
tathāpinarakaṃdaityaṃprājyo tipapatiṃbalāt || 66 ||

hatvāstrīṇāṃsahasrāṇipoḍaśaivajahāra saḥ |
tāsāṃratiphalaṃ bhuktvāputrāṇāṃnavartitathā || 67 ||

saha srāṇi sasarjāśumatsyecāṃ'ḍaṃmahādbhutam |
strīṇāṃtathāpinotṛptodivyānāṃturate ryadā || 68 ||

tadārādhāstriyaṃ kāṃcinniśidhai ryādadharṣayat |
tathāpi paranārīṇāṃlaṃpaṭonityamevahi || 69 ||

sakāmācā'nuraktāca yatonārīsudurlabhā |
vicitraṃ hisukhaṃ muktāvityāhuryematanatat || 70 ||

bhāti meśmavadīśovātataḥ prakṛtimāsthitaḥ |
yadānyonyahṛtaṃ cetoyūnoḥ samupajāyate ||71 brahmāpadmodbhavaḥ śāṃtoyogadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ || 72||

caturvakraśvaraktāṃgogurūṇāṃ sadguru mahān |
dīrghāyurjanakaḥprājñovedavedāṃgapāragaḥ || 73 ||

gauryāvivāhetatpādaudṛṣṭvā praskhalito'bhavat |
yatrate vālakhilyā stujātāḥsabrahmacāriṇaḥ || 74 ||

itikathitamaśeṣaṃdivyanīlānakāṃtermadhuripuritināmnāśuddha nīlānnavarṇam |
caritamati rahasyaṃ divyalāvaṇyayuktaṃmadanavijitabuddheḥ padma yoneśvaviprāḥ || 75 ||


|| 9 ||

sūtauvāca

śvetorakko mahānīlobhinnāṃjanacayopamaḥ |
sarvarūpadharorudrodaṃṣṭrālodā ritānanaḥ || 1 ||

śāṃtodāṃtojitakrodho saṃyamīvigataspṛhaḥ |
nārāyaṇastato jātaḥsṛṣṭyarthatenayojitaḥ || 2 ||


tapaso'te kariṣyāmisṛṣṭimityevaciṃtyasaḥ |
tapasāsupariśrāṃtaḥ kadācitkṣīrasāgare || 4 ||

suṣvāpasuciraṃ kālaṃ durlakṣyastuharoyadā |
tadāpirajasāviṣṇuḥ satvenacacatu rmukhaḥ || 5 ||

prasādajaḥpunaḥsṛṣṭoniyuktovidhivatkṛtaḥ |
prathamaṃtāmasaṃsargapaṃcabhedamathā'sṛjat || 6 ||

tatastumānuṣaṃsargama sṛjadrājasaṃśanaiḥ |
daivamaṣṭaprakāraṃtusātvikaṃcā'sṛjatkramāt ||7||

tataḥsthānānidharmāśvajīvitaṃparipālanam |
āhārama pisarveṣāṃtathācānyonyabhakṣaṇāt ||8 ||

kṛtvā sarvagatadevastatraivāṃtaradhīyata |
evaṃrute tato rudrasyaktvānidrāṃ samutthitaḥ ||9||

saṃsaransṛṣṭikāmaśvadadṛśepūritaṃjagat |
bhūtairnānāprakāraistu jagadetaccarācaram || 10 ||

urdhvamadhye'thapātāletādṛk sthānaṃna vidyate |
yannabhūtagaṇairvyāptaṃmithunairyugmacāribhiḥ || 11 ||

modamānaṃjagadṛṣṭvā sṛṣṭiheturmahādbhutam |
cicchedakupitoliṃgaṃ kimaneneticityasaḥ || 12 ||

mahājvālāmayaṃghorastaṃbhabhūtaṃjagatraye |
yasyanatiṃna madhyaṃcanomūlamihadṛśyate || 13 ||

nopa mānaṃpramāṇaṃvātejasovāsthavīyasaḥ |
praśāṃtaroṣastatkṛtvārudrarthyā'tardadhepunaḥ ||14||

tatobahutithekāle gatedarpasamanvitau |
brahmaviṣṇūprakupitauliṃgaṃparamapaśyatām || 15 ||

jvālāliṃgaṃsamudrāṃ jvalaṃtaṃsvena tejasā |
kasyedamitisaṃciṃtyasarvākā raṃsulakṣaṇam ||16||

taddṛṣṭvādarpanaśanaṃtayoranyonyadarśanāt |
śāṃtadapatatastau tu pitāputraucasauhṛdāt || 17 ||

parasparaṃsamā liṃgyakāryametatpracakratuḥ |
idaṃjvālāmayaṃliṃgaṃkasya syāditicityatau ||18||

yasmālliṃgasahasrāṇinirgacchaṃtiviśaṃtica anveṣyāvo'syayanmūlaṃyadūrdhvacehadṛśyate || 19||

viṣṇunācata tomūla manveṣṭuṃ brahmaṇātathā |
samyagūrdhvacayugapadyāvadaṃtonavi dyate || 20 ||

tatastenaivamārgeṇa niṣkramya jaladhestaṭe |
parasparaṃsamāliṃgyaśrāṃtoviviśatuśvatau || 21 ||

aśrauṣatāṃtatovā caṃnabhasaḥ kasyacittuha |
idaṃ caṃdralalāṭasyaliṃgaṃjvālāmayaṃ surau || 22 ||

kupitenatuyacchitvābhūmautyaktaṃkapardinā |
etatsaṃ pūjyasiddhirvābhaviṣyatyadbhutopamā || 23||

etaddhiśāṃkaraliṃgaṃ pūjayiṣyaṃtiye bhuvi |
balipuṣpopahāraiśvateṣāṃ prītaḥ pinākadhṛ kū || 24 ||

pradāsyatyuttamān kāmānsadevobhaktavatsalaḥ |
tacchrutvācakratuḥ stotraṃ tasmaidevāyaraṃhasā || 25 ||

taupūjayitvāvi dhivatsaṃprāptausiddhimuttamām |
tato'nyasminmahākalpedevairdṛṣṭastuśaṃkaraḥ || 26 ||

ardhanārīśarīrastu kāmaśatruḥ pinākadhṛk |
anyasminkalpasamaye gṛhasthāśramavānapi || 27 ||

himavaṃtaguhāyāṃtubhūtasaṃghaiḥsamāvṛtaḥ |
kadācitpārvatītatra kālīgauratvalavyaye || 28 ||

brahmāṇamarcayitvātutapastaptuṃgatāvanam |
divyaṃsatīsaraśvāpijayaṃtīṃtu mahāguhām || 29 ||

rājavāsaguhāṃcāpitatradṛṣṭvāpitāmaham |
harigātraiḥ samagraiśvasaṃspṛśyaca mahāmatiḥ ||30||

śuddhajāṃbūnadaprakhyāprasādādbrahmaṇo'bhavat |
tapovanaṃtugacchaṃtyā uddāmakusumāsakhī || 31 ||

bhartuḥ srīlaṃpaṭasyāpi rakṣārthaviniveśitā |
ekasmindvāradeśetudvitīyo vīrakaḥ sutaḥ || 32 ||

īrṣyādoṣeṇa tāvuktau bhavadbhyāṃnityamevahi |
rakṣitavyolaṃpaṭo'yaṃ yathā'nyāṃmagṛhekhiyaṃ ||33||

praveśyanopabhoktāsyātpatimai jānhavīpriyaḥ |
saṃdhyārāgīkāmaśatruḥ saṃdhyārāgasamaprabhaḥ || 34||

bāḍhaṃbāḍhamitītyuktvātāvidānīṃpraṇamyatām |
gaurīddāragataunityaṃdivārātraṃtutasthatuḥ || 35 ||

āḍīnāmatatodaityastasminkālepitāmaham |
ārādhyatapasādevamamaratvamayācata ||36||

tataḥ satenadurmedhāna kiṃcidamarastviti |
amaratvemahādaityahetumātraṃgṛhāṇabhoḥ || 37 ||

tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvādaityohetuṃtamuktavān |
devaraudramuhūrtetu ṛkṣe cāṃdratisaṃjñite ||38||

yadārupatrayaṃdeva kurmimamitadātvaham |
ākhaṃḍalomahāvṛṣṭipuṣkarāvartakairghanaiḥ || 39 ||

kalpāpāyenirukaṃdraḥsarīsartyanilomahān |
śikhaṃḍīcanarī nartidṛṣṭvā pūrṇapayodharam || 40 ||

yadātadāme bhagavanpradānādastu memmṛtiḥ |
evamastviti tenoktaḥ sa daityo mudito'bhavat || 41 ||

mattaḥ kādaṃbarī pītvātuṣāraśikharaṃyayau |
yatrasāśaṃkara guhādākṣāyaṇyāgṛhaṃ mahat || 42 ||

uddāma kusumāṃtatravīrakaṃcadadarśa saḥ |
papracchatausatābhyāṃcayathāvattamathā'śṛṇot || 43 ||

daityastrīlaṃpaṭarudrasaṃvijñāyatayormukhāt |
praveṣṭukāmastuguhāṃpau rupaṃrūpamatyajat ||44||

āśrityasarparūpaṃ tupraviśyacaguhāṃtaram |
tenopāyena taumūḍhaumohayitvācamūḍhadhīḥ || 45||

gaurīrū paṃkṣaṇātkṛtvātṛtīyamapicā'dbhutam |
maheśvaramuvācedaṃvirahotkaṃṭhamānasam || 46 ||

bhobhocaṃdralalāṭā'haṃ pārvatī girigo carā |
saṃprāṃtotkaṃṭhitātubhyaṃbahmadattavarāsatī || 47 ||

paśyajāṃbūnadaprakhyāṃmāṃ krīḍasvacamepriya |
tadvākyonmīlitākṣastu virūpākṣaḥsphuṭākṣaram || 48 ||

priyāmivabhujābhyāṃtuparyaṣvajatadṛṣṭavat |
kṛtrimāṃturatikṛtvā tatobātyaratiṃpunaḥ || 49 ||

aṃtarecagatoliṃgaṃsadadarśa mahādbhutam |
viśvakarmakṛtaṃtasyāṃkanakābhaṃmahadhimat || 50 ||

paśvātriśūlamadhyetuyathāsadbhāva jāṃratim |
snehasadbhāva liṃgaṃta ttacca gātraṃ mahāmṛdu || 51 ||

tathāpiśaṃkitaḥ kiṃcijjātaḥ kāmāturaśvasaḥ |
cakārasuratakrīḍāṃ tāvadvajjamayobhagaḥ ||52||

utpādyadaṃḍamuśa letasya liṃgavikartanam |
kartusamudyato ghorodānavaḥ strīvapurdharaḥ || 53 ||

haropitāṃ mahāmāyāṃvijñāyatvanyathā'sṛjat |
śūlapāśupatādīniliṃgāgrānniḥsṛtānyapi ||54||

tatoratāṃte rudrasya daityārūpaṃ jahaukhi yāḥ |
paricchinnasuhṛdvaṃdhuvipayāṇīṃdriyāṇica || 55 ||

hatvā daityaṃmahādevovīrakāyāpyadarśayat |
tataḥ saśaṃkitaḥ strīṇāṃvi śvāsaṃnā'karotvacit || 56 ||

tatradaityenapatatāpṛṣṭenokto maheśvaraḥ |
he bhadradāna va varakhvayāyuddhe parājitaḥ ||57||

ahaṃmṛtomamavātāvidyate sumahābalaḥ |
tvahiṃtuṃsaihāgaṃtāpuna gauravapurdharaḥ || 58 ||

tvāṃmārayiṣyatyathavāmamamārgagamiṣyati |
śatroruktvāvacastvetatpapātacamamāraca || 59 ||

itithṛtkamale rutvā yadīyaṃnatupārvatī |
svayamevapriyābhāryāmahāsādhvīmanāgasā ||60||

praviṣṭāstrītisaṃciṃtyarudraḥ krodhavaśānugaḥ |
tasminkāle mahādaityaṃdṛṣṭvānārīvapurdharam || 61 ||

uhāmakusumāgatvāgauryai sarva nyavedayat |
devinā'haṃ samarthā'smipatisaṃrakṣituṃtava || 62 ||

vīrakeṇa mahādhanyākācinnārīpraveśitā |
tayā sahamahākrīḍāṃcakrebāleṃduśekharaḥ || 63 ||

ahaṃtuvrīḍitācā''saṃdṛṣṭvāta krīḍitavapuḥ |
tacchrutvākupitāputramaśapadvīrakaṃcasā ||64||

gaurī labdhavarābhūtvā svagṛhaṃgaṃtumudyatā |
prāṅmukhīcābravīdvākyaṃtoyenā''camya satvarā ||65||

manuṣyobhavitāviprojanmamṛtyujarāśrayī |
vīrakoyenamenārīgṛhekācitpraveśitā ||66||

pramādināvīrakeṇastriyorudronarakṣitaḥ |

dadarśasvagṛheputraṃ patiṃ cā'nāgasaṃtathā |

liṃgaṃtadarcyatāmasya sarvasiddhipradaṃprabhoḥ maṃtrairvedādibhiḥ puṇyairmanovākkāyasaṃyutam || 5 ||

cakuraṣṭādaśāṃgāṃtupūjaliṃgāyaśaṃbhave |

najānaṃti yatobrahmacinmātrasvethṛdisthitam |

adatvāsaṃvibhāgaṃtu bhayamarthavatāṃyataḥ |

majjāśanāyacaṃḍāyaparivārayutāyaca |

vidvāṃsaṃtarpayetpatnīnāsāta. tkilaṃpriya |

tābhiryastuvatobhartātasmaideyāmayācatāḥ || 29||

itimatvāmahābuddhemamakṣaṃ tumato'rhasi |
mamakanyāpuraṃvipramanniyogātpraviśyatām || 30 ||

tatryākācidevatvāṃvarayiṣyatimāmakī |

tatovisarjitastenamuniḥ kanyāpuraṃyayau |
ātmayogānmahāyogī rūpaṃ kṛtvāmanoha ram ||32||

rūpayauvanalāvaṇya saubhāgyānāmivāspadam |
tatomunivaraṃ dṛṣṭvākumāryastāḥ suvismitāḥ || 33 ||

ātmā naṃpradadustasmai manmathopamatejase |
tataḥ sudhanvanātasmaidattāḥ sarvāstukanyakāḥ || 34 ||

tasmindivyevivāhetuśukrādyāḥ sumahāgrahāḥ |
svakṣetroccatrikoṇasthāḥ keṃdropacayasattamāḥ || 35 ||

vainateyabhayātsarve śubha saubhāgyaputradāḥ |
āsantsacā'pitāḥ sarvāḥ kumārīḥprāpyadurlabhāḥ || 36 ||

prāpyatāṃrājyalakṣmīṃtucikrīḍa camumodaca |
tāsāṃratiphalaṃ labdhvā putrānprāpyacatatsamān || 37 ||

kṛtakṛtyastataḥ svargajagāmavibudhaiḥ saha |
śāṃḍilīṃ taruṇīṃkanyāma paśyadgaruḍovane || 38||

himācalaguhāsaṃsthāṃsurūpāṃcāruhāsinīm |
vaiśvānarasutāṃ bhaktyānetukāmastriviṣṭape || 39 ||

vi jñātastutayāsayodagdhapakṣobabhūvaha |
athadhyātastayābhūyaḥkṛpayādayayātathā || 40 ||

anurodhāttathāviṣṭohemapakṣaḥ kṣaṇā tkṛtaḥ |
dīrghaśmaśrurjaṭālaśvasurakṣāmāṃgovibhāṃḍakaḥ || 41||

munistīkṣṇana khonadyāṃkadācittrātumudyataḥ |
dūrādabhinavāṃnā rīṃdṛṣṭvāpraskhalito'bhavat || 42 ||

tadretojalasaṃpṛktaṃmṛgyāpītaṃvidhervaśāt |
tatastasyāṃtuputropiekaśṛṃgommṛgo'bhavat ||43||

vibhāṃḍakenamuninārakṣitovardhitastathā |
munivatsaṃskṛtaḥ paśvādvedamadhyāpitastataḥ ||44||

ṛṣyaśṛṃgastatobālaḥśāṃ todāṃtojiteṃdriyaḥ |
śuddhaḥ sabrahmacārīcanityameva na saṃśayaḥ ||45||

śākapiṇyākabhojīca piturbhītastusaṃyataḥ |
svādhyāyī nityajāpīcastrīvilāseṣva kovidaḥ ||46||

athāṃgarāja siṃhenalomapādena māyayā |
śamanārthamanāvaṣṭenītaḥ svaviṣayaṃprati ||47||

nānāvidhairvyājaśataiḥpuṃścalībhiḥ pralobhitaḥ |
vidhvasta puṇyoniḥśaṃkaḥsaṃmukhobhāvasaṃyutaḥ || 48 ||

munīnāmivave śyānāṃpūjakovidhivatkila |
adhītavānbharadvājaḥ saputraḥ susvaraṃhaṭhāt || 49 ||

tataḥ parāvasorbhāryā taruṇīṃgahane vane |
yava kītena rudatīṃstrībhāvamupalaṃbhitāṃ || 50 ||

jñātvāraibhyaḥ khalomānīchitvā krodhavaśaṃgataḥ |
ḍutvāva vhestataḥ kṛtyāmakarodrākṣa sānvitām ||51||

yavakrītaṃgrasa svetipreṣayāmāsatāṃtadā |
tataḥ sāṛṣipatnyāṃśvarūpaṃ kṛtvā manoharam || 52 ||

yavakrītamuvācedamehikrīḍasvamepunaḥ |
saṃsthitaṃcāgniśālāyāmupāyaiśvavikṛṣyatam || 53||

vanejaghāna kupitaḥ śūlahasta śvarākṣasaḥ |
tatovṛddhaḥpitātasya saṃprāptastamapaśyata || 54 ||

mṛtaṃputraṃtataḥ krodhāddarśayanmitrameva hi |
raibhyaṃ mitraputranaṃsvapu comārayiṣyati || 55 ||

etāvaduktvāsamunirvanhiprajvālyakānane |
dadāhadehaṃ putrasya svaśarīraṃ caduḥkhitaḥ || 56 ||

tatastunnṛpa tiyajñeyājayitvā parāvasuḥ |
kṛtāhāroyayauruṣṭaḥ svagṛhaṃtuniśāmukhe || 57 ||

vṛkṣagulmalatāmadhyādborarūpādirestaṭāt || 58 ||

tato'dhakāreraibhyaṃ tu kṛṣṇasārasya carmaṇā |
gṛhītāṃgaṃ tato dṛṣṭvākāṃḍairvivyā dhadāruṇaiḥ || 59 ||

mṛgayāṃtyācataṃ hatvārātryāṃ dagdhvāparāvasuḥ |
prabhāte yajñabhūmiṃ gatvāśbhācenyavedayat || 60 ||

bhobhrāta stātonihatomayābhrāṃtanakānane |

niśamyaparāvasunābhrātrācoktaṃtuta caca |
ahaṃvanaṃ gamiṣyāmitvadarthatvamitoyaja || 62 ||

brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ nāśayi tvātataḥpunaḥ ||63||

kadācidāgatodṛṣṭobhātrātenamahāyaśāḥ |
saṃprāptedakṣiṇākāle dṛṣṭamātra studhikṛtaḥ || 64 ||

reredurjana. tātaghnakiṃpraviṣṭosigacchabhoḥ |

śrāṃtorājajanairghorairgṛhīto janasaṃsadi |
ha nyamānastuśuddhātmānacakrodhamupeyivān || 66 ||

badhvāṃjalimuvācedamagnimiṃdrayamaṃtathā |
hatoyadimayātāto bhavaṃtasta nāgasaḥ || 67 ||

madahadhvamadharmajñakṛtaghnaṃ pāpapūruṣam |
noce pīyūpadhārābhiḥplāvayadhvaṃcatatkṣaṇāt || 68 ||

yadidattaṃya dihutaṃ gurava stoṣitāyadi |
tenasatyenate sarva punarjīvaṃ tumāmakāḥ || 69 ||

tatodevaiḥ prasannaiścapuṇyairamṛtabiṃdubhiḥ |
bharadvājoya vakrītoraibhyaśvotthāpitāḥpunaḥ || 70 ||


kṣīṇasaṃsāradoṣaśvavanavāsīsusaṃyataḥ |
tataḥ kadācidiṃdrastubhayācchāradvatīsutam || 72 ||

preṣayāmāsāpsarasaṃtataścāla yituṃtapaḥ |
dṛṣṭvātāṃtumuneśvetaḥ praviṣṭaṃkāmajavyathām ||73||

vāyurvastrāṇicoddhūyamuneragreva vaummṛdu |
vasaṃtaḥ puṣpasaṃchannān drumāṃśrvakretadāśrame ||74||

nārīvastrāṇicāhartumunerabhimukhaṃyayau |
nagnāmapsarasaṃprekṣyatāṃcadṛṣvāsatāpasaḥ || 75 ||

rarāma kṣubhitoretaḥskaṃnaṃdroṇyāṃdadhāraca |

kadācidrahmaṇovākyānmārtaṃḍobhaga vānpunaḥ |
jajñedeveśvarorājāpṛthivyāṃkhecarovasuḥ || 77 ||

caturāśramarakṣārthaṃcacārapṛthivītale |
mṛgayādharmaśīlava siṃhavyāghrāṃśvataskarān ||78||

mārayaṃśvāpi duṣṭāṃśva rājyasyaparipaṃthinaḥ |
śyenahastaḥ śarīdhanvīrathevyomacaresthitaḥ ||79||

vasaṃtekāma saṃtaptodārebhyodūragogṛhāt |
pālāśapuṭagarbheturetaḥskannamadhārayat || 80 ||

puṭikāṃ pradadau cātha śyenāyapratikā mine |
dārebhyonetumāghrātumayodhyāṃputrakāmukaḥ || 81 ||

rājājñayātataḥ śyenojagāmannṛpatergṛhaṃ |
tāṃcaṃ cupuṭagarbhasyāṃpuṭi kāṃdhārayankhagaḥ ||82||

anyenapakṣiṇādṛṣṭastataśrāmiṣagṛddhinā |
samāhūtastatoyuddhepuṭikāyāmunejale || 83 ||

patitaṃ taccatadretaḥpītaṃgirikayātadā |
divyanāryāmahāmatsyābrahmaśāpābhibhūtayā || 84 ||

paśyatastasya sāmatsītadāgarbhabharāla sā |
gṛhītājalabaṃdhenadāsarājñīsupīvarā || 85 ||

vidāritāpatadgarbhāllebhenṛmithunaṃnṛpaḥ |
tataḥ karārthavasave matsyodaravi nirgatam || 86 ||

kumāraṃpradadaudāśomahāratnamahadhimat |
tataḥ svavīryasaṃbhūtaṃrājoparicarovasuḥ ||87||

dāśātprāpyacasaṃ skṛtyasvīyarājye'bhyaṣecayat |
dāsarājñātataḥ kanyāduhitāvardhitāgṛhe || 88 ||

pitṛkanyātumārtaṃḍīyamunāyoginīnadī |
kaliṃda girijādevīdivyanī lānnasaṃnibhā || 89||

bibhratīmānuṣaṃrūpaṃśākteyasya muneḥkṛte |
kṛtanāmātudāśenasatyāsatyavatīti || 90 ||

parāśarastuśākteyastadṛṣṭvātha punaḥkṣamī |
nīlotpalanibhāṃdṛṣṭvā yamunāṃpuṇyavāhinīm ||91||

cakremanasibhāvaṃtutaruṇīsyādyadīdṛśī |
kāmayānāsamāṃkrīḍāṃ karomisakṛdeva hi || 92 ||

iṃdra stanmatamājñāyakāliṃdīmabravīdvacaḥ |
munestuyadabhipretaṃ tatkuruṣva mahānadi ||93||

tathetisāpratijñāyapraviṣṭāyogamāyayā |
matsīgarbhastatojātobhrātrāmatsyena saṃyutā || 94 ||

matsyadeśe'bhavanmatsyorājārājñovasoḥ kule |
yamunādāśakanyātupi turvacanakāriṇī || 95 ||

tasmiṃstuyāmunetoye nāvaṃvāhayatesadā |
tataḥ kadācinmunināsādṛṣṭāprārthitāsatī || 96 ||

datvā varāṇāṃtuśataṃdurlabhaṃyatsurairapi |
śarīredivya saurabhyaṃ kanyātvaṃ punarevahi ||97||

yathecchayātathābhuktāsṛṣṭvānīhāramaṃdiram |
vyāsaśvotpāditaḥ putroyogītena mahātmanā || 98 ||

tathāca bhagavānvyāsodevānāṃca hitāyaca |
kṣatriyasyamṛtasyāpināṭa dāreṣusaṃgataḥ || 99 ||

trīnputrānjanayāmāsadhṛtarāṣṭramukhāṃstataḥ |
śukaṃputramaraṇyāṃcanirmathyacāgnināsaha || 100 ||

u tpādayāmāsamuniryogināṃprabhuravyayaḥ |
śukastucaturaḥputrānjanayāmāsayoginaḥ || 1 ||

gauravaṃkapilaṃkṛṣṇaṃtathānīlaṃca tāpasam |
yogācāryāmahātmānastve kākanyāca bhāminī || 2||

kapālamālānaddhāṃgaḥ pūrvaṃvāmaśirāmuniḥ |
nāgalokaṃja gāmāśukhahartukṛtodyamaḥ || 3 ||

sarvavidyādharāṇāṃtuyenasyādrājasattamaḥ |
nāgasyabhavanaṃśoṣyahomajāpyasamādhibhiḥ || 4 ||

khādiraiḥśaṃkubhiḥsūtraiḥ paṃcaraṃgaiḥ śaraistathā |
tanmaṃtraśaktyāpātāledatya mānetupannagaiḥ || 105 ||

|| 56 ||


trayodaśe purādṛttakīrtanātpuṣpadhanvanaḥ |
durjayatvaṃrāgayuktamāsanaiḥ samudīryate || 1 ||

dīyamānebhayātkhaṅgeveśyayāvaṃcitāṃpisaḥ |
aparājitamaṃtraṃtudarśayitvāmanoharam || 6 ||

kiṃkhaṅgenetisaṃcitya ghṛtapūrṇā sucaṃjaha || 7 ||

pūrṇāhutimadatvātuvyutthitaḥkṣubhiteṃdriyaḥ |
nāgadāsīṃtataḥ prāpya cikrīḍacajahāsaca || 8 ||

tatoratatinirdagdhaḥ śatrubhiḥ pannagaiḥ kṣaṇāt |
tuṣṭairvigatasaṃtrāsairnāryāsaṃrakṣitairapi || 9 ||

itiparamam ṛṣīṇāṃmanmathakṣobhitānāṃ caritamidamaśeṣaṃvarṇitaṃvomayāya |
prathamakathitakīrtiryatradagdho mahātmātadanukathitaśiṣṭova ṇitovāmadevaḥ || 110 ||


sūta uvāca ||

kṣatriyogādhiputrastuviśvāmitro mahāmanāḥ |
vasiṣṭhasya munerlakṣmīṃdṛṣṭvā saṃtaptamānasaḥ || 1 ||

dīkṣitobrāhmaṇatvāya trailokyakṣobhaṇāyaca |
saṃśuṣkāṃgastapasvīcadṛṣṭvādevaiśvavaṃcitaḥ || 2 ||

vijanemenakāṃnārīṃtayāsakilamohitaḥ |
jaḍonmattasvarūpobhūtkāmarāgavaśīkṛtaḥ || 3 ||

tathāśuṃbhaniśuṃbhauca durjayaudevadānavaiḥ |
anyonyenahataumohāddṛṣṭvāgopeṃdrakanyakām || 4 ||

tatputrāvasurau ghorautathā suṃdopasuṃdakau |
avadhyau sarvabhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭvānārīṃtilottamām || 5 ||

mameyaṃnatavaiveṣāityuktvāyuddhalālasau |
anyonyenahataumohātkāmarāgavaśānugau || 6 ||

iṃdralokopa maṃrājya maikṣvākaḥprāpyadaṃḍake |
sūryaprabhāṃcaśukrasyavaśayitvāsutāṃpriyāṃ || 7 ||

sarāṣṭraḥ sapurodagdhodaṃḍyaḥ śukreṇadurjayaḥ |
ghoreṇapāṃsuvarṣeṇadinaiḥ saptabhirevaca || 8 ||

adyāpidaṃḍakāraṇyaṃnirvakṣatṛṇasānumat || 9 ||

itikaṭhinamatīnāṃ dhārmikāṇāmṭaṣīṇāṃcaritamihapurāṇevarṇitaṃtemayāya |
sakalanṛpatimukh yoyatragādhiḥ saputrastadanuditijaputrau kīrtitaukīrtimaṃtau || 10 ||

rāvaṇaśrvajana śyāmodaśava krobhayānakaḥ |
viṃśatyācabhujairyuktaścatuppācca jagajjayī || 11 ||

puṣpotkaṭāyāḥ putrastu munervi śravasaḥsutaḥ |
iṃdrajitvācabatdhvācakṣitavāndurgapaṃjare || 12 ||

meghanādasyaśaktyātu śyenaṃśākuni koyathā |
labdhvā trailo kyarājyaṃ tudivyāśvavibudhastriyaḥ || 13 ||

tāruṇyagarvasaubhāgyalāvaṇyaiśvasamanvitāḥ |
mayasyatanayāṃprāpyaṃ bhāryāmaṃdodarīma pi || 14 ||

tathācaparanārīṇāṃ laṃpaṭaḥ kāmamohitaḥ |
manmathetugatenāśaṃprajāpatisutāratiḥ || 15 ||

bhūyomaya syagehetujā tājagatisuṃdarī |
hṛtāsāśaṃbareṇāyatayāsakilavaṃcitaḥ || 16 ||

nārīṃdārumayīṃrutvādivyālaṃkārabhūṣitām |
ātmanaḥ sa dṛśīṃyogānnijīvāṃjīvayanniva || 17 ||

tayāsābrahmavādinyāratyāsaṃpreṣitāsatī |
pāṃcālikātatodaityaṃśaṃbaraṃkāma mohitam || 18||

prakrīḍayāmāsaśanairniśāsudivaseṣuca |
māyāvatīṃtādṛśīṃcatato hartudaśānanaḥ || 19 ||

jagāmavivaraṃbhūme vicārya sumahābalaḥ |

pātāle divyaratnāvyaṃsvargādiṣṭaguṇaṃ mahat |
praviṣṭamātrasta trāsaumayamāyāsamudbhavaiḥ || 21 ||

baddhastuvividhaiḥ pāśaistatrameṣaiśvapīḍitaḥ |
vajñalepapralipte tu mārgamamnastudāruṇe || 22||

cūrṇīkṛte mahāmaṃtrairvarairlabdhairmayenave |
sarvaitatraprahastādyānimagnāvajrakardame || 23 ||

nānāśastrairvibhinnosaudivyairlohama yairnaraiḥ |
daṃḍibhirdvārapālaiśvatathāgirimayairhataḥ || 24 ||

tacchrutvāduḥkhaśokārtātitomaṃdodarībhayāt |
pitaraṃśrāvayāmāsabha rturdoḥśīlyacāpalam || 25||

tatomayena tuṣṭena māyākācidvinirmitā |
tayālepairvinirmuktaḥ sasainyorāvaṇastadā || 26 ||

samāśvasto'thasavrīḍasṭapitastu bubhukṣitaḥ |
uktastayāsā'vahāsaṃsamābhāṣyāthamaṃdadhīḥ || 27 ||

rāvaṇeṃdramukhaṃkiṃtedaśapannagasaṃnibham |
catvāraśvaraṇāstavaipaśukalpapaśoriva || 28 ||

kimidaṃrūpasauṃdaryatavakṛtsnaṃpiśācavat |
kathaṃdevairalabhyāṃmāmapahartutvamāgataḥ || 29 ||

yasyāsti nārīsvagṛhesa kimarthaṃniśācara |
lubdhaḥ syātparanārīṣupāpabuddhiracetanaḥ || 30 ||

kvāpivāparanārīṣukoviśeṣastuvidyate |
paranārīṣvanāyuṣyaṃgamanepāpacetasām || 31 ||

śarīrāvayavānāṃtu yeṣāṃ saṃjāyatespṛhā |
chinnāstaeva saṃtrāsaṃ kurvatirudhirokṣitāḥ || 32 ||

yenehacāyuṣaḥśeṣojīvitasya canānyathā |
rajasācābhibhūtānāṃkhīṇāṃtumanasaḥ priyam ||33||

yaccharīraṃtunārīṇāṃ taruṇīnāṃmanoramaṃ |
tadevadṛṣṭvā kuṇapaṃtrāsātsaṃjāyatemmṛtiḥ || 34||

dardurasyabhavevyāpteviniṣkrāṃtasyakutsitam |
uttānocchūnadehasya tādṛśasya kṛtehikim || 35||

narāḥkurvaṃtipāpaṃtudoṣāṃścaiva tathātmanaḥ |
aṇumātraṃ sukhaṃ puṃsāṃ paradārasamāgame || 36 ||

tadevapariṇāmenaduḥkhaṃparvatasaṃnibham |
ahobatanajānaṃtinarānāryaśvadāruṇaṃ || 37 ||

pariṇāme mahadduḥkhaṃtadātvecasukhaṃ laghu |
tatkartavyaṃprayatnena narairduḥkhaṃ mahātmabhiḥ || 38 ||

paścāttāpona yenasyātriyamāṇasyadehinaḥ |
gacchamāsāhasaṃkārṣīḥ śaṃkitavyāḥ striyaḥ sadā || 39 ||

meghāṃtakāgnipātālavipasarpāsisaṃnibhāḥ |
striyomāyāsahasrāṇijānaṃtijaḍacetanāḥ || 40 ||

guravaḥ kitavānāryaunarāśvapiśitapriyāḥ |
māyopajīvanānityaṃ etāḥ kovaṃcituṃkṣamaḥ || 41 ||

tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvāvivarā niryayautataḥ |
rāvaṇomaṃdabuddhistuvicaranpṛthivītale || 42 ||

kanyā māṃgirasa syāthapunarvedavatīṃtataḥ |
carmacīradharāṃdṛṣṭvā keśavārpitamānasām ||43||

anicchamānāṃrudatīṃ svayaṃgrāhādadharṣayat || 44 ||

tenavidhvaṃsitāsātudadāhasvakalevaram |
rāvaṇasyavadhārthāyasā'bhūdbhāryā suśobhanā || 45 ||

bhūmausamutthitāyajñemithi lādhipaterapi |

ekāeva sapatnībhirvarjitānityalālitā |
dviputriṇītata śvāṃteprāviśanmedinītalam || 47 ||

punaḥ kṣīrodaśayane bhartrāsaha samāgamam |
labheyamitisaṃcityapraviṣṭāmedinītalam ||48||

viṣṇustasyāviyogācca kāmāsaktamanābhṛśam |
mṛtyulokaṃnasāyātitasmādbahupatistvabhūt || 49 ||

tataḥ kadāci kailāserāvaṇastu niśāmukhe |
surāpānenasaṃmūḍhodadarśajalajekṣaṇām || 50 ||

hṛdyāṃrūpavatīṃraṃbhāṃmattaddiradagāminīṃ |
ba lādanicchamānāṃtāṃstuṣāṃmohātparāmṭaśat || 51 ||

tannimittaṃtuśato'saudhanādhyakṣasutenaca |
najāturāvaṇogacchedanyāsa ktāṃtukāminīm || 52 ||

gataḥ pramādāttatkāle bhasmībhūtobhaviṣyati |
tataḥ kadācitpātālaṃgatojetuṃdaśānanaḥ || 53 ||

ka smiṃścittatrabhuvanedadarśādbhutadarśanam |
vyomālikajjalaśyāmaṃ puruṣaṃraktalocanam || 54 ||

kalpāṃtadahanajvālātulyaromā limaṃḍitam |
mahāvidyullatākārajaṭākāravibhūṣitam || 55 ||

divākarasahasrābhaṃkeśaśmaśruvibhūṣaṇam |
nāgeṃdramukhasaṃbhū taviṣānalapaṭāvṛtam || 56 ||

ghoṇāśvāsaistathāghorai yajanānāṃśataṃtataḥ |
āsīnaścatathānītastena kālātmanātadā || 58 ||

kṣaṇānniśceṣṭatāṃnītovaraṃyācitavāṃstataḥ |
tvaddhastādbhagavanmṛtyurmamāstvitipunaḥpunaḥ || 59||

avaśyamititenoktova limocayituṃ gataḥ |
tatrakālaṃvarṣadharaṃ dvibhujaṃ muśalāyudham || 60 ||

viṣaṇṇaḥ śaṃkitodīnaḥ pātālānniryayaukṣaṇāt || 61 ||

paścānmānuṣamātrasya kṣatriyasya nitaṃbinīm |
atharāmasya bhāryāṃtucīravatkaladhāriṇīṃ || 62 ||

piṇyākaśākapānīyamāṃsamūlaphalāśinīṃ |
valmīkadhūlisikatākaṃṭakaiḥ kṛtavikṣatām || 63 ||

bhraṣṭarājyasyadīnasyatyaktasyapitṛmātṛbhiḥ |
atyaṃtabhaktāṃraktāṃcadṛṣṭvākāmavaśaṃyayau || 64 ||

sauvarṇahariṇaṃ kṛtvāmārīcaṃrākṣasādhamaṃ |
vidrumāṃkuracaṃgaṃtuvaiḍūryamaṇilocanam ||65||

śrīmanmarakatavrātasaṃchannapṛṣṭhamaṃḍalam |
pūrṇedusadṛśākāraṃmukkāmālāvibhūṣitam || 66 ||

suśuddhapadmarāgaiśvacatuśrvaraṇaśobhitam |
visarjayitvā rāmasyaparṇaśālāṃśanaiḥśanaiḥ || 67 ||

svayaṃcayatirūpaṃ vaikṛtavānkṛtināṃvaraḥ |
mṭagaṃsāmānuṣīdṛṣṭvāpatilobhādvyasarjayat || 68 ||

tasmingate mṭagaṃhaṃtuṃ vane rāmaśarairmṛgaḥ |
viddhodaśānanaṃ prāhabhośyātarnihato ramyaham || 69 ||

tacchrutvāśbhrātarityevaṃ vākyaṃ sītāvyasarjayat |
bharturanveṣaṇārthāyadevaraṃvijanesthitā || 70 ||

rāvaṇenahṛtā: sītā mohaṃnītākṣaṇādiva |
tāṃprārthya mānorāmeṇanihatorākṣasaiḥ sahaḥ || 71 ||

dakṣiṇaṃjaladhiṃtītva kā~ḍai ghorāgnisaṃnibhaiḥ |
tatolabdhajayastāṃtulabdhvāsvaviṣayaṃyayau || 72 ||

sūryavaṃśasamudbhūtorāmodāśarathirhariḥ |
nānāmāṃsāni madyaṃcapītvābhuktvācarākṣasaḥ || 73 ||

mattaḥ sumodāritāsyaḥ kuṃbhakarṇobhayānakaḥ |
kalpāṃtadahanajvālālolajjihaḥ purāgṛhe || 74 ||

tataḥ saṃgrāmasamayerāvaṇā yaiḥ prabodhitaḥ |
tato rathairmahānādairaśvaiḥ kāṣṭhāsimudgaraiḥ ||75||

jalāgrigirivṛkṣaiśvatāḍyamānopirākṣasaiḥ |
nabubodhayatastatra tadāstrībhiḥprabodhitaḥ || 76 ||

ramyaiḥ payodharottānairurobhiḥpīḍitaḥsaca || 77 ||

itidaśamukhanāmnorāvaṇasyāpiṭattaṃvara girigurudhāmnodbhūtasatvasyapūrvam |
virutabhujagadaṃṣṭrātulyakhaṅgogradhārāvara mukhavarapadmotlattanāśaṃgatasya || 78 ||

madana viṣamabāṇāsāranirbhinnabuddhermanujanṛpatidārasparśasaṃlobhitasya |
varanaraśatamāṃsaiḥ śoṇitaiśvāpi samyakkṣubhitajaṭhara vanheḥkuṃbhakarṇasyacāpi ||79||


sūta uvāca||

mānuṣeṇaśarīreṇa svargādāyātiyātica || 1 ||

avatāryatilānsvargātṛpitṛ ṇāṃtṛmayetadā |

kaikeyyāstu sadoṣeṇarājyaṃprāṇān sutān jahau |
rāmodāśara thirvīroyuddhaśauṃḍomahāmuniḥ || 3 ||

anyenayuddhyamānaṃtubhūmidārahareṇatu || 5 ||

|| 5 ||

oṃnamaḥ śivāya śivārcāyāḥ paṃcadaśenityanaimittikovidhiḥ |
śraddhāpraśaṃsanamukhovistareṇaprakīrtyate || 1 ||

itthaṃ sugrīveṇaviruddhenaśbhrātrāparamaśatruṇā |

raghupatikulacaṃdra vṛttametadviruddhaṃ daśaratha itināgnistrīvilāsābhilāṣāt |
sakalavibudhamukhyānaṃta kīrteśvavi ṣṇostribhuvanabhayabhettustatsutasyāpivṛttam ||8||

ajitaitividitvāliṃgasaṃsthaḥ smarāriḥ svamabhimatamaśeṣaṃdarśaya~llokahetoḥ || 9 ||

ṛṣayarḍacuḥ |
sūtaputrapurāsmābhiḥ sarvadharmāśvaśāśvatāḥ śrutābahuṣuśāstreṣuyathoddiṣṭāmanīṣibhiḥ || 1 ||

naśrutāścaiśvarādharmāihāmutraphalapradāḥ |
tvayedānīṃyataḥśaṃbhoḥ kīrtitaṃguṇa kīrtanaṃ || 2 ||

dharmābahuvidhāḥ proktāyedevanaśru tāstvayā |

jagaddhitāyanṛpa tirātmanaśvavibhūtaye |
naraṃparopakārāyaśubhadharmeniyojayet || 48 ||

tanniyogādayaṃ lokaḥsamācaratibhaktitaḥ || 49 ||

dharmaniṣṭhaḥ kṛtorājātatprāmāṇyādbhayenavā |

tasmādadharmemajjaṃtamubhayārthecapaṃḍitaḥ |

prajāpīḍanasaṃtāpātsamudbhūtoḍatā śanaḥ |

napatibodhayettasmātprajāḥ syurdharmatatparāḥ |

maṃtrauṣadhinidhikṣetravīja mūlarasairddhanaṃ |


jagadguruḥ savijñeyorājānaṃ yonibodhayet |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena sarvalokānukaṃpayā ||60||

śivaṃjñātvātmatatvenayaḥ samuddharatejanam |

vākyaṃ munivaraiḥśāṃtairbhāṣitaṃyatsuśobha naṃ |

vihāyaśāstrajālānijñātvāsvaṃ jīvitaṃ laghu || 71 ||

paṃḍitenāpikiṃtena susamarthenadehinā || 72 ||

surabhiḥsaṃsthitābhūmausrānā śaṃkarasyaca |

gomūtrādguggulurjātodevatānāṃsutṛptaye || 85 ||

evaṃpūjyadadetpaśvāddhṛtayuktaṃ suguggulum |

nivedayecchi vāyaivasubhakṣyaṃ ghṛtapācitam |

bhuktvābhogān suvipulānpṛthivyāmekarāṃ bhavet || 99||

yeṣāṃpatrāṇipuṣpāṇiliṃgasyaivārcanesadā |

ityuktaḥsatubaśyāmajaṃbukomedinītalam||20 kāvitastāvetravatyostusaṃgamelokaviśrute || śāpāṃtobhavitātatrapūrvoktogirikanyayā || 21 ||

kaṃvinayānvitaḥ |


yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃvipragṛtyavṛttitvanuttamām |

evaṃpravartitetatrapuṇyepustakavācane || 30 ||

ṣaṇmāsegatamātretukāle munivarottama |





yathā me syānmahātrītirnaśrāddhaiḥ sarvakāmikaiḥ |
kanyāśrāddhemahātrītirmamasyānmunisattama || 36 ||

athakiṃbahunoktenanānyatprītikaraṃmama || 37 ||

puṇyavyākhyānatovipragutyametatprakīrtitam |

ayamāsīnnaraśreṣṭha asminnevapurottame |



pātradānaphalādeṣaprāptavānbrahmaṇaḥpadam |
etatkarmaphalaṃcāsyanacānyatkṛtavānayam || 42 ||

phalaṃhikarmaṇastasya yanmayāpūjitaḥsvayam || 43 ||

vācakaṃpūjayedyastuśraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ |

vācakaṃpūjayedyastubhakṣyabhojyairanuttamaiḥ |

vācake satkṛte cehabhojitemunisattama |
tṛptirbhavatimeviprasaṃvatsaraśatadvayam || 46 ||

nakevalaṃmamaprītirvācakebhojitebhavet |


matsādhanāyapūjyaṃtetataḥ saṃpūjyatāṃgatāḥ || 49 ||

snānaṃpūjājapohomaḥ śrutaṃśaucamahiṃsatā |
vratopavāsadhairyacamauna maṃdriyanigraham || 50 ||

dhībiṃdyāsatyamakrodhodānaṃkṣāṃtirdayādamaḥ |

kṛcchracāṃdrāyaṇaṃyajñāstī rthānisvāśramāṇica |

dharmādarthaśrvakāmaśvamokṣaśvamunisattama |
ya tsukhaṃviyate kiṃcinmattaśvehaparatratat || 53 ||

duḥkhaṃpāpādvijānīhitasmāddharmamayaṃ sukhaṃ |
trividhaṃtaṃvijānīhi satvarājasa tāmasam ||64||

sadāsatvaṃtapovidyānmunisiddheṣunityaśaḥ |
rājasaṃdivideveṣudanudaityeṣutāmasam || 55 ||

ityetatkathitaṃ karmaśuddhamābhyāṃ mahāmune |
nacānyaccakratuḥ kiṃcitsatyaṃtekathitaṃmayā || 56 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

kimatratathyaṃdeveśakathyatāṃkautukaṃ mama || 57 ||

tāvubhausādhu puṇyosināstitulyastathāparaḥ || 58 ||

yaduktaṃ dharmarājena sarvapratyakṣadarśinā ||59||

sūryeṇāpiyaduktaṃ hitathyaṃ vainānyathābhavet |
yadāsīnme mukhaṃ putra paṃcamaṃlokapūjitam ||60||


natathācaturove dānacāṃgānimahāmate ||62||

dadātuvāca


itihāsapurāṇānispaṣṭīkaraṇamuttamaṃ |

bibhetyalpaśrutāddedomāmayaṃprahariṣyati || 65 ||


yastukārayatetatradharmaśravaṇamuttamaṃ |

nāścaryapātradānāddhidānapātraṃ hitatparaṃ || 68 ||


gaṃgāca sarva saritāṃdevaśreṣṭhoyathāśivaḥ |
pātrāṇāmapisarveṣāṃvācakonātra saṃśayaḥ || 70 ||

vācakaṃpūjayedyastunarobhaktipuraḥsaraḥ |

sanatkumāra uvāca||

itthaṃdinedineyastu devadevasya maṃdire || kārayeddharmaśravaṇaṃsayātiparamaṃ padaṃ || 73 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

śastānighoradānānimahādānāninityaśaḥ |
pātrebhyastupradeyāni ātmānaṃtārayaṃtite || 1 ||

gṛhṇatau tau pavitrāṇitārayaṃtisvamevatam ||2||


hetutulyabaleśastetyadhikāca sarasvatī |
nityamanaḍuhogāva śchatraṃvastramupānahau || 4 ||

saṃkalpavihitoyortho brāhmaṇebhyaḥpradīyate || 5||

arthibhyaḥpīḍitebhyo himanasvītenajāyate |

mahāghorāṇivakṣyāmitrāhmaṇyamupahanyate || 7 ||

pātrāṇiśṭaṇukāleyattyājyānyasmā dvināpadaṃ |
mṛtaśayyājinaṃkṛṣṇaṃ pāpādvikrayamātmanaḥ || 8||

cāṃḍālātpatitānmlecchāttathācobhayatomukhī |
tulāyāṃroha ṇaṃdānaṃkurukṣetrapratigrahaḥ || 9 ||

tilairāpūraṇaṃ kṛṣṇairdānānyevapṛthagdaśa |

sayātinarakaṃ ghoraṃ pūrvajanmanitasyahi |
dātāpaṃkeyathākṣipyapādamaśmanitattathā || 11 ||

dātupratigrahītuśvamayaitatkathitaṃpunaḥ || 12 ||

pravakṣyāmigaticaiṣāṃ pralobhāthṛtacetasāṃ |
saṃprāpyasumahādānaṃyadī cchedgatimātmanaḥ ||13||

manīṣīsakalaṃyacca viprebhyaḥ saṃprayacchati |
rutvārddhena tu kāryāṇivadānyaḥ kurutesadā || 14||

evaṃsanvipramukhyopigatiṃśreṣṭhāṃ prayātihi |
kanakaṃcatilānāgāḥ kanyādāsīgṛhaṃrathaḥ || 15 ||

maṇayaḥkapilācaiva mahādānā nivedaśa |
gṛhītvātānisarvāṇibrāhmaṇojñānavitsadā || 16 ||

vadānyaṃtārayeccaiva ātmānaṃcanasaṃśayaḥ |

etatsavitraṃ paramametatsvastyayanaṃ mahat |

suvarṇayeprayacchaṃtinarāḥ śuddhenacetasā || 19 ||

devatāsteprayacchaṃtisamaṃtāditinaḥ śrutaṃ |

vandyabhāve ca kurvaṃtivahnisthānaṃ cakāṃcanaṃ ||21||

aṣūtaṃyadavācyaṃcaduṣṭaṃhipūtatāṃvrajet || 22||

piḍhadevamanuṣyāṇā miṣṭaṃ tatsarvadāmune |
adbhiḥsaṃśutdhyate sarvamāpaḥśuddhivajaṃtihi || 23 ||

hemnātasmātsadāśuddhaṃhāṭakaṃvanhisaṃbhavaṃ |
yastvenaṃ jvālayitvāgnimādityodayanaṃprati || 24 ||

svarṇadaḥ sarvadaḥ proktaḥ sarvānkāmā~ labheddivi || 25 ||

apsarogaṇakīrṇena gaṃdharvagatinādinā || 26 ||

haṃsavāhana yuktena kāmagenanarottamaḥ |
divyagaṃdhavahaḥ svargepariyātiyatastataḥ ||27||

natya taḥ paramaṃlokesadāpāpavimocanaṃ || 28 ||

suvarṇaye prayacchaṃtinarāḥ śuddhena cetasā |
bahunyabdasahasrāṇi modatedividevavat ||29||

vyāsauvāca||

bahuprakārāṇimayāśrutāni ahotvayoktānityānitāni |
śubhāni sarvāṇiviśeṣitānipra brūhidānaṃtvamatodharāyāḥ || 30 ||

yaddīyatedānamihāpisvalpaṃ svalpena dānenadharādhipatvaṃ |
khyātaṃtvayāpūrvatarairnarādhipairdānā dhipaiḥ sarvabhujobabhūvuḥ || 31||

tadevadānaṃ bhagavaṃ stvidānīṃsaṃpatkaraṃ sarvamathonarāṇām || 32 ||

sana kumārauvāca ||

sarvāṇidānānibhavaṃtidātuḥsamyakpradattānimunehisamyak |
tattepravakṣyāmikṛtaṃhiyenadānottamaṃ tattvadhu nāśṭaṇuṣva || 33||

rājācitrarathonāmapurāsīnnṛpasattamaḥ || 34 ||

bubhujesaka lānbhogānsanadvīpavatīṃmahīm |
samārutyagataḥ sopisuratha stridaśālayam || 35 ||

śāsanaṃkārayāmāsasvakīyaṃtatratatraha || 36 ||

evaṃ pālayataḥ samyaktrailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
suvismayamabhūttatradṛṣṭvārā jyaṃsukhaṃsvakam ||37||

putrāṇāṃṣaṭsahasrāṇikośaṃcākṣayamevaca |
janānāmanurāgaṃcaprabhūtavaravāhanam ||38||

sphītirja napar̤hānāṃcanākālamaraṇaṃtathā |
bhāryāyāścaiva saubhāgyaṃrūpaṃ cāpratimaṃtathā || 39 ||

etatsaṃcitayitvātha kathamapyanyajanma ni |
punaḥsyādihasaṃprāptiḥpūrvadharmādahaṃmunīn || 40 ||

pṛcchāmisarvadharmajñān kariṣyesakalaṃpunaḥ |

rūpaṃcāpratimaṃ lokaṃ bhāryāmestisuśobhanā || 42 ||

śarīrārogyamaiśvaryadānaśaktiranuttamā |
striyonnapānasāmarthyahāniḥ syānnaiva mekvacit || 43 ||

balaṃnāgasahasrasyadhārayā myarisūdanam |

dharmahāniśvamenāstiśaktirmepālanebhuvaḥ |
yadyadicchā myahaṃ kartuṃ tatkaromi mahāmune ||45||

sarvaṃ pūrva kṛtāddharmādevaṃprāptaṃmayākhilam |
etanme sarvamācakṣva pūrva janma kṛtaṃ phalam ||46||

citayitvāciraṃ kālaṃśṭaṇubhūpānyajanmani || 47 ||

yatkṛtaṃtepravakṣyāmikuyonima nuvartate |
avaṃtīnagarīnāmapṛthivyāja ghanesthitā || 48 ||

dharmapālonṛpastatraṃ sarvadharmānuśāsakaḥ |
varṇavātyastvama pyāsītsvadharmamanuvartakaḥ ||49||

vasatastetvanādṛṣṭirāsīcca bahuvārṣikī |
annakṣayāttatastvaṃtu gatvātuvanamāśrayat ||50||

tatratevasatolokecahavaḥ samupāśrayāt |
kṣutkṣāmakarṣitāḥsaṃtaḥ phalamūlamahāśinaḥ || 51 ||

nirannecatatoloketasminphala vivarjite |
kṣudhāta bhāryayāyuktaḥ prāgādaṃgārakārakaḥ || 52 ||

tasmāttvaṃ bhāryayāyuktodārūṇyādāyasatvaraḥ |
prāviśannaga rīṃsopivikretutānisarvathā || 53 ||

najagrāhajanaḥ kaśvidaṃpatyoraṭamānayoḥ |
tataḥsāyaṃkṣudhārtautaudhvaniṃśuśruvatustadā ||54||

vaṇigmukhyasyaviprāṇāṃjavhatāṃtagṛhāṃgaṇe |
taugatvātatra kāṣṭhānijvālayāmāsatustadā || 55 ||

pratāpārthaitumā ghasyapūrṇimāyāṃ samāgame |
rāvhanayormahābhāgatatratāvūṣatustadā || 56 ||

palānāṃśatame kasyasamupoṣyajanārdanaṃ |
arca yitvāvidhānenavaṇiksopikṛtādaraḥ || 57 ||

pṛthivīṃsamarpayāmāsa hai maviprebhyaeva hi |

sādṛṣṭādvīyamānā vai yuvayoḥ kliśyamānayoḥ |
taṃdṛṣṭvāduḥkhasaṃta maunakṛtaṃpuṇyamāvayoḥ || 59 ||

yenedṛśau bhaviṣyāvodharaṃtaumanasātviti |
daṃpatyoryuvayorājaṃstene yaṃvṛddhiruttamā || 60 ||

prāptādharma phalādrājyaṃ tasmāttvaṃ dehisāṃprataṃ |
yenatvamakṣayā~llokānprāpnopidevadurlabhān || 61 ||

etatte kathitaṃ samyakyathā vṛttamabhūtpurā |
tasmāttvaṃ dehirājeṃdrapṛthivīṃ sarvakāmadāṃ || 62 ||

yenācyutiṃsamāpnotisvargānnṛpatisattama |
dīyamānāṃtrapa śyaṃtipṛthvīṃrukmasyabhaktitaḥ ||63||

śṛṇubhūpālabhardratemana sāyedharāpradāḥ ||64||

sābdhidvīpavatīṃ dṛṣṭvā teṣāṃ vākyaṃ tathātathā |

bubhuje pārthivaṃ kṣetraṃ sadevāsura rakṣasāṃ |
gāyaṃti munayoyasya kīrtiyasyatubhūtale || 66 ||

taddattaṃcopajīvaṃtita thānyebhūbhṛtopica ||67||

yāvatsūryaudetismayāvacca pratitiṣṭhati |
sarvacaivapṛthoḥ kṣetraṃ trailokyāṃtapravarttakaṃ || 68 ||

śaśāsadharmeṇapunarnādharmodṛśyatekutaḥ || 69 ||

nānyāyakṛnnavāśuddhonāstenomūṣakādayaḥ |
dhanuṣkoṭyārutāyenaniradriḥ pṛthivīpurā || 70 ||

tasyaitadabhimānaṃcavīryacapṛthivīpateḥ |
rūpaṃdṛṣṭvāśubhāpatnītasyābhūccā tivismayā ||71||

tataḥsācitayāmāsasammṛjyāvismitāsatī |
kathaṃsyātsaṃpadeṣāme kiṃpunaraṃnyajanmani || 72 ||

evaṃsāva hudhāciṃtyapṛthuṃcaivasamānudat |
aniścayaparorājāvainyastuvismayānvitaḥ ||73||

tataḥ papracchasarva jñānbrāhmaṇānādibhūmipaḥ |
praṇipatya mahārājovacanaṃ cedamabravīt || 74 ||

tadahaṃ praṣṭumicchāmikiṃcittadvaktumarhatha || 75 ||

vaktumarhathasarvajñāḥ sarvese rvopakāriṇaḥ || 76 ||

munaya ūcuḥ||

yastaṃmanasi saṃdehoyaśvasāṃśayiko bhuvi |
gadiṣyāmoyathānyāyaṃtaṃ pṛcchādya mahīpate||77৷ vayaṃ hinṛpaśārdūlabhavatāparitopitāḥ|| samyakprajāḥpālayatāsarvadvīpeṣupārthiva ||78||

suropivapupātejona kaścitsahate dhunā |
sohamicchāmi taṃjñātuṃyathāceyamaniṃditā || 84 ||

atibhāticamekāṃtyābhāryeyamatiśobhanā |
