satamāhata tobhūpaṃciṃtitārthoyatavrataḥ || 88 ||

marīciruvāca ||

bhāryayāsahitaṃprāpnamataeka manābhava || 89 ||


yadi tvaccittānugatānityaṃtavaśuśrūṣaṇeratā |

tyajyamānāpisāsādhvīnotyaje ttvāmaniṃditā |

nītāhemamayīpṛthvīdhaninovṛṣalasyatu |

paricaryākṛtādāturmanasāpuṇyakāṃkṣiṇaḥ |
saṃmārjanādikaṃ sarva kṛtaṃte bhaktitonṛpa || 94 ||

sarvakāmapradaṃ karma rukma dhenvāḥ kṛtaṃtvayā || 95 ||

tenedamakhilaṃrājyamaśe ṣājagatītava |

tanmayatvena saṃprāptaṃ mahīmānamanuttamam |
kiṃpunaryonarobhaktyā pṛthvīṃhaimīṃprayacchati || 97||

śataṃ pūrvāparaṃ cāpi kulānāṃtārayennṛpa |

nasva rgācyavate tāvadvimuktaḥ sarvapātakaiḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣaṃcayadicchettattadāpnuyāt ||99||

ityetatkathitaṃrājansarvabhūmipradobha va |
yadicchenmanasā sarvatattadāpnotyasaṃśayam || 100 ||

dvijaṃhitvāpiyodadyānmucyate sarvapātakaiḥ |
ityetatkathitaṃ rājanpṛ thivīdānamuttamam || 1 ||

manasācitayedyastu sasvargamadhigacchati |

vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥsvargalokaṃcagacchati || 3 ||


vyāsauvāca ||

yādṛśīṃkārayāmāsayatpramāṇāṃyadudbhavām || 1 ||

paṃcaviśehemamayyāḥ sābdhidvīpayujobhuvaḥ |
svarupadānamāhātmyaṃyathāvadupavarṇyate || 1 ||

yasminkāledadatsopitadrūhi tvamaśeṣataḥ |
sanatkumārauvāca || kuryātsvaśaktitovyāsauttamādhamamadhyamāḥ || 2 ||

saṃtī ha mānavālokeśaktyādāturlabhaṃtite |
lakṣaṇasuvarṇena sahasreṇaśatenatu || 3 ||

paṃcāśatāvāniḥsvopi suvarṇenatukārayet |

hemābhāve sutāmrasya kṛtvā hai maṃtuniḥ kṣipet |
etanmātreṣuśaileṣusarvataḥ praki reddasu || 5||

ratnābhāvādaṇuṣveva haimaṃ teṣuvinikṣipet |
tanmadhye cotthitomerumaṃdaraṃtasyapūrvataḥ || 6 ||

gaṃdhamādanasaṃjñaṃtumero ścaivatudakṣiṇe |
nīlaṃtupaścime bhāgevipulaśvottaretataḥ ||7||

merārddhabahuratnāninyastāvaiḍūryamaṃdire |
dakṣiṇevidrumaṃcaiva mahā nīlaṃtu paśvime ||8||

mūrdhnisaṃvinyasettatra padmarāgaṃtathottare |
tadbhāvāddhiraṇyaṃtu mauktikāditataḥ paraṃ || 9||

kadaṃba jaṃbukāśvattha śrīpaṇapallavaṃ kramāt |
nāgavayāstathābhāvātsamyakpatrāṇivinyaset || 10 ||

lavaṇasyasamudratutaddātye parikalpayet |
lavaṇenapavitreṇabātyaṃtatparipūrayet || 11 ||

tadvā lakṣadvīpaṃtu abdhirikṣurasasyavai |
tenāpūryatatodvīpaḥ śālmaliśvāparo mune || 12||

sarpiṣastusamudravai pūrayetsarpiṣācataṃ |
kuśadvīpātparodanastenaivāpūrayettataḥ || 13 ||

krauṃcātparastudugdhenaśākadvī pamataḥparaṃ |
tasmāttaṃmadhunāpūryatadaṃtepuṣkaraṃsthitaṃ || 14||

saṃkṣepātkathitaṃvyāsa pṛthivyādānamuttamaṃ |

hasta mātraṃkhanetkuṃḍaṃ homaṃtatraiva kārayet |
yavasiddhārthakairyuktastilairvyāhṛtibhistathā || 17 ||

dikpālaistu grahaiścaiva svaisvairmatraiḥ savaidikaiḥ |
viṣṇostutritayaṃ hutvā āpodevetyanukramāt || 18 ||

vasoḥ pavitraṃtritayaṃ hutvānnāvā hutīstataḥ |
pṛthivītipuna rhutvāpūjayettatrakeśavaṃ || 19 ||

maṃtrairebhirdharāṃtadvanmaṃtreṇānenavaimune || 20 ||

dharādharādhāradharābalāyya sarvāśrayastvaṃhipumānaśeṣaḥ |
devaiḥ supūjyosisupūjitome saṃpūjyamānobhimataṃdadasva ||21||

sṛṣṭātvayeyaṃ pṛthivīdhṛtāpunardātātvamasyāḥ pratisaṃgrahītā |
tubhyaṃmayāhemamayīniveditāgṛhṇīṣva kāmābhimanaṃdadasva ||22||

evaṃpūjyavidhānena vastrairābharaṇaistathā |
brāhmaṇānvācayitvāgreprīyatāṃ dharaṇīdharaḥ || 23 ||

evaṃdatvādvijebhyastāṃśṭaṇukālecayatphalaṃ |

tatosāve kacchacaṃturājyaṃ prāpnotibhūtale || 25 ||

tatrasthasyaivasābuddhirvartate dharmaevahi |
kramānmokṣamavāpnotidānādasyāḥprabhāvataḥ || 26 ||


vyāsauvāca ||

brahmahatyāgurorasti kiṃnaśyatitapovinā |
saṃśayaḥ sarvatatvajñodānāttvaṃbūhi pṛcchataḥ || 1 ||

yattaduktaṃ purātenata ddānaṃbūtyaśeṣataḥ |
yenadānenasadyonāpāpādbhavatyakilbiṣī || 2||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

yatsādhayennirjadehaṃ kṛtvā pāpānyaśeṣa taḥ |
mucyatetaddhikālena etate kathitaṃsphuṭaṃ || 3 ||

jīvaṃ hi daihikāḥ prāṇāmadhye vātyesadāvasu |
dehasaṃpīḍayātābhyāṃnaraḥ pāpāya mucyate ||4||

tasmātpṛcchaṛṣiśreṣṭhasāṃprataṃkathayāmite |
vyāsauvāca || asminkaliyugemartyāḥ sadācāravivarjitāḥ ||5||

bahupāparatāmūḍhābhaviṣyaṃtimahāmune |
rauravādipughorāṇāṃyātanāḥ kila śuśruma || 6 ||

kāṃtāraṃ gahanaṃtatra tadarthaṃ brūhimedvija |
yathānapaśyetkāṃtāraṃ suduṣpāraṃ sudurgamaṃ || 7||

dānenatapasāvāpinarastanme vadaprabho |
sanatkumāra uvāca || śṛṇu rājanmahāgu tyaṃmahāpātakanāśanaṃ || 8 ||


gaṃdha puṣpaiḥ sunaiveyairarcayedacyutaṃbudhaḥ |
tilaprasthamayaṃ kṛtvānāgaṃ sarvaguṇānvitaṃ || 10 ||

dhenuṃvatsaṃsuvarṇatusudīpaiḥ sarvataścitaṃ |
muktāphalekṣaṇaropyadaśanaṃvidrumaccchadaṃ || 11 ||

kāṃsyaghaṃṭāyugayutaṃ kṣaumakakṣāvibhūṣitaṃ || 12 ||

mauktikāpūrṇakuṃbhaṃtusiṃdūrāvatama stakaṃ |
vicitravastrasaṃvītaṃ muktāmālāvirājitaṃ || 13||


tasyāgreṣṭadalaṃpadmaṃ saṃlikhetkuṃkumārutaiḥ |
āpūryācyutamīśānamarcayetkaṇikāpade || 15 ||

dhātāraṃviśvakartāraṃ pūrvapatretuvāsavam |
namostudevapatayeparjanyāyamarutvate || 16 ||

namognaye hutāśāyahavyavāhāyavanha ye |
namaḥ pitṛṇāṃpatayedharmāyānaṃdadāyine || 17 ||

namonirṛtināthāyadharmāya nirayātmane |
namopāṃpatayetubhyaṃ bhavabhītā tihāriṇe ||18||

namastepṛṣadaśvāyavāyave jagadāyuṣe |
namorikthādhipatayesmerasmayamadāyate || 19 ||

kāṃtārapatayedhātretrahmarudrāyaviṣṇave || 20 ||

evaṃ kalpavi dhānenatatra kāṃtāradevatāḥ || 21||

devadevaṃ tatobhyarcyaviṣṇuṃviśvapatiṃpunaḥ |

kā~ tāramadhvanomārgamibhenānena keśavaḥ |
sukhaṃva jāmitaṃdurgatathātvaṃ kurume prabho || 23 ||

yathābhadraghaṭonityaṃ kāmadhenurmahādru maḥ |
ciṃtāmaṇistathādaṃtī sadābhavatu mevibho || 24 ||


tatonaktaṃsamaśnīyāddīpaṃdadyāttathāgaje |
pārthiverājaśārdūla kārtikyāṃvāpradāpayet || 26 ||

evaṃyaḥ ku rutesamyakvidhānenaivaśaktitaḥ |
yamamārgabhayaṃghoraṃnarakaṃcanapaśyati || 27 ||

hatvāpāpānyaśeṣāṇisabaṃdhuḥsamutsṛjjanaḥ |

kāṃtārasyapradānena samaṃsyānmunisattama || 29||

yaccobhayamukhīnāṃcaśatenalabhate phalam |
kāṃtārasyapradānenata tphalaṃnātra saṃśayaḥ ||30||

dharādānānnatatpuṇyaṃkāṃtāreṇa yadaśnute || 31 ||

kṛṣṇājine nayatpuṇyaṃ bhūyaḥ śataguṇenaca |
puṇyaṃ tatsakalaṃlabhyaṃ mune kāṃtārahastinā || 32 ||

gajāśvarathadhenūnāṃ yatphalaṃ hinigadyate |
tatpha laṃsakalaṃvidyātkāṃtāreṇa mahāmune || 33 ||

tilājyajaladhenūnāṃyatpūrvoktaṃphalaṃlabhet |
tanmūrtitanmayīṃda tvāpuṇyaṃśataguṇaṃla bhet || 34 ||

evaṃjñātvāmuniśreṣṭhaśreṣṭhakāṃtārakāraṇam |
dadyādanenamaṃtreṇa saṃpūjyagajapuṃgavam || 35||

namaḥkāṃtārarūpāyapa rameśāyaśaṃbhave |
namoṣṭadigibheśāyanamorūpāyaviplave || 36 ||

anenatilakāṃtāradaṃtidānena daityahā |
prīyatāṃpuṃḍarīkākṣaḥ prabhuḥ kāṃtāratārakaḥ || 37 ||

etatpāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃmukhasvargakaraṃ param ||38||

kariṣyāmītyahaṃ bhaktyā svargalokaṃsagacchati || 39 ||

vyāsauvāca ||

pūrvoktaṃtedinaikena pūjitaḥsamaheśvaraḥ |
phalaṃdadātivipula metatsarvaṃ mahāmune || 1 ||

gurūṇāṃnabhavetkucavacanaṃmukhaniḥsṛtam |

sanatkumāra uvāca ||

nāsatya metaduktaṃmeśṭaṇuvākyaṃ mahāmune |
niyamenārcitaḥ śaṃbhurdinaikenaprasīdati || 3 ||

krīḍādine caturdaśyām |
vṛthāśramohibahubhiḥkleśairnaivāśnute phalam ||4||

yathācaratnavikretuḥsusūkṣmāda bhutemune |
vyāsauvāca || kathayasvasamāsena tadvataṃmemahāmune ||5||

lokānāṃ hitakāmena kṛtaṃyeneha tadvada |
sanatkumārauvāca || āsītpūrvayugerājājaṃbūddīpe sudhārmikaḥ || 6||

sarājāstravidāṃ śreṣṭhovedaśāstrā thaṃpāragaḥ || 7||

dharmiṣṭhaḥ satyavākśūrodātābhoktātithipriyaḥ |
nityadātādvijebhyosauhemavastrādiśobhanam || 8 ||

yadiṣṭa mānonityaṃtattattacchraddhāsamanvitam |
dadātyanudinaṃvyāsateṣāmarthastriyādikam || 9 ||

evaṃrājyaṃśurutvādī rghakālaṃmahī patiḥ |
śatānīkastatonāmnāsahasrānīkamevaca || 10 ||

sutaṃjajñetataḥ kālātsitīśosaumṭatastu saḥ |
kulakamāgataṃ rājyaṃca kresaunṛpasattamaḥ ||11||

nyāyatodharmataścaivarājyeśāstivasuṃdharām |
kāṃtyācaṃdramasaścaiva pratāpādityasaṃnibhaḥ || 12 ||

brāhmaṇebhyodadātīśoyathātasyapitānasaḥ || 13 ||

pratigrahamalapsaṃtaḥ samājagmustadaṃtikam || 14 ||

pūjitāste susaṃdṛṣṭāḥprocurnṛpatisattamam || 15 ||

dvijāūcuḥ||


yekecidbrāhmaṇāḥ saṃtiyathākāmaṃyathāsukham |
saṃvardhitāḥ svakedhāgnisamabuddhirayaṃnṛpaḥ || 17 ||

sāṃprataṃtavarājyetusaṃprekṣyacagatādvijāḥ |
vasāmeotraparikhinnānnṛpatesakuṭuṃbinaḥ ||18||

itaḥ kiṃ kurmaevaṃ hiyāsyāmaḥ kutrapīḍitāḥ |
daridreṇaivanobuddhiryathāśṭaṇunṛpottama || 19 ||

etacchrutvāsa tānviprānprovācapra hasanniva |
rājovāca || satyametadvijebhyopidattaṃdānaṃ mahāphalam || 20 ||

paratranyāyatoviprāḥ kiṃcidvakṣyāmi kāraṇam |
dānātsvargamavāpnotiihāyātaḥ sukhībhavet || 21 ||

pitāmamadvijaśreṣṭhākathaṃ svargeva satyasau |
jñātvaivaitatsamāsenakathayasva yathātathaṃ ||22||

etacchrutvātatoviprāvajrapātopasaṃvacaḥ |
samuttasthuḥ samājñāpyarājānaṃdīnamānasāḥ || 23||

samavāyīkṛtā stetutathānyonyamabhāṣata |
rājāpaṃcatvamāpannaḥkvāsautiṣṭhatidhārmikaḥ || 24||

evaṃvi bruvatāṃtepadināni subahṛnica || 25 ||

gatānijuvhatāṃvyāsaravirvyākulatāṃgataḥ |
rājñāṃdharmavatāṃcāpiprajāḥ pālayatāṃsa tām || 26 ||

navarṣajāyatetasmātsubhikṣaṃ kṣemamevahi |
agnaunyastā hutiḥ samyagādityāyopagacchati ||27||

annātsaṃjāyatesarvaṃ jagattṛptistusarvadā || 28 ||

hi tāyateṣāṃ saṃdeṣṭuṃ dvijo bhūtvātutāntravīt || 29 ||

kiṃviṣaṇṇādvijāyūyamihepsataḥ svakarmatat |
naityakaṃpaṃcayajñottha manaṃtaphala 'dāyakam || 30 ||

naityakaṃkarmakurvāṇoyonyatphalaṃ samīhate |
tattatsaṃpretya dadatastatctyaktvānyannatadbhavet || 31 ||

bahūniśubhatī rthāni phaladānīhasarvataḥ |
śrūryatena phalaṃdātuṃ svakarmaphalahātinaḥ || 32 ||

pañcayajñaphalaṃnityaṃcaturāśramavāsināṃ |
bhuktimuktikaraṃviprāstasmāttannaparityajet || 33 ||

kurvannityaṃtunaimittaṃkāmikaṃ casamāśrayet |
nityakārītunaimittālabhetpuṇyaṃśatottaraṃ ||34||

sahasraguṇitaṃ kāmyāttathāpnotipumānsadā |
pratigrahavihī 'naṃ tusadājapyaparodvijaḥ || 35 ||

yastaṃ mṛtaṃ samākhyāsye ttatpurādāgatonnṛpaṃ |
etacchrutvāvrataṃtasyapraṇipatyacataṃdvijāḥ ||36||

tatastenveṣayāmāsurharṣāddtvācaturdiśaṃ |
tayāvidhaṃnapaśyaṃtaḥ pattaneṣupureṣuca || 3 ||

gatvāpaśyaṃstapasyataṃ bhārgavaṃdvijasattamaṃ |
ūrdhvabāhuṃnirālaṃbaṃpādenaikena saṃsthitam || 38 ||

taṃdṛṣṭāpraṇipatyāśuparivāryadvijottamam |
pratyagvisaṃsthite sūryetatosau dvijasattamaḥ ||39||

naityakaṃkarmakṛtvāpi samuttasthau satāndvijān || 40 ||

upaviṣṭāntsamadrākṣītkṛtāṃjaliḥsapūrvavat |
pūjayāmāsatānsarvānpādyādyairvidhipūrvakam || 41 ||

paryapṛcchayathānyāyaṃ phalamūlaiḥ sutarpitān |
tataste sarvamācakhyūrājñodurvṛttamātmanaḥ ||42||

tathādṛṣṭasyaviprasya yadvākyaṃtanniśamyasaḥ |

tyajedahaṃtapaḥkiṃvāpyathavārtivināśayet |
tapaso'dhikamevāsaudharmoduḥkhavi nāśanaḥ || 44 ||

prāṇināṃ bhayabhītānāmabhayaṃ yatpradīyate |
sarvasatveṣuyaddānamekabhūtetuyādayā || 45 ||

sarvasatvapradānācca dayaikātraviśiṣyate |
evaṃsaṃcityaviprosaudvijaiḥ sārddhajagāmaha || 46 ||

sadvijaḥ pṛṣṭhalagnobhūttasyavaitapasobalāt || 47||

yamamārgasya cādhvānamācakrāma divākaraḥ || 48 ||

najalaṃnacatatrānnaṃvṛkṣacchāyāsukhaṃnahi |
śītaṃvātāṃdha kāraṃtuduḥsahadevadānavaiḥ || 49 ||

narakāṇitadagretuduḥsahānikrameṇatu |
aṣṭāviṃśatikoṭyastumadhyetāsāṃyayauraviḥ || 50 ||

paśyantsabrāhmaṇastāṃtuyātanāṃpāpakarmiṇāṃ |
yathodaredatya māneloḍyamānecaśatrubhiḥ || 51 ||

ekaikaṃ nirayaṃ tadvat śrūyate kaṃdatāṃ ravaḥ |
tatosaupravaroviprogacchamānomahāpathaṃ || 52||

vittobrāhmaṇaikena tadālakuṭapāṇinā |
bhobhodvijakkayāsyayyāmada tvāmamadakṣiṇāṃ ||53||

yādattātepurāmatyaṃkasminkāletutāṃ smara |
ityuktaḥ satutāṃ smṛtvāpratyuvācadvijaṃprati ||54||

uktaṃma yāpurāṇaikaṃtubhyaṃtenāsmiyācitaḥ |
gṛhaṃgatvāpradāsyāmi tirobhavamamāgrataḥ || 55 ||

tamāhāsaudvijobhūyone hāyaṃtatraviya te |
martyetuvyavahāropinateyattvaṃdadāmi te || 56 ||

tacchrutvāddijaṃ prāhanāsti kiṃcidihaiva me |
tamāhāsaudvijastestisukṛtaṃya tḷtaṃtvayā ||57||

tattebahudhanaṃ tvastitasyārddhasaṃprayaccha me |
ityuktonadadātyenaṃ dharmārddhanaṃdivākaraḥ ||58||

provācasāṃtvayi tvāsau nyāyataḥ saṃpradāpayan |
ṣaḍaṃgaṃcaivadharmasyatatoviprogatoyayau || 59 ||

gacchamānaṃtutaṃ bhūyogopālastaṃtathādharat |
kā kiṇyarthe tu gaustasyarakṣitānatuyācitaḥ || 60 ||

viprosaulajjayātasmādvismṛtaḥ kālaparyayāt |
ṣaḍvāgaṃtena dharmasya gṛhītaṃtasyavaibalāt || 61 ||

tathaivavasudhautenadharmakāreṇavāpunaḥ |
vastrakārapavitreṇatasyaivavaṇigdharmiṇaḥ || 62 ||

gṛhītaḥ sarvadharmopitatosaudvijasattamaḥ |
nistejāśvābhavattasmādgatuṃcāgrenaśaktavān || 63 ||

tatodivākarastaṃtusaṃgṛtyāśukareṇavai |
prayayauyatrarājāsaunirayesamavasthitaḥ ||64||

taṃdṛṣṭvā pacyamānaṃ tukuṃbhamadhyagataṃnṛpaṃ |
vanhināsaudvijaśreṣṭhovismayaṃsamapadyata ||65||

ha |
bhobhorājanpradattānimahādānāniṣoḍaśa || 66 ||

brāhmaṇebhyastvayāpūrvaratnaṃ vastrāṇicaiva hi |
grāmāṃvavividhāṃścaivanagarāṇipurāṇica || 67 ||

śrūyaṃtemunimukhyebhyovacasitapasādivam |
prāpnuvaṃtisudānaistunarābhaktyopapāditaiḥ ||68||

kasyedaṃkarmaṇaḥpāpaṃ yattvaṃsaṃdatya sesadā |
suguptoyamadūtaistuyadijānāsitadvacaḥ || 69 ||

tacchrutvāprāhataṃrājāśṛṇuviprayathātathaṃ |
rājovāca || mayādānānidattānibrāhmaṇebhyoyatastataḥ || 70 ||

subahūni pragṛtyāpiparebhyo'jñānacakṣuṣā |
prajāpīḍana saṃtāpātsamudbhūtohutāśanaḥ || 71 ||

nacaivākṛtapuṇyānāṃdevebhaktiḥsujāyate |
vipreṣucaviśeṣeṇatathāsādhutapasviṣu || 76 ||

tasmādeva paraṃ devaṃ manyehaṃtvāṃna saṃśayaḥ || 77 ||

yasmāttenārakāḥ sarve darśanāt sukhinobhavan |
mamatesaṃnidhauvipranasu khaṃvidyatesataḥ ||78||

kiṃciddharmavihīnasya devavatsaṃsthitasyavā |
tasmādvadasva me kiṃciddharmaṃ yagrastibhūtale || 79 ||

saṃsārepatitolokaḥ kṣetradāragṛhādiṣu || 80 ||

yathāsaṃkliśyatetadvayadidharmana sevate |
kinnaprāpnotisaṃmūḍhaḥ svargamokṣasukhānica || 81 ||

etacchrutvātutasyārkova canaṃprāhataṃdvijaṃ |

pṛṣṭastehaṃkṛtaste yodharmaḥsaṃvardhayiṣyati |
dharmākhyāturjanebhyastuyatkartuḥ saphalaṃ bhavet || 83 ||

tataḥ śuddhā sujivhāyāstasmādāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ |
tasmāddharmapravaktavyaṃlokānāṃ vihitaṃcayat || 84||

tattevakṣyāmiśrutvemaṃ sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet |
vatsarasya mukhaṃcaitraṃ pavitraṃśaṃkarapriyaṃ || 85 ||

devatānāṃpriyaṃtatrasvargādāryāāṃti sarvadā |
krīḍārtha bhuktimādāyasaṃvi hṛtyapunaḥpunaḥ || 86 ||

susaṃtuṣṭāḥ svakaṃdhāmavarṣaprativrajaṃtite |
gauryāsahahara stasminvasate krīḍatesaढ़ā || 87 ||

bālarūpaṃsa māsthāya sukanyārūpibhirgaṇaiḥ |
caitramāsetuye martyādīpadānamahotsavaṃ || 88 ||

kurvetisatataṃyadvat kārtikevāhidharmiṇaḥ |
śivāyacānyadevebhyaḥprekṣaṇaṃcamahotsavaṃ || 89 ||

teṣāṃdhāmatuyāṃtyeteye bhavaṃtyatipāpinaḥ |
tasmāccaturdaśīṃruṣṇāṃśuklāṃvā samupoṣyaca || 90 ||

ekadivadadyāddhenuṃdvijātibhyaḥśivāyaparikalpitāṃ |
evaṃhatvācapāpāniyātisvarganasaṃśayaḥ || 91 ||

etacchrutvāsa sūryeṇa sahāmātyadvijottamān |
samutthāpyayathātathyaṃ rājñe sarvanyavedayat || 92 ||

tataḥsarājātususaṃprahṛṣṭaḥ śrutvā vacastasyadvijottamasya |
niveśyatatraivatamevapaśvādvihāyasadyaḥ prayayauvideśaṃ || 93 ||

saraḥ sa dṛṣṭvaiva nikhanyamānaṃpṛṣṭvā tu mūlyena cakāra karma |
dinedineyāvadabhūtsuvarṇatathācagomūlyamaśeṣadānaṃ || 94 ||

tataḥ pradṛṣṭaḥ prayayausvadeśaṃ surakṣitaṃ maṃtrivaraistathānṛpaiḥ |
dvijopadi ṭaṃmacakārarājāvrataṃyathāvacca tadevadānaṃ || 95 ||

vraterutetasyapitākusaṃsthoyayaudivaṃ pūrvapitāmaheśaiḥ |
ārutyayānaṃsuvimānamagryaṃsaṃkrīḍamānaḥ sahasāpsarobhiḥ || 96 ||

idaṃvataṃyaḥ kurute susamyagaikānhikaṃvyāsaśivaprasādāt |
labheddivaṃpūrvapitāmahaiḥ sahanihatyapāpānipurākṛtāni || 97 ||

caturdaśīyaḥ samupoṣyayatnāddaraṃtu saṃpūjya tupūrṇimāyāṃ |
dhenuṃ pradyāddijasattamāyatasyāabhāvātsughṛtasyapātraṃ || 98 ||

savyaṃjanaṃ vastrayutaṃsahānnaṃ datvāphalaṃ yallabhate sudhenāḥ |
nihatyapāpāni purākṛtānidivaṃprayātīhanasaṃśayotra || 99 ||

ciraṃtyuṣitvāsaturudralokarājābhavedatrasudharmaśīlaḥ |
ekāṃprakuryātsuśubhāṃva saṃte caturdaśīṃsatyayutaḥsamaṃhi || 100 ||

datvābhavenmaṃtravidhānapūrva tuṣṭiryathāsyādihaśaṃkarasya |
maṃtrādhikoyodvijaeva śuddhaḥ sakṣatriyovaiśyavaropiśuddhaḥ || 1 ||

puṣṭaṃsusarpatusuvarṇavātyodadhāti maṃtreṇavalātśopi |
maṃtrātmakāḥ sarvaimehidevāḥ suyakṣarakṣaḥ pitaromanuṣyāḥ || 2 sanaktārātribhojanasahitā || 6 ||

sanatkumārauvāca

maṃtrāṇicoktāni purāva tesmintairarcanīyo bhagavānpinākī || 4 ||

kurvantsukṛcchrahicaturdināni caturdaśīvyāsaupoṣaṇīyā || 5 ||

ekādaśyāmekabhaktaṃsakṛtpānīyame vaca |
ayācitaṃtudvādaśyāṃsanaktācatrayodaśī || 6 ||

caturdaśīnirāhārā kartavyācamahāvrate |
pauṇimāyāṃtugodānaṃphalaṃka tvātadanute || 7 ||

yaditvamātmanaḥ kiṃciddbhāvodayāddijātaye |
śivaḥ saṃprīyatāmevaṃityuktvā phalamaśnute || 8 ||

vratasyāsyasa magrasyadravyābhāvācchṛṇuṣvatat |
palairdvādaśabhiḥ pātraṃśulvasyatutadardhakaṃ || 9 ||

palatrayasyavākuryātpalasyaikamakiṃcanaḥ |
ghṛtena pūrayitvātusa hiraṇyaṃ tukārayet || 10 ||

vastreṇaveṣṭayitvātutadabhāvātsasūtrakaṃ |
dadyāttaddijamukhyāyaśivaḥ saṃprīyatā miti || 11 ||

sthāṇumīśaṃjagannāthaṃśūlapāṇimaheśvaraṃ |


caṃdrārdhadhāriṇaṃdevaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūpitaṃ |
praṇato smijagannāthaṃsame pāpaṃ vyapohatu || 14 ||

triśūlapāṇinaṃtrya kṣatripathāṃkasudhāriṇaṃ |
praṇatosmiumānāthaṃsamepāpavya pohatu || 15 ||

rudracittagatoyanmerudrobuddhigataśvayaḥ |
yaśvāhaṃkāra korudraḥ samepāpaṃvyapohatu || 16 ||

evaṃpūjyaśivaṃmaṃtrai statodānaṃprakalpayet |
vrataṃrutvā tu yairdattamasminmatyaṃ mahāphalaṃ || 17||

surūpāḥ subhagāstetujāyate bhokriṇodhanaṃ |
evaṃ yaḥ kurutesamyakcaturmāsyāścaturdaśīṃ || 18 ||

dvayaṃyathāśatānīkoyātyasauśāṃkaraṃ padaṃ |
kulānāṃśatamuddhṛtyanippāpīsurapū jitaḥ || 19||

aṣṭāviṃśe mahāpuṇyaṃ śivanāmasahasrakaṃ |
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ yathāvat parikīrtyate || 1 ||

evaṃ vā kurute yastu cāturmāsīvidhānataḥ || 21 ||

yathā vai kumudo rājā svargayāti kalau yuge |
ekabhaktena naktena tathaivāyācitena yaḥ || 22 ||

vrataṃ prakurute bhaktyā tasya puṇyam asaṃkhyakaṃ |

brahmāṃḍarūpamadhyāyaiḥ kīrtyatesodbhavakṣayam || 1 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

evamuccāryataddyāddijebhyaḥ sarvadāhitu |
naikasyāpipradātavyaṃnanistāyaitatobhavet || 61 ||

dadātyevaṃtuyovyāsatulāpuruṣamuttamaṃ |


|| 4 ||

vyāsauvāca||

yenaikena hidattena sarveṣāmāpyate phalaṃ |
dānānāṃ tanmamākhyāhimānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthataḥ || 1 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

śṛṇukāleyayaddānātphalaviṃdatimānavaḥ |
ekasmādapisarveṣāṃdānānāṃtachṛṇuṣvame || 2||

dānānāmuttamaṃdānaṃbrahmāṃḍaṃkhalumānavaiḥ |
dātavyaṃmuktikāmaistusaṃsārottāraṇāyavai || 3 ||

brahmāṃḍesakaledatteyatphalaṃlabhatenaraḥ |
tadekabhāvādāpnotisaptalokādhipobhavet || 4||

yāvaccaṃdra divākaraunabhasivaiyāvatsthirāmedinītāvatsopinaraḥsvabāṃdhavayutaḥsvargaikasāmokasi |
sarveṣvevamanonugaiḥsabahubhirbrahmāṃḍadaḥ krīḍatepaścādyātiparaṃ sudurlabhapadaṃdevairmunemādhavaḥ || 5 ||

vyāsauvāca ||

bhagavanbrūhibrahmāṃḍaṃyatpramāṇaṃ yadātmakaṃ |
yadādhāraṃyathājātaṃyena mepratyayo bhavet ||6||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

muneśṭaṇupravakṣyāmi yadutsedhaṃtuvistaraṃ |
brahmāṃḍaṃtususaṃkṣepācchṛṇupāpātpramucyate || 7 ||

yattatkāraṇamavyaktaṃśivaṃpadmanāmayaṃ |
yasmādutpadyate sarvalīyatecapunaḥpunaḥ ||8||

tasmātsaṃjāyate vyomapuṣkarādvāyusaṃbhavaḥ |
vāyoraniratastvāpoyovaisaṃjāyatedharā || 9 ||

dharādibhūtamunmathya tadaṃḍamupajāyate |
tasmātsaṃjāyatebrahmādvīdhābhūtādvikālataḥ || 10 ||

pātālānitusamaivabhuvanānita thordhvataḥ |
ucchrāyaṃdviguṇaṃ tasyajalamadhyasthitasyaca || 11 ||

tasyādhāraḥ sitonāgaḥsacaviṣṇuḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
brahmaṇovacaso hetorbibhartisakalaṃtvidaṃ || 12 ||

yonaṃtaḥpaṭhyate siddhairdevarṣigaṇapūjitaḥ ||13||

sahasraśirasovyaktaḥsarvāvidyotayandiśaḥ |

kirīṭīsragviṇobhātiyaḥ sadaivaikakuṃḍalaḥ || 15 ||

sāyaṃgaṃgāpravāheṇaśvetahāropaśobhitaḥ |
nīlavāsāma dodriktaḥ kailāsādririvonnataḥ || 16 ||

lāṃgalāsaktahastāgroviśvanmusalamuttamaṃ |

saṃkarṣaṇātmakorudroviṣānalaśikhojvalaḥ |
kalpāṃteyasyavakrebhyoniṣkramyāttijagatryaṃ ||18||

āste pātālamūla sthaḥsaśeṣaḥ kṣitimaṃḍalaṃ |
viśvatsarṣapavadbhūtaṃśeṣośeṣa surārcitaḥ || 19 ||

nahivarṇa yituṃ jñātuṃsvarūpaṃ rūpamevaca || 20 ||

āstekusumamālevaphaṇāmaṇiśilāruṇā |
yasyaipāsakalāpṛthvī kastadvīryavadiṣyati || 21 ||


nāṃtaṃguṇānāṃgacchaṃtisiddhāmunisu kinnarāḥ |
gaṃdharvoragadevāśvatenānaṃtoyamavyayaḥ || 23 ||

muhuḥśvāsānilāpāstaṃlo hitaṃharicaṃdanaṃ |
yasyanāgavadhūhastairdiśāṃyātisuvāsatāṃ || 24 ||

jñātavān sakalaṃcaiva gagajyotīṃṣitatvataḥ |
yamārā dhyapurāṇarṣirnimittapaṭhitaṃ phalaṃ || 25 ||

bibhartimālāṃlokānāṃśirasāvivṛtāmahī |

daśasahasramekaikaṃpātālaṃmunisattama |
sutalaṃvitalaṃcaivanitalaṃcagabhastimat || 27 ||

ucchrāyaṃdviguṇaṃ caiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃratnabhūmayaḥ || 28 ||

ratnavaṃ toyaprāsādābhūmayohemasaṃbhavāḥ |

nivasaṃtimahābhāgārākṣasādaityasaṃbhavāḥ |

svarlokādapiramyāṇitebhyosāvāgatodivi |

pātālekasyanaprītiritaśvetaśvaśobhate || 32 ||

devadānavakanyābhirvimuktasyāpijāyate |
divārkara śmayoyatranabhavaṃ tividhorniśi || 33 ||

naśītamātapoyatramaṇitejotrakevalaṃ |
bhakṣyabhojyānnapānānibhujyaṃtemuditairbha śaṃ ||34||

yatranajñāyatekālogatopimunisattama |
puṃskokilarutaṃtatravanyānikamalākarāḥ || 35||

nadyaḥ sarāṃsi ramyā ṇimanojñānyaṃbarāṇica |

daityoragaiśvabhujyaṃte pātālevaisukhānica || 37 ||

tapasāsamavāptānidānavaiḥ siddhamānavaiḥ || 38 ||


sanatkumārauvāca ||

teṣāmūrdhvopariṣṭādvainara kāstāṃcchṛṇuṣvame |
vadatomemuniśreṣṭhapacyaṃteyatrapāpinaḥ || 1 ||

rauravaḥśūkaro rodhastālovivasanastathā |
mahājvālastaptakuṃbholavaṇopivilohitaḥ || 2 ||

vaitaraṇīpūyavahāka milaḥkṛmibhojanaḥ |


śvabhojanopyadṛṣṭaśvamahārauravaśālmalī |

kūṭasākṣyaṃtuyovaktivi nāvipraṃsurāsavam |
sadānnṛtaṃ vadedyastusana royātirauravaṃ || 6 ||

bhrūṇahāparasvahartāgorodhoviśvaghātakaḥ || surāpotrahmahā hartā suvarṇa syatuśūkare

taiḥ saṃsargīsavaiyātirājñovaiśya gurorva dhāt |
tama kuṃbhe svasurmāturgāmīcaduhitustuyaḥ || 8 ||

tamaloheṣu pacyaṃteyaśvabhaktaṃparityajet || 9||

devadūṣayitācaiva vedavikrayaruccayaḥ || 10 ||

avīcirodhepatati maryādādūṣa kastathā || 11 ||

sayātirumibhakṣecarumilecaduriṣṭakṛt || 12 ||

pitṛdevasurānya stuparyanātinarādhamaḥ |
lālābhakṣesayātyajñoyaḥ śastrakūṭakṛnnaraḥ ||13||

viśasanesayātyugre asadvāhītuyodvijaḥ |

rudhirāṃdhepataṃtyetesomavikrayiṇaśvaye |
mitrahāmadhuhāyātikrūrāṃvaitaraṇīṃnadīṃ || 15 ||

navayauvanamattāścamaryādābhedina śraye |

asipatravaneyātivṛkṣacchedī vayaivayaḥ |
auraśnakomṭagavyādho vanhijvālepataṃtite || 17||

pātyaṃtedvijatatraivayaścāpāpeṣuvanhidaḥ |


putrairadhyāpitāye catapataṃviśvabhojane |


bhujyaṃteyānipuruṣairnara kāṃtara gocaraiḥ |
varṇāśramaviruddhaṃcakarmakurvaṃtiyenarāḥ || 22 ||



yāvaṃtojaṃtavaḥsvargetāvaṃtonara kaukasaḥ || 25 ||


tayātathai vasaṃsmṛtyaproktāniparamarṣibhiḥ |
guruṇi gurubhiścaivalaghubhiścalaghūnica || 27 ||

yāniteṣāmaśeṣāṇāṃtāni karmāṇyaghānivai || 28 ||


maheśvaramavāpnotimadhyānhā diṣu saṃsmaran || 30 ||

prātarniśicasaṃdhyāyāṃsadyaḥ pāpakṣayaṃnaraḥ |
muktiṃprayātisvargāptiḥ samastakleśasaṃkṣayaḥ || 31 ||

śivasyasmaraṇādeva tasyabhaṃ gonumīyate |
tasyāṃtarāyovipreṃdrajapahomārcanādiṣu || 32 ||

maheśvaretu bhaktasya deveṃdratvādikaṃ phalaṃ |
pumānnanarakaṃyāti saṃsmaranbhaktitomune || 33 ||

śivamaharniśaṃtasmātsaṃkṣīṇāśeṣapātake |
narakaḥ svargasaṃjñovaipāpapuṇyairdvijottama || 34 ||

yastvekameva duḥkhāyasukhāyerṇyodbhavāyaca |



atordhvatepravakṣyāmisamastaṃ maṃḍalaṃ bhuvaḥ || 38 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

pārāśaryasusaṃkṣepācchṛṇutvaṃvada tomama |

śākastu samamaḥ sarve samudraiḥ samabhirṭatāḥ |
lavaṇesurasaiḥ sarpi dadhidugdhajalaiḥ samam || 2 ||

meroruparikāleyayojanānatithāpuraṃ |

samaṃtādbrahmaṇaḥ puryāḥ plāvayi tveṃdu maṃḍalaṃ |

dharmiṇāmālayātyetabhaumāḥ svargāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

sanatkumārauvāca ||

vakṣyehaṃ bhārataṃva himādreścaivadakṣiṇaṃ |

navayojanasāhasrovistārosya mahāmune |
karmabhūmiriyaṃ svargāpavargapuṇyapāpibhiḥ || 2 ||

ataḥ saṃprā pyatesvargomokṣonarakaevaca |

iṃdradyumnaḥ kaseruśvatāmraparṇogabhastimān |

nā nājanapadāsteṣuva saṃtomaṃḍaleṣuvai || 13 ||

āsānusaṃti || 14 ||

rutādīninacānyeṣuddīpeṣuprabha vaṃtihi |
dānānicātradīyatejuvhate cātrayajvanaḥ || 15 ||

tapastapyaṃtiyatayaḥ paralokārthamādāt |

tatrāpibhārataṃ śreṣṭhamatonyābhogabhūmayaḥ |
kadācillabhyatemartyaiḥ sahasairmunisattama || 17 ||

atra janma sahasrāṇāṃ mānuṣyaṃ puṇyasaṃcayaṃ || 18 ||

svargāpavargāspadamārgabhūtedhanyāstutebhāratabhūmibhāge |
gāyaṃtidevāḥ kilagī takānibhavaṃtibhūyaḥ puruṣāḥsuratvāt || 19 ||

avāpyamānuṣyamayaṃ kadācidvihṛtyaśaṃbhauparamātmabhūte |
phalānisarvāṇitu karmajāniyāsyāmahetattanutāhitasya || 20 ||

prāpsyatidhanyāḥkhalute manuṣyāvayaṃkṛtāḥkarmaṇisaṃniviṣṭāḥ |
jānīmataista dviśivaṃ sukhāyate bhārateceṃdriyavipradhanyāḥ || 21 ||

jaṃbūdvīpaṃ jayākhyātaṃ kṣārodadhi susaṃvṛtaṃ || 22||

saeva dviguṇobrahmanlakṣadvīpaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 23||

gomedaścaiva caṃdra śvanāradoduṃdubhistathā |

varṣācaleṣuramyeṣumuditāḥ satataṃprajāḥ |
vasaṃti devagaṃdharvāvarṣeṣveteṣunityaśaḥ || 25 ||

nādhayovyādhayovāpijanānāṃtaṃtrakutracit |


kṣudranayastathāśai lāstathāsaṃtisahasraśaḥ |

tretāyugasamaḥ kālaḥ sarvadaiva mahāmune || 29 ||


stannāmasaṃjñovailasodvīpodvijottama |

hariścabhagavānbrahmāyajñairmatraiśvavaidikaiḥ |

śvetoya haritaścaiva jīmūtorohitastathā || 33 ||

vaidyutomānava ścaiva suprabhaḥ samamo mune |
śālmalenatuvṛkṣeṇatenaśālmalisaṃjñitaḥ || 34 ||


varṣāśivyaṃjikānagra stāsāṃnāmānimeśṛṇu ||35||

śuklāraktā hiraṇyācacaṃdrāśukrāvimocanā |

bhagavaṃtaṃsadāviṣṇumijyatevividhairmakhaiḥ || 37 ||

devānāṃ tatrasānnidhyamatī vasumanorame |

dviguṇenakuśadvīpaḥ samaṃtādvātyataḥsthitaḥ |
vasaṃtitatradaiteyāma nujaiḥ sahadānavāḥ || 39 ||


tacaiva cakuśadvīpe brahmabhūtaṃjanārdanam |
yaja̭ticatatheśānaṃsarvakāmaphalapradam || 41 ||

maṇidrumo mahāśailaḥ samamomaṃdarācalaḥ || 42 ||


anyāḥ sahasraśaḥsaṃtiśubhāpohemavālukāḥ || 44 ||

kuśadvī pekuśastaṃ boghṛtodenasamāvṛtaḥ |

dviguṇenasamudreṇadadhimaṃḍoda kāṭataḥ |

divācya manācaiva puṃḍarīkaśva duṃdubhiḥ || 47||

nivasaṃtinirātaṃ kāvarṣaśaileṣuteṣuvai |
sarva sauvarṇaramyeṣusahadevagaṇaiḥ prajāḥ ||48||

saṃtitatra mahānadyaḥ saptānyāstusahasraśaḥ || 49 ||

khyātiśvapuṃḍarīkācayāḥ pibaṃtipayaḥ śubham || 50 ||

bhagavānpūjyatetatrayogairudra svarūpavān |

pūrvetatrodayagirirja ladhāropareyataḥ || 52 ||

śyāmo yastaṃ giriścaiva aṃbikeyaśva kesarī |



anyāḥ sahasraśastatra kṣudranadyo mahāmune |

dharmahānirnateṣvastisvargādāgatyamānavāḥ |
varṣeṣuteṣupṛthivīṃna saṃgharṣaḥ parasparam ||57||

śākadvīpetuvai sūryaḥ pūjyatejanapadaiḥ sadā |
yathoktairijyatesamyakkarmabhirniyatātmabhiḥ || 58 ||

kṣīrodenāvṛtaḥsopidviguṇena samaṃtataḥ |

dviguṇenamahāvarṣastatrakhyātothamānasaḥ |

tāvataiva tulakṣāṇi sarvatovalayākṛtiḥ |
puṣkaradvīpavalayomadhyamena bibhartica || 61 ||

tenaivavalayākāraddayava rṣasamākṛtiḥ |

adhamottamataste pāṃnavyavasthāmahāmune || 63 ||

nyagrodhaḥpuṣkaradvīpe brahmaṇaḥ sthānamuttamam ||66||

ravicaṃdramasoryāvanmayūkhābhāsayatihi |
tāvatpramāṇāpṛthivībhūrlokaḥ satugīyate || 1 ||

bhūmeryojanala kṣetusaṃsthitaṃravimaṃ ḍhalam |

śaśinastuprahāṇoyojagataḥ paricakṣate |
raverūrdhvaśaśītasthaulakṣayo janasaṃkhyayā ||3||

grahāṇāṃmaṃḍalaṃkṛttraṃśaśinoparisaṃsthitam |

budhastasmādatho kāvyastasmāduparimaṃgalaḥ |
bṛhaspatistadūrdhvaṃtutasyopariśanaiśvaraḥ || 5 ||


meḍhībhūtaḥ samastasyajyotiścakrasyavaidhruvaḥ |
bhūrbhuvaḥ svaritijñeyaṃbhuvordhvatudhuvādavā k || 7 ||

ekayojanakoṭītuyatra te kalpavāsinaḥ |

viśvedevāstathārudrāḥ sādhyānāgāḥ surādayaḥ |

tebhyaḥ putrāstathānyeṣāṃbījānyanyānivaiyataḥ |
mahadādayoviśeṣatāstadbhavaṃtisurādayaḥ || 23||

sūryakāṃta maṇeḥ sūryāyadvaddahniḥ prajāyate || 24||

tadvatsaṃjāyate sṛṣṭiḥ śivastatrana kāmayet |
śivaśakti samāyogedevādyāḥprabhavaṃtihi || 25||

tathā karmasvanekeṣuprarohamupayāṃtivai |

yasmādutpadyate sarvaṃ yasmiśvalayameṣyati |
kartākriyāṇāṃ sarvāsāṃsaśivaḥ parigīyate || 27 ||

munaya ūcuḥ ||

paḍakṣaraṃtvayā proktaṃpurāsarvārthasādhakaṃ |
śivasyaparamaṃmaṃtraṃ bhuktimuktikaraṃśivaṃ || 28 ||

vayaṃtuśrotumicchāmomaṃtrānparamasādhanān |
yaiḥ purāmunayaḥ siddhāḥ śivādamaratāṃgatāḥ || 29 ||

sūtamāṃḍavyādayaḥ sarvetānsarvāstvaṃva dasvanaḥ |
sūtauvāca || pravakṣyāmima taṃśaṃbhoryaduktaṃ hipurā kila || 30 ||

praṇavaṃ prathamaṃ gṛtyapaśvāddevaṃsamuddharet || 31 ||

trivargasyatṛtīyaṃtu paṃcama svarasaṃyutam |
masta kebiṃdu saṃyuktaṃpaṃcamaṃ parigṛtyaca ||32||

aṣṭamasya tṛtīyaṃtusavisargasamuddharet |

paromokṣaḥ paraḥ śuddhaḥparodharmaḥparovikṣuḥ |
brahmahatyādipāpāni agamyāgama nānica ||34||

viśvasta mitrahācaiva gurughnaḥpitṛghātakaḥ || 35||

mātṛhāstrīgurughnaśvaa nyānyuktānisaṃghaśaḥ |
smaraṇānmaṃtrarājasyabhasmasādyatinityaśaḥ ||36||

śatajā pyasyamaṃtrasyapuṣpaṃnyasyaśivasyake || 37 ||

koṭimaṃtrārjitaṃ puṇyaṃlabhatesādhakottamaḥ |
trisaṃdhyaṃyoja penmaṃtraṃśatamekaṃprayatna taḥ || 38 ||

saṃpuṭenāvaroheṇana mṛtyorvaśagobhavet |
lalāṭeva kahṛdayenābhaugulye tathaiva ca || 39 ||

bākostuhastayoḥ pārśvaṣṭa ṣṭhejānvostu jaṃghayoḥ |
gulphayoḥ pādayoścaiva sṛṣṭinyāsakrameṇatu || 40 ||

evaṃvinyasya dehetuimaṃ maṃtramanusmaret |
mucyatesa rvapāpebhyaḥkoṭijanmaśatairapi || 41 ||

vañcopalamahāvarṣacauravyāghrabhayādibhiḥ |
ādhibhiḥsādhvasairanyairjvara kuṣṭhabhayādi bhiḥ || 42 ||

rogairvimucyatesarvairyebhyoduḥkhamihāgataṃ |
saṃgrāmejayamāpnotisaubhāgyamatulaṃbhavet || 43 ||

japaṃcamānasaṃ kuryāddivārātraṃcasādhakaḥ |
sarvāvasthāgatonityaṃjapantsiddhimavāpnuyāt || 44 ||

trikālaṃ bhāryayāyuktoyaṃtrārūḍhastusādha kaḥ |
nopavāsaṃnamaunaṃ cabrahmacaryaṃ na cāstikam || 45 ||

sarvakarmapravṛttastu siddhyatyevanasaṃśayaḥ |
punaranyatpravakṣyāmidhyā naṃsarvārthasādhanam ||46||

kuṃbhamudrāṃparāṃbadhvāsukhāsanasthitaḥpumān |
ātmadehesadādhyāyenmaṃtraṃ mṛtyuṃjayaṃ paraṃ ||47||

varṣamā ṇaṃsudhārābhiḥsahasradalasaṃyutaṃ |
śvetapadmaṃ sucaṃdraṃvai payastālunisaṃsthitam || 48 ||

evaṃhidhyāyatastasyadivyaṃ rūpaṃ prajāyate |
aṇimādiguṇāvāptirmānasīgatirūpavān || 49 ||

sarvajñaḥ sarvagotdṛṣṭoharadarśīparāśrutiḥ |
vatsarātsarvamāpnoti ekaci ttaḥsamāhitaḥ ||50||

tatrāsyābhyāsayuktaśvakhecaratvaṃprajāyate || 51 ||

sūtauvāca ||

devyāśrvakārtikeyenanaṃdinoktamṛṣestuyat || 52 ||

oṃ namaḥ śivāya ṣaṭtriśeśivasaṃprītikārako yoga uttamaḥ |
devyaiśivenakathitaḥ parisphuṭa mudāhṛtaḥ || 1 ||

agastyapramukhāviprāmunayovītakalmaṣāḥ |
babhūvurmaṃtrarājenajarāmaraṇavarjitāḥ || 53 ||

mayātubhyaṃsamākhyātaṃmaṃtraṃsmṛtyuvināśanam ||53||


|| 4 ||

sūtauvāca ||

etaddomesa māravyātaṃpunarvakṣyāmyataḥ paraṃ |
hṛdayaṃmaṃtrarājasyadhyānaṃjapyaṃ sadārcanam ||1||

sayādipaṃcavaktrāṇi nityaṃsaṃnihitāni |
yasyaiva masti vijñānaṃ sohaṃnāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ||2||

nāsyasṛṣṭyāvi sṛṣṭyāvāpaṃcavakrāṇi meśubhe |
maṃtrarājaṃjapedyastumucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 3 ||

sidhyaṃtisarvakarmāṇiaṇimādiguṇāapi |
devyuvāca || śrutaṃdhyānaṃmayādevamaṃtraṃ cāmṭatyukārakam || 4||

saṃyatairyogibhiḥprāpyaṃna kadāciddurātmabhiḥ |
kāmakrodhādibhi deṣirajasāvyākulaṃjagat || 5 ||

dvaṃdva duḥkhaśatāviṣṭamiṃdriyārthaiḥ susaṃyutam |
jagatīdṛgvidhedevasaṃsāreduḥkhasaṃjñite || 6 ||

hi tārtha sarvamatyāṃnāṃmokṣopāyaṃvadasva me |
ityuktastāmuvācedaṃśṭaṇuṣvāryeyathātatham || 7 ||

dehesminnahamityekaḥ pṛthakcityaḥ sadābudhaiḥ |
evaṃjñātvācaraṃllokemucyate bhavabaṃdhanāt ||8||

mametiparamaṃduḥkhaṃnamametiparaṃsukham |
dvāvimaubaṃdhamokṣāṇāṃna mametimametica || 9 ||

yasyanāstyātmanodehastasya dārādikaṃ katham |
gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ tadvadevaṃ baddhonamucyate || 10 ||

epapāśupa toyogaḥ samāsātkathitomayā |
devyuvāca || yattvayoktaṃnatasyāpyaṃmānavairajiteṃdriyaiḥ || 11 ||

mamāhamiticākhyātivaca naṃhimanojavaḥ |
mahādevauvāca || jetuṃrāgādayaḥ śakyānatujanmāṃtarairapi || 12||

kṛteyugepideveśi alpāyurmanujoyataḥ |
tretāyāṃdvāpare caiva kimuprāpmekalauyuge || 13 ||

vyāsauvāca||


sanatkumārauvāca|| tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvākṛpāyuktomaheśvaraḥ || 15 ||

martyānāṃ hitakāmāyaprā hedaṃpārvatīṃtataḥ |
śaṃkarauvāca || yadyevaṃ muktikāmāsi mānavānāṃ hitāyaṃvai || 16 ||

sayogaḥprāpyatesadbhiścittasyaivātmanātuyaḥ || 17||

evaṃ jñātvātupratha maṃkriyāyogaṃsusaṃgatam ||18||

kriyāyogaṃvināgaṃtuṃśaktonaiva kathaṃcana |
tatodvijaiśvarājanyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdraistathāpriye || 19 ||

śūdraiśvakarmanirataiḥ kriyāyogāśritaṃmanaḥ |

malliṃgārcanaśaktānāṃnakiṃcidbhu vidurlabham |

japapūjāparācaivatasyaivayajaneratāḥ |
vratopavāsanira tāhiṃsādiparivarjitāḥ || 22 ||

rudratulyābhaviṣyaṃtimamalokenarottamāḥ |
yomapaśyati sarvatrasamavasthitamīśvaram ||23||

sattāvāntsarvabhūteṣusarudronātra saṃśayaḥ |
jaṃgamājaṃgameyomāṃve cyātmaniparatathā || 24 ||

samatāsarvabhūteṣusarudronātrasaṃ śayaḥ |
saṃproktalakṣaṇaliṃgaṃkārayitvātusthāpayet || 25 ||

suvarṇarajatādyaistusatkṛtaṃvāsudhātubhiḥ |
supāṣāṇaiḥ suranaiśvamṭa nmayaṃvāpiśaktitaḥ ||26||

vividhaiḥpūjopahāraiśśrvabhaktyātannityamarcayet |
saevaṃpūjitonityaṃdhyātaścaiva maheśvari || 27||

bhuṃjangacchaṃstathātiṣṭhanpārśvapṛṣṭhetathāgrataḥ |
uparyadhastathādhyātaḥ satumuktikarobhavet || 28 ||

jalaiḥ śubhairdhṛtāyaistunā nairga 'dhānulepanaiḥ |
caṃdanāgarukarpūrairgītavādyairmanoramaiḥ || 29 ||

prīṇayantsatataṃliṃga marcayenmatparāyaṇaḥ ||30||

matkathāyāṃsadācittaṃsaṃdhyāyetparameśvaraṃ |
śrotavyābhairavādyāstukathāḥ paurāṇasaṃbhavāḥ || 31 ||

evaṃsamarpitamanāḥpuruṣoma tparāyaṇaḥ |
cittasthānakhilānbhogānprāpnotyakhilanāyakaḥ ||32||

kāmānusaktapuruṣomatkriyāparamaḥ paraṃ |
padamāpnotijñā tvaivaṃmakkriyāparamābhava ||33 ||

śivaṃcittagataṃyasyaśivaṃbuddhigataṃcayat |
tathāhaṃkāragorudroyo rudrodehasaṃsthitaḥ || 34 ||

karotikartṛbhūtosaucarasyasthāvarasyaca |

mayyevasanyasetsarvamātmanaḥkarmake valaṃ |

etenaivopadeśenatavākhyātaṃmayādhunā |
mānuṣāṇāṃhitārthāyasaṃsārabha yabhīruṇā || 37 ||

kriyāyogastuvādyārthīdvayormuktirudāhṛtā || 38 ||

kriyāyogaṃvināyogaṃduḥsādhyamamarairapi |
kriyāyogāddhidevatvaṃ tato muktimavāpnuyāt || 39 ||

tathāliṃgārcane bhāvodahetpāpānyaśeṣataḥ || 40 ||

mudhaivoktaṃ bhavetpāpaṃmudhaivoktaṃ bhavetkṣayaṃ |
mudhaivanaṣṭaṃtajja nmayatranārādhitoharaḥ || 41 ||

navastrabhūṣaṇairatnairnasuvarṇasyarāśibhiḥ |
tuṣṭiṃyātimaheśānovinābhaktiṃ maheśvari || 42 ||

ka rmabhūmauhimānuṣyamāpyatebahubhiryugaiḥ |
tatprāpyanārcayelliṃgaṃtasyajanmanirarthakam ||43||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

ityetatkathi tadevyāḥśaṃkareṇapurāmayā |

vyāsauvāca||

yairmaṃtrairarcayaṃtismakramānme brūhitānmune || 45 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

āmnāyairvihatairmatraistairyajaṃtidvijo ttamāḥ |
tadṛtetupurāṇoktairarcayetsamupāditeḥ || 46 ||

taddīkṣayābhiyuktastvaṃbhūtapūrvomahāmune || 47 ||

śivamūrtibhistatsarva jagadetacca rācaram |

sarvagaḥśaśisūryauca śivaprakṛtayaḥparāḥ || 49 ||

sarve maṃtrātmaka devāmaṃtrāviṃdusamāśritāḥ |
biṃdurnādasthitonityaṃnādaśvaparamātmani || 50 ||

maṃtraistaiḥ pūjyatedevaḥ śivaḥ sarvamayomahān |
yakārasyāṣṭamaṃbījaṃmātrādvādaśabheditaṃ || 51 ||

akṣaraṃnirataṃrutvāṣaḍaṃgaṃśiva mucyate |
dīrghādhaṃgāḥ samākhyātāthṛtsthāvakrāṇiśūlinaḥ || 52 ||

śivasyaparamātyeṣāpūjāproktāmanīṣibhiḥ |

ihaloke pare caiva munesatyamumāpateḥ |
maṃtroyaṃ sarvagonityaṃ sarvavyāpyavyavasthitaḥ || 54 ||

jñātavyaḥ suprayatnenadhyeyaḥ pūjyaśva sarvadā |
prasādomekṛtodevi maṃtrākhyānānmaheśvari || 55 ||

prāsādayenavikhyātaṃ yāsyatīhamahītale |
yonavaivettisaṃyuktaṃpaṃca maṃtraṃmahātanum || 56 ||

sukalābhiḥsamāyuktaṃnasamāheśvarobhavet |
paṃcavaktvā ṇidevasyanityaṃsaṃnihitānica || 57 ||

tānivakṣyāmiyairmatraira cyate cehaśūlinaḥ |

dvipādau samākhyātaiauṭatīyaṃśṭaṇutānaghāḥ |
bhavebhavenātibhavebhajasvamāṃbhavodbhavaḥ || 59 ||

vāmojyeṣṭhaśvarudraśvakālarudrastṛtīyakaḥ || 60 ||

kālaḥ kalavikarṇaśvabalapramathanastathā |
sarvabhūtadamovāpi manonmanastathaivaca || 61 ||

aghorebhyothaghorebhyoghora ghoratarebhyaśvasarvataḥ || 62 ||

sarvebhyaḥ sarvaśarvebhyo namaste stu rudrarūpebhyaḥ |
catuṣpādaṃ samākhyātaṃ namaskāreṇa saṃyutaṃ || 63 ||

sarvaṣāṃcaiva maṃtrāṇāṃ maṃtrarājaṃ prakīrtitaṃ |
asyaivābhyāsayogena nāstitayannasādhayet || 64 ||


trailokyeyāḥ striyaḥ kāśvi dākṛṣṭāḥ śataśoparāḥ |

devāndaityāṃstathāsiddhān saṃyakṣoragarākṣasān |
bhūtavaitālapaiśācān usarvāṃca vaśamānayet || 67 ||

mṛtyubaṃdhārtibhayadaṃ duḥkhaṃgrahasamudbhavaṃ |
smaraṇānmaṃtrarājasya bhasmasādyātinityaśaḥ || 68 ||

cāturthikaṃtathāghoraṃ nāśayatyāśudarśanāt || 69 ||

daṃtodbhavaṃ nakhodbhūtaṃ haṃti maṃtraprabhāvataḥ || 70 ||

sumaṃtrī śatajapyena śatajanmasamudbhavaṃ |
pāpahaṃti nasaṃdehaḥ svargamokṣaṃ caviṃdati || 71 ||


munayaūcuḥ ||

evaṃ prabhāvasya hi maṃtrarājño vidhānamuktaṃ munināpurāte |
tadbrūhi naḥ sarvam aśeṣato nyad dhitāya kāle yatato narāṇāṃ || 1 ||

vyāsauvāca ||

śṛṇudhvam etanmunayaḥ sametāḥ sanatkumāreṇa vidhānamuktaṃ |
samāsato deva mato vidhānaṃ sumaṃtrarājasya hi sādhanāya || 2 ||

deśe sugupte sumanorametु snātvā śuciś caikamanā pi bhūtvā |
saṃsmṛtya maṃtram anasā tadaṃgai nyasaṃ karābhyāṃ karayoś cakuryāt || 3 ||

hakārayuktena tu vahninā tha paśvāt ṣaḍaṃgaṃ guruṇā svareṇa || 4 ||

caturbhirhṛdayaṃproktaṃcaturbhistuśirobhavet |
aṣṭabhistuśikhājñeyā aṣṭabhiḥ kavacaṃnyaset || 5 ||

aṣṭabhirnetramuddiṣṭaṃphaḍustesaṃ niyojayet |
evaṃvinyasya dehetu maṃtrarūpamanusmaret || 6 ||

māheśvaraṃvapuḥrutvājaṭāmukuṭabhūṣaṇaṃ |

kalavikaraṇāyanamobalapramathanāyanamaḥ |
sarvabhūtadamanāya namonmanonmanāyanamaḥ || 8 ||

sarvābhara ṇapūrṇāgaṃcaṃdrārddhakṛtaśekharaṃ |

sthaṃḍilevāpiliṃgevāpaścādyajanamārabhet |

oṃ krīṃ aghorebhyaḥ śirase svāhāśiraīśānyāṃnyaset |
oṃḍūṃghora ghoratarebhyaḥ śi khāyai vaṣaṭśikhāṃnairṛtyevinyaset || 11 ||

oṃ sarvataḥ sarvebhyaḥ kavacāyanamaḥ kavacaṃvāyavyevinyaset |
oṃ krauṃnamasterudra rūpebhyo netrābhyāṃvaṣaṭnetramuparivinyaset || 12 ||

oṃ kraḥ astrāya phaṭ astraṃ caturdikṣu vinyaset |
evaṃnyasyavidhānenahakāraṃ vahnisaṃyutaṃ || 13 ||

nādabiṃdu kalākrāṃtamaghoraṃhṛdayeyajet |
madhyeghāreśvarīdevīṃtasyotsaṃgetupūjayet || 14 ||

daṃḍaśaśika raṃcaiva aṃkuśaṃkartarīdhvajau |
savisargāparādevīaghorāparikīrtitā || 15||

ādibījaṃsamuddiśya dvitīyasya caturthakaṃ |
tra yodaśenacākrāṃtaṃvanhimekādaśānvitaṃ || 16 ||

aṣṭamasyādimaṃrūpaṃ samasta syacaturthakam |
hakāraṃtrisvarākrāṃta huṃphaṭkāreṇa saṃyutaṃ || 17||

eṣāvidyāmahāvidyānāmnāghoreśvarītica |
sakārepūjitādevīsadyaḥpratyayakārikā || 18 ||

evaṃsaṃpūjaye deva maghoraṃ ghoranāśanaṃ |
manasāpūjayedyastuekacittaḥ samāhitaḥ || 19 ||

sarvānkāmānavāpnotiihalokaparatraca || 20 ||

kapālamālābharaṇaṃcaturbhujaṃva ke kṣaṇaṃśūladharābhayapradam |
trilocanaṃkhaṃḍaśaśāṃkadhāriṇaṃdhyāyeddharaṃ yaḥ salabhetaīpsitam || 21 ||

sarvāvasthāgatonityaṃyaḥ pūjayatiśaṃkaram |
apavitraḥ pavitro vāsvacchaṃda stena kīrtitaḥ || 22||

bilvapuṣpaistuyo lakṣaṃjabhvāhomaṃtukārayet |
ekaliṃgeśucaudeśetasya pratyakṣatāmiyāt || 23 ||

dadātyabhimatasiddhimaṇimādiguṇātmi kām |
kamalairyoya jellakṣaṃ paśvāddhomaṃsamārabhet || 24 ||

tataḥ prasannatāṃ gatvādehenānyenaśaṃkaraḥ |
nayatyabhīpsitāṃllokānja rāmṛtyuvivarjitān || 25 ||

śrīpatraguggulasyāpi guṭikābhiḥ samācaret |
japahomaṃvidhānenapātāle siddhimāpnuyāt || 26 ||

satataṃyojapenmaṃtraṃ aghoraṃkleśanāśanam |
tasya sarvāḥ prasiddhyaṃtisiddhayomanasepsitāḥ || 27 ||

tilahomenanaśyaṃ tigrahapīḍāyupadravāḥ |
saghṛtaśarkarayāca āyurvaddhirbhavediha || 28 ||

kṣīravāsamidbhistu samṛddhirupagacchati |
sadā ghore śvarīdevīṃyojapedarcayennaraḥ || 29 ||

aghoraṃhiyathādaivaṃ dviguṇaṃ tasya tatphalam |

taṃtaṃprāpnotyasaṃdigdhaṃdevyāścaiva prabhāvataḥ |
japyahomārcanenaivadevyāḥkarmaṇisādhane || 31 ||

badhvāghoreśvarīṃmudrāṃda rśayeddevatuṣṭaye |

ubha yostarjanībhyāṃtusaṃgṛhṇīyādanāmike || 33 ||

saṃmṛśyamadhya mecordhva maṃguṣṭhau śleṣayettayoḥ |
kṛtvātyadhomukhāṃmudrāṃlalāṭesaṃ niveśayet || 34 ||

baṃdhaurutvāta tonetrejivhāṃcaiva hi cālayet |
saṃsmaretparamaṃmaṃtraṃ sarvasiddhipravartakam || 35 ||

tataḥ siddhyaṃ tibhūtānisadevāsuramānuṣāḥ |
grahavetālaḍākinyo duṣṭāśvānyesahasraśaḥ || 36 ||

pralayaṃyāṃtite sarvamaitrī māryāāṃtimānavāḥ svacchaṃdasya mayākhyātaṃpūjanaṃdiśisaṃsthitam || 37 ||

ihalokeṣare svargarājyaṃ caivalabhennaraḥ || 38 ||

yathaivapūjyateśaṃ bhurmatraiḥ paṃcabhireva ca |
munayastaspravakṣyāmi svargamokṣa karaṃ param || 39||

tannorudraḥ pracodayāt || 40 ||

oṃnamaḥśivāya aṣṭātriṃśemahādevaḥ paṃcabrahmāṃtarāśritaḥ |
bhuktimu ktikaraḥ pūjyaitisamyakprakīrtyate || 1 ||

caturthastu mahāvakaṃ gāyaśyaitannigadyate |
paṃcamaṃtutathāvakaṃśṛṇudhvamekacitayā || 41 ||

prajāpatirbrahmāsvayaṃbhuvebrahmaṇe namaḥ || 42 ||

sadyādibhiryajaṃtīhayeśivaṃ sarvakāraṇam |
temokṣamāpnuvaṃtīhamuna yomokṣakāṃkṣiṇaḥ ||43||

īśānādikrameṇaivaīśānamarcayaṃtiye |
tesvargasya hibhoktāromartyalokasukhasyaca || 44 ||

pūrvava kraṃmayākhyātamūrdhvavakraṃhitāpasāḥ |
tasmātsaṃpūjayennityamekenaivayatātmavān ||45||

liṃgevāsthaṃḍileśaṃbhuṃsarvakāmaphalā ptaye || 46 ||


||9||

vyāsauvāca ||

bhuktimuktikarodevaḥśaṃkaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
vināmaṃtrācitaḥśaṃbhuḥ kiṃpunamaitrasaṃyutaḥ || 1 ||

jñānaṃmayāmuniśreṣṭhakimetatsakalaṃ jagat |
jñānavānmohitodaivānmohomāṃsamupāśritaḥ || 2 ||

lokaḥ suptovi buddhastukathaṃnidrāmupeyivān || 3 ||

ādyenasvasyajānātimṛtyusenāpatiṣyati |
kasmānnayatate dharme mokṣaṃpratimahāmune || 4 ||


sanatkumārauvāca ||

paṃcabhūtātmakaṃ sarvajagatsthāvarajaṃgamam |
svakarmavaśagāḥsarveprāṇinaḥsaṃbhavatyataḥ ||6||

jñānenahīnāḥ paśu bhiśvatulyāḥsvakarmabaddhāḥ prabhavaṃtinityam ||7||

nayatkṛtenāpiyathātathāpibhāvastadājñānavatāṃnarāṇām |
prayāṃtisamya gvidhināsvakarmacaturvidhābhūtagaṇānnṛloke || 8 ||

bhavaṃtiyāṃtīhapunarvināśaṃ prabuddhyamānānahicodvijaṃ |
susaṃcaraṃtoba huduḥ khakhinnāḥkṛtvehapāpaṃnirayeprayāṃti ||9||

te sthāvaratvaṃ nirayāvatīrṇāstiryaktvamicchaṃtipunaḥsamastam |
paścāttathāmānuṣatāṃ kuyoniṃ stavaṃ na kurvatiyadāsvadharmaiḥ || 10 ||

śive gurauvā pihimuktihetoḥ svakarmabaddhāstu bhavenimagnāḥ |
yathājale budruvatkṣaṇāddhi || 11 ||

devyuvāca ||

paṃcabrahmavidhānaṃtumānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthataḥ |

īśvarauvāca||

īśā nasyasubhaktānāmihāmutraphalapradam ||13||

kapilāpaṃcagavyenasnātvājatvāṣṭakaṃnaraḥ |
juhuyādaṣṭasāhasraṃtilairmucyetapāta kaiḥ || 14 ||

tayodakena saṃpūryakuṃbhaṃjamvāśataṃśatam |
dinedine tu saṃstrāyātsa lakṣmyāputravānbhavet || 15 ||

devasya dakṣiṇāmū terjapedāstamanādraveḥ |
aṣṭāveva sahasraṃ tuhutvāsarvopapātakaiḥ || 16 ||

guggulasyaguṭīḥ kṛtvāpaṃcāmṛtenamiśritāḥ |
hutvā nauvaśyatāṃ yāṃtiyakṣarākṣasamānuṣāḥ || 17 ||

saṃgrāmejayamāpnotinakleśairabhibhūyate |
upasargetrirātraṃtu devasya dakṣiṇe sthitaḥ || 18||

jaghvācāṣṭasahasraṃtu juhuyātsamidhastataḥ |
dadhimadhughṛtāktāstuhutvāśāṃtirbhavediha || 19 ||

kapilāyāḥ sa vatsāyādadhyājyamadhusaṃyutam |
sahasramodanaṃ hutvāsuputrānīpsitān labhet ||20||

guggulaṃ ghṛtasaṃmiśraṃ hutvācāṣṭasahasrakaṃ |
sarve vaśyābhavaṃtīhadutvāśatasahasrakam || 21 ||

padmādīninidhānāniprāpnotimucyatetathā |
hatyayābrāhmaṇasyehaśivasā yujyatāmiyāt || 22 ||

śikṣāhārobhimaṃtryāpoyaḥ śivāya pradāpayet |
bilvaṃvābilvapatraṃvāsośvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 23 ||

paṃcagavyāgniṣṭomasya tīrthādbhiḥ sarvajātikam |
sakuśaiḥ sarvagaṃdhaiśrvasarvābhayaphalaṃ labhet || 24 ||

sudhanīśatahomena satilena ghṛtenahi |
vrīhibhistukuletasyanadaridrobhijāyate || 25 ||

jabhvācāṣṭaśataṃ vidvānvrajetkṣemī sadāpathi |
durgebhayākulenadyāṃnabhayaṃvidyatekvacit || 26 ||

ādityābhimukhobhūtvā ūrdhvabāhuḥ śivārcakaḥ |

salilasyaḥ saptadinānya bhakṣaṇātsaviśuddhyati |
surāpānāccharunmūtrātsteyātsvaguru dāragāt || 28 ||

mucyatepātakātsadyonirāhārojiteṃdriyaḥ |
rājñāṃpratigṛhītvātuśūdrānnaṃ bahudhātyadan || 29 ||

trisaṃdhyaṃ cajapennityaṃmāsenaikena śudhyati |
gajasiṃhavarāheṣuśatrurājakuleṣuvā || 30 ||

nabhayaṃvidyatetasya manasāyāṃjapedaya |
manasāyojapenmaṃtraṃīśānaṃdhyānamāsthitaḥ || 31 ||

dehenānenasaṃsiddhaḥ prayātiparamaṃ padaṃ |
kṛtvā suduṣkṛtaṃdevi maṃtreṇānenamāṃ smaret || 32||

vimuktaḥpātakaiḥ sarvairmamaloke mahīyate || 33 ||


śaṃkarauvāca ||

tatpuruṣasyavakṣyāmi phalaṃtacchṛṇusutrate |
bilvoduṃbara yorhatvāsamicchatasahasrakaṃ || 1 ||

brahmahatyāvi nirmuktomaṃtreṇānena suvrate |
gṛhītvādakṣiṇe karṇe ajāṃrātrau sunirbhayaḥ || 2 ||

liṃgasyadakṣiṇāmūrttermāsamekaṃtu yakṣiṇī |

karavīrasyapuṣpāṇighṛtayuktānijuṅkataḥ |
nāmnāstrīvaśamāyātisamarā trāttadagrataḥ || 4 ||

karṇikārasyapuṣpāṇāṃdevagaṃdharvayoṣitaḥ |
samāgamyadadaṃtīṣṭaṃmanasovatsarārdhataḥ || 5||

pratidinaṃjapeya stuliṃgamāśrityayatnataḥ |
dehenānena medhāvīdevimāṃpratipadyate ||6||

ādityābhimukhobhūtvājapanpāpaiḥ pramucyate |
salava ṇāṃstilāndutvāvaśamānayateripūn || 7 ||

saghṛtenayaśaḥ kāmīsadūrva yāyurevaca |
mārjanenāmayānhaṃtisarṣapairakṣaṇaṃsadā || 8 ||

liṃgasyadakṣiṇāmūrteḥsakāmānīpsitāṃlabhet || 9 ||

satataṃjapateyovaivarṣavarṣārdhamevaca |
kṣiprasiddhiṃ dadatyāśusvayamāgatya kanyakā ||10||

devasyadakṣiṇāmūrterjapeddā satataṃsudhīḥ |
vatsarādahamevāsau siddhiyacchāmivarṇini || 11||

paṃcagavyakṛtā hārojapanasvamanugacchati |
mitāśīyojapennityaṃ mokṣaṃyātinasaṃśayaḥ || 12 ||

parvaṇyevajapedyastu sarvakratuphalaṃlabhet |
sarve satvāvaśamīyuḥ śmaśānejapataḥ śubhe || 13 ||

śāṃtikāmolabhedutvāśāṃtiṃtusitasarṣapān |
raktapuṣpairvaśekāryekṛṣṇapu ppaistu māraṇe || 14||

vidveṣepītapuṣpāṇidhūmrāṇyuccāṭane tathā |
etadeva japetsarva maṃtraṃ sarvārthasādhakam || 15 ||



śaṃkarauvāca

natithirnacanakṣatranopavāsovidhīyate |
aghora smaraṇāddevi sarvapāpakṣayobhavet || 1 ||

trātvādaupaṃca gavyenaśivamabhyarcyamaṃtrataḥ |
japedayutamekaṃtu sahasraṃ juhuyāddhṛtam || 2||

puraścaraṇametattutrailokyaṃvaśamānayet |
maṃḍūkasya vasāṃgṛtyacaturdaśyāmathāṣṭamīm || 3 ||

devasyadakṣiṇesthitvājatvācāṣṭasahasrakam |
vartikāṃpadmasūtrasyakapālarutakajja lam ||4||

tenaivāṃjitanetrastuadṛśyojāyatenaraḥ |
kusuṃbhatailamathavāpūrveṇavidhinākṛtam ||5 ||

tenaivāṃjitane trastu adṛśyobhavatikṣaṇāt || 6 ||

brahmedra viṣṇulokāṃstudehenānenaga cchati |

śrautāṃjanaṃsamādāyaprakṣapātremusamabhiḥ |
pidhāyahavanaṃkuryātkṛṣṇayā vāsitaistilaiḥ || 8 ||

ayutaṃjitane trastu sodṛśyaḥ pracarenmahīṃ |
tathāgorocanāṃgṛtya sahasreṇābhimaṃtritaṃ || 9 ||

śivasyaivahi nirmālyaṃsahasreṇābhimaṃtritam |
dhūpanaṃtenarakṣāṃsibhūtavetālasugrahāḥ || 10 ||

vināyakāstuḍākinyojvarāpasmārasaṃbhavāḥ |

śmaśānamadhyamāsthāyaśavaṃ gṛtyarujākṣatam |
susaṃsnāpyagaṃdhatoyaiḥ sthāpayenmaṃḍalebudhaḥ || 12||

gaṃdhapuṣpairalaṃkṛtya chādayecchuklavāsasā |
śavehastaṃ samādhāyakṛṣṇāṃcaturdaśīmanu || 13 ||

ka pilānavanītena prakṣedaṣṭaśataṃsudhīḥ |
evaṃ tajjapatovegātsamuttiṣṭhatinirbhayaḥ || 14 ||

bravītikiṃdadāmītisiddhosmītya hamevate |
ityuktaśvepsitānkāmāntravītitatrasādhakaḥ || 15 ||

sadatvāpatate bhūmauniśveṣṭojāyatekṣaṇāt |
tālakaṃkaravīra syarasenaparisrakṣayet ||16||

kuryātkṛṣṇa caturdaśyāṃjapannaṣṭasahasrakam |
liṃgasya dakṣiṇesthitvātenaivāṃgaṃsamālabhet ||17||

adṛśyojāyatesagroyatheṣṭaṃgacchatītiha |
devasya dakṣiṇāmūrtausthitvālakṣaṃjapedyadi || 18 ||

daśāṃśenatathāho maṃbilvaiḥ praku rutetuyaḥ |
trividhājāyatesiddhirviṣadṛṣṭyābhavaṃtihi || 19 ||

jvarādīnāmayānsarvānapi kīṭapataṃgakān |
satataṃhijapedyastu tannāstiyannasādhayet || 20 ||

pātālasvargapuṭakānde henānena gacchati |
brahmacaryeṇavaimāsaṃpaṃcagavyakṛtāśanaḥ || 21 ||

jabhvātu sarvapāpaistumucyate brahmahatyayā |
ekaliṃgaṃsamāśrityajapenmūlaphalāśanaḥ || 22 ||

bhikṣāhāropiṣaṇmāsātpratyakṣaṃ māṃsapaśyati |

śatajāpyenadeveśibhasmasādyatinityaśaḥ |
ityagho rakalpaḥ||śṭaṇutvaṃvāmadevasyakathayiṣyāmi suvrate || 24 ||

liṃgasya dakṣiṇāmūrterjapedaṣṭasahasrakam |
paṃcagavyenacasnātvājuhuyādayutaṃpunaḥ || 25||

śvetapuṣpaiḥ suraktairvāpītaiścaivahiśobhanaiḥ |

trirātraṃ samupopyāthabhāsasyāsthisumaṃtrayet |
bāhaubadhvātvadṛśyatvaṃ kurute bhūtarākṣasān || 27 ||

mānuṣādīnsukravyādānvaśitvaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
kapilāgāṃmayenaivakṛtvāputtalikāṃdhruvam || 28 ||

maṃtreṇānena saṃsthāpyaghṛtasyaivāhutīhunet |
nāmnāmaṣṭausahasrāṇi yakṣiṇīrākṣasītathā || 29 ||

surabhipuṣpāṇi sugaṃdhitānyaṣṭausahasrāṇidevasyoparinidhāpayet|| sarvānkāmānavāpnoti || 30 ||

yavavrīhihomena dhanapatirbhavati |
śatasahasraṃ jatvātha liṃgamūrdhani niḥkṣipet || 31 ||

tataḥ śrīḥ svayamāgatya sarvānkāmāndadāti |
nāgapuṣpapunnāgāśokapāṭalākarṇikārādīnikathitāni ||32||

athasargasyaghṛtamadhumiśrāhutisahasraṃjuhuyāt |
arthalābhobhavati ||33||

sūtreṇarakṣāṃ karotisarṣapaiḥ sarvagrahe bhyomuṃcāpayati || 34 ||

laghubhirdiśāṃvaṃdhanaṃkaroti |
udakenavaśamānayati || 35 ||

sarvapuṣpābhiṣekaṃrutvātmānamabhiṣiṃcedalakṣmīṃnāśayati || 36 ||

tagaramallikākuṃdāśokasaugaṃdhikābhiṣekena śrīrbhavati |
gataṃrājyaṃ samānayati || 37||

pratipadārabhya sarvaśuklapuṣpāṇāṃpratidinamaṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt |
paurṇamāsīyāvattatobhavānīśvaraḥ || 38 ||

itivāmadevakalpaḥ |
īśvarauvāca || atha sadyojātenamaṃtreṇa kṛṣṇayorekatareṇa maṃtreṇatrirātropoṣito bhūtvā'ṣṭasahasraṃjapet || 43 ||

ātmārthajuhuyādyadicchatitalla bhet |
puṣpāṣṭasahasraṃjuhuyānmadhughṛtāktaṃyakṣarākṣasāpsarasaḥ striya āgacchaṃti ||44||

rasaramāyanasiddhīḥ prayacchaṃti |
śiva sya mahatīṃ pūjāṃrutvāvyapagatakāmakrodhalobhamohobhikṣābhujovāmaunīśuklavāsājiteṃdriyaḥ || 45 ||

guggulaguṭikābhi raṣṭasahasraṃjuhuyādaṣṭaguṇamaiśvaryabhavati || 46 ||

athadevasya dakṣiṇāmūrtauśatrunāmnājapet |
śatrurnabhavati || 47||

athamadhu payasāṣṭasahasraṃjuhuyādrājyacyutorājyaṃ labheta || 48 ||

athabilvākaduṃbarāśvatthānāṃsamidaṣṭasahakhaṃjuhuyāt |
pṛthvīrājyaṃ prāpnoti || 49 ||

athasahasramabhimaṃtryātmano'vikalobhavati || 50 ||

athavastramālyābharaṇādīnabhimaṃtrya saubhāgyaṃbha vati || 51 ||

athavaṭādhaḥsthitvāśatasahasraṃjapetyakṣiṇīsidhyati |
aṃtarjalepaṃcasahasraṃjapet brahmahatyādipāpairvimuktobha vati ||52||

mahāpāpānipāpānitathopapātakānitu |
lakṣārddhārddhatadarddhena naśyaṃtiśataśastathā || 53 ||

ekaliṃgejapelakṣaṃ devaḥpratyakṣatāmiyāt |
niṣkāmopijapedevaṃ jñānīsarvajñatāṃtrajet || 54 ||

itisadyojātakalpaḥ |
svayaṃbhūbāṇaliṃgebājyo tirliṃgepisuvrate | pūrva sevāṃprakurvīta bhaktyātadgatamānasaḥ || 55 ||

brahmacaryaratomaunī bhūmiśāyījiteṃdriyaḥ || 56 ||

asādhakodeśikovāpipūrva sevāṃsamārabhet |
yogābhyāsenavāyogījapadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ ||57||

pradakṣiṇānamaskāraiḥ kriyā yogenaśaṃkaraḥ |
bhaktistukāraṇaṃtatrabhaktigrātyomaheśvaraḥ || 58 ||

sarvāśramāṇāṃvarṇānāṃbālavṛddha striyāmapi |

sitdhyate hikimāśvaryaṃ prasādācchaṃkarasyavai |

japenmaṃtraṃtuyomaṃtrīsarvaṃsidhyatinānyathā |

evaṃsaṃbhāvayanyogīsi ḍyatevatsarārddhataḥ ||62||

śivaikaniṣṭhastvanuyogadakṣaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣusadāsthitosmi |
dhyāyanjapanvāmanasāyatātmāso'nenade henabhavecchivohi ||63||

maṃtrajāṃ siddhivakṣye haṃtacchṛṇuṣvavarānane |
nadīnadasa mudrevāparvatepugṛheṣuca || 64 ||

paścimābhimu khaṃyatraliṃgaṃtiṣṭhatibhāmini |
kapilāgomayenaiva upalipyaprayatnataḥ ||65||

punastatpaṃcagavyena snātvāsadyena maṃtrayet |
sthā payeddāmadevenadiśāsuvidiśāsuca || 66 ||

paṃcāmṛtādibhistāyaiḥ snātvātatpuruṣeṇavai |
gaṃdhapuṣpādikaṃ sarvaṃ vastrālaṃkārabhū paṇam || 67||

aghoreṇasamaṃrutvāīśānaṃmūrdhnivinyaset |
īśānenārcayennityaṃsiddhikāmotyataṃdritaḥ || 68 ||

svayaṃbhūvā ṇaliṃgaṃvādevāsurapratiṣṭhitam |
sarvalakṣaṇahīnaṃcaraphuṭitaṃśīrṇamevaca || 69 ||

siddhidaṃtadvijānīyājjīrṇoddhāraṃna kāraye t |
īśānaṃyojapennityaṃliṃgamāśrityayatnataḥ || 70 ||

tasyasiddhirnasaṃdehaḥ paṇmāsairvatsareṇavā |

samābhijāyatedṛṣṭirīśānenanasaṃśayaḥ |
abhimaṃtryāṣṭasāhasraiḥ kṣipeddījānyaśeṣataḥ || 72 ||

kṣe tre syātsasya nippattirnopadravabhayaṃ bhavet |

aupasargikadopāṇāṃnabhayaṃ vidyatekvacit |
parvatāgresa mārutyajapenmaṃtramataṃdritaḥ || 74 ||

dehenānenadeveśaṃ varadaṃ paśyatevaraṃ |
sarvapāpairvimuktaḥsyādyojapelakṣamādṛtaḥ || 75 ||

annaṃbhuṃjan japedyastu tejasvījāyatesa dā |
pratyahaṃśatamekaṃtujapetsiddhimavāpnuyāt || 76 ||

kubhuktasyakupānasyadivāsvapnasya mehataḥ |
asthānesaṃmukhasyāpiddija godevayoṣitāṃ || 77 ||

kuvātātkupathādeva īśānaṃsatataṃsmaret |
evaṃyovartatenityaṃśivapūjārcane rataḥ || 78 ||

vimuktaḥsa rvapāpebhyaḥ śivaloke mahīyate |
īśvara uvāca || paṃcabrahmavidhānaṃtuyathāvatkathitaṃmayā || 79 ||

sāṃprataṃhisubhaktānāṃtathāśu ddhatapasvinām |
yepunaḥśuddhakarmāṇaḥsuśuddhatapasānvitāḥ || 80 ||

samarcayaṃtimāṃnityaṃvedyāstepiyathātdyaham |
nātaptatapa soyāṃtiśivalokamanāmayam || 81 ||

tapasādivimodatepratyakṣaṃdevatāgaṇāḥ |
ṛṣayomunayaścaivasatyaṃjānīhi suṃdari || 82 ||

sudurdharaṃdurārādhyaṃsuduraṃ duratikramam |
tatsarvatapasāsādhyaṃta pohiduratikramam ||83||

tāpasyesaṃsthitobrahmānityaṃ viṣṇurahaṃtathā |
tvaṃ tathādeviyenāhaṃprāptobhartāsudurlabhaḥ || 84 ||

yenayenahibhāvenasthitvāyatkriyatetapaḥ |
tattatsaṃprāpya teṃdeviihalokenasaṃśayaḥ || 85 ||


tāmasaṃdānavānāṃhirakṣasāṃkrūrakarmaṇām |
nirjaṃdehaṃ susaṃpīḍyahimātapa suduḥsahaiḥ || 87||

tattapastāmasaṃproktaṃmano bhipretasādhanam |
japaṃdhyānaṃtudevānāmarcanaṃ bhaktitaḥ śubhe || 88 ||

sātvikaṃtadvinirdiṣṭamaśeṣaphalasādhanam |
ihalokepa recaivakramānmokṣaṃvrajedataḥ || 89 ||

uttamaṃsātvikaṃvidyātdharme buddhisuniścalā |
strānaṃpūjājapohomaḥ śuddhaśauca mahiṃsatā ||90||

vratopavāsacaryācamauna miṃdriyanigrahaḥ |
dhīvidyāsatyamakrodhodānaṃkṣāṃtirdamodayā || 91 ||

kṛcchracāṃdrāyaṇaṃyajñaḥ sutīrthānyāśramāṇica || 92 ||

dharmasthānāni caitāni sukhadānimanīṣiṇām |
saṃkrāṃtiviṣuvayogonādamukrāṃtiyuṃjatām ||93||

dhyānaṃbiṃdulavaṃ jyotiru nmanībhāvadhāraṇā |
recakaḥ pūrakaḥ kuṃbhaḥ prāṇāyāmastrikaḥsmṛtaḥ ||94||

nāḍīsaṃcaravijñānaṃpratyāhāranirodhanam |
turīyaṃtadvadho buddhiraṇimādyaṣṭasaṃyutam || 95 ||

kāṣṭhāvasthāmṭatāvasthāharitācetikīrtitā |

nārīśayyāsanaṃyānaṃvatraṃ dhūpavilepanaṃ |
tāṃbūla bhakṣaṇaṃpaṃcarājyaiśvaryavibhūtayaḥ || 97 ||

hemabhāraṃtathātāmragrahāśvaratna dhenavaḥ |
pāṃḍityaṃ vedaśāstrāṇi gītanṛtyavibhūṣaṇam ||98||

śaṃkhavīṇāmṛdaṃgāśvagajeṃdrachatracāmaram |
bhogarūpāṇicaitāni ebhīraktonuyujyate || 99||

ādarśavanmunisnehastilavatatrapīḍyate |

vyapadiśyamaharṣervainimittaṃśītajaṃtadā |
svāgatasvāgataṃcāstutamṭa ṣimunisattamaṃ || 85 ||

sopagūtyabhujābhyāṃtaṃbhagavaṃtamabhāṣata |

kāmena mohitācāhaṃstrīṇāṃ puruṣatodhṛtiḥ || 87 ||

brahmanna kāmakārostitāṃ bhajaṃtīṃbha jasvamāṃ |
upagūhacamāṃviprasamāgacchamayāsaha || 88 ||

etaddhitava dharmātmaṃstapa saḥ pūjyatephalaṃ || 89 ||

prabhutvaṃtaṃva sarvatrayaccānyadapi paśyasi || 9 ||

sadyacedaṃvanaṃyaccamamacaivanasaṃ śayaḥ |

ramaṇīyevanāddeśeramatvasahito mayā |


yathāpuruṣasaṃsargaḥ parametaddhinaḥ phalaṃ |
ātmacchaṃdenavartatenāryomanmathunoditāḥ || 94 ||

nacadatyaṃtigacchaṃtyaḥ sutamairapipāṃśu bhiḥ |
aṣṭāvakrauvāca || paradārānahaṃbhadrenagaccheyaṃ kathaṃcana || 95 ||



khyuvāca || nānilognirnavaruṇonacāpitridaśādvija || 98 ||


tathāśatasahasreṣuyaṭikācitpativratā |
naitājā naṃtipitaraṃnakulaṃnacamātaraṃ || 100 ||

sanatkumārauvāca || tataḥsamunirekāgrastāṃstriyaṃpratyuvāca || 2 ||

|| 6 ||


athaciṃtayatastasyatamarthajñātumicchataḥ ||8||

divyaṃ cavividhaṃ cakresopahāraṃ munestadā || 15||

tasya buddhiriyaṃkiṃnu mohastatvamidaṃbhavet |

tatravaiśayanedivyetasya tasyāśvakalpite |

inaṃnadharmacchalamastite |

sanatkumārauvāca ||

punaranyattuvakṣyāmi aruṃdhatyāyathāva caḥ |

tathātithigṛhasthānāṃnṛpādīnāṃdvijottamāḥ |

bhavaṃti sukhinonityaṃdevāiṃdrapurogamāḥ |
ihāmirdharmakāmānāṃparatracaśubhāgatiḥ ||4||

ataḥ samīkṣitosmābhiḥ satyamastīhayoṣi tām |

śrūyaṃteyoṣitaḥ kāści tsattvadharmesamāsthitāḥ ||6||

dvijāstathāsvakarmasthāgṛhiṇogṛhakarmaṇi |
rājānothaviśaśvāpiprajāpālanatatparāḥ ||7||

asyāḥ sāmyenatadrūpaṃnacakāra pativratā || 8 ||

dhanyātvamasikalyāṇiyātvamekāpativratā ||9||

natarūpa mahaṃkartuṃ śaktācānyākariṣyati |
tasmāttvayāḥ striyaḥkā śvitprekṣiṣyaṃtisubhāvataḥ || 10 ||

karaṃspṛṣṭavoḍhakāletu bharturdvijāgnisaṃnidhau |

evaṃyāsābhimaṃtavyāguṇairyuktāpativratā |
procyatestrībhiranyābhirguṇīvettiyatoguṇān || 12 ||

gacchāmotovayaṃpraṣṭuṃvataṃ strīṇāṃpativratām |
ityuktvāteyayurdevāstrayaḥ sūryedravanhayaḥ || 13 ||

tatodadṛśurāyāṃta svadhāmnoghṛtakuṃbhakām |

dṛṣṭvātāṃharṣanastasthuragretasyāstuvartmani |
tataḥsātānmudādṛṣṭvājñātvādevānaruṃdhatī || 15||

prāhatānmunikṛtyaṃyattadduvaṃtudivaukasaḥ || 16||

praṣṭuṃvayaṃsamāyātāḥkaṃcitpraśnaṃhinovada || 17 ||

sācatānāhamedhāgniviśramadhvakṣaṇādanu |
kathayi ṣyāmitatsarvapraśnaṃceṣṭitamātmanaḥ || 18 ||

yāvadāyāmisaṃgṛtyajalapūrṇamimaṃghaṭam |
tetāmūcurghaṭaṃtetujalenāśuabī bharan || 19 ||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

vivāhaṃkārayetpaśvādvidhivadvijapuṃgavaiḥ |
tataḥ saṃpūjayedviprānsapatnīkānmahāmune || 1 ||

bhakṣyairbhojyairgadhamālyairvastrairābharaṇairdvijaḥ |
tataḥ kṣamāpayedviprānimaṃmaṃtramudāharet || 2 ||

yathāmadreśaduhitāyamātmānuvatīvarān |
tathāhaṃprāpnuyadeivavarāntsarvānmanoramān || 3 ||

ityuktvāpraṇipatyāthadvijeṃdrāstānvisarjayet || 4 ||


devyuvāca ||

bhagavaṃstvatprasādena jñātaṃmesakalaṃmatam |
yathārcanaṃ tutedevayaimaitraiśvayathāvidhi || 1 ||

adyāpisaṃśayotyekaḥ kālacakraṃpratiprabho |
mṛtyucinhaṃ yathādeva kiṃpramāṇaṃtathāyuṣaḥ || 2 ||

tathākathayamenāthayaya haṃtavavallabhā |
īśvarauvāca || satyaṃte kathayiṣyāmiyenakālaḥprabudhyate || 3 ||

ahaḥpakṣaṃtathāmāsammṛtuṃ cāyanavatsaram |
sthūlasūkṣmagataiśvinhairbahiraṃtargataistathā || 4 ||

akasmātpāṃḍuraṃdehamūrddharāgaṃsamaṃtataḥ || 5 ||

mukaṃkarṇautathāca sujivhārastaṃbhoyadābhavet |
tadāmṭatyuṃvijānīyātṣaṇmāsābhyaṃtaraṃpriye || 6 ||

rauravānugataṃbhadredhvaniṃnākarṇayehutam |
ṣaṇmāsābhyaṃtaraṃmmṛtyurjñātavyaḥ kālavedibhiḥ || 7 ||

ravisomāgnisaṃyogādyadodyotanapaśyati |

vāmahastoyadā devisaptāhaṃsyaṃdatepriye |
ulmīlayaṃtigātrāṇitālukaṃśuṣyateyadā || 9 ||

jīvitaṃtutadātasyamāsamekaṃnasaṃśayaḥ |
nāsātusravateyasyatridoṣepakṣajīvitam || 10 ||

vakrakaṃṭhaśvaśuṣyetaṣaṇmāsatigātāyuṣaḥ |
sthūlājivhā bhaveyasyadvijāḥ kliyaṃtibhāmini || 11 ||

ṣaṇmāsājjāyatemṛtyuścinhaistairupalakṣayet |
aṃbutailaghṛtasthaṃtudarpaṇevaravarṇini || 12 ||

paśyatenayadātmānaṃvikṛtaṃsthūlameva ca |
ṣaṇmāsāyuḥ savijñeyaḥ kālacakraṃ vijānatā || 13 ||

śirohīnāṃ yadāchāyāṃsvakīyāmupalakṣayet |
athavāchāyayāhīnaṃ māsamekaṃnajīvati || 14 ||

āṃgikāḥ kathitābhadrebātyasthāṃcchṛṇusāṃpratam |
raśmihīnaṃyadādevi somārkamaṃḍaladvayam || 15 ||

dṛśyate paṭalākāraṃmāsārddhenavipadyate |
aruṃdhatīmahāyānamiṃdulāṃchanavarjitam || 16 ||

sopijīvativarṣārddhatathāvādṛṣṭatārakaḥ |
meghahīnaṃyadādevividyudbhirjvalinaṃnabhaḥ || 17||

dṛṣṭegṛhecadiṅamohaḥṣaṇmāsājjāyatedhruvam |
rātrau dhanuryadāpaśyenmadhyānhe colka pātanam || 18||

veṣṭya gṛdhrakākaiśvaṣaṇmāsāyurnasaṃśayaḥ |
ṛṣayaḥsvargapaṃthāśvanaivadṛśyetacāṃbare || 19 ||

ṣaṇmāsāyurvijānīyātpuruṣaiḥ kālavedibhiḥ |
akasmādrāhuṇāgrastaṃsūryavāsomamevaca ||20||

dikcakraṃbhbhrāṃtavatpaśyetṣaṇmāsāntriyatesphuṭam |
makṣikairnīlavarṇābhaira kasmādveṣṭyatepumān || 21 ||

jīvatemāsamekaṃtu jñātavyaṃparamārthataḥ |
gṛdhaḥkākaḥ kapotaśvaśiraścākramya tiṣṭhati || 22 ||

śīdhaṃtukhiyatejaṃturmāsaikena na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ cāriṣṭa bhedaṃtubātyasthaṃ samudāhṛtam || 23 ||

āyudhānāṃ hitārthāyasaṃkṣepeṇavadāmyaham |
hastayorubhayordevi yathākālaṃvijānate. ||24||

vāmadakṣiṇayorbhadre pratyakṣaṃtevadāmyaham |
vāmaṃśuklaṃ vijānīyādakṣiṇaṃ kṛṣṇasaṃjñitam || 25 ||

dakṣiṇaṃ sūryamityā durvāmacaṃdraudāhṛtaḥ |
evaṃ pakṣausthitauddautusamāsātsurasuṃdari || 26 ||

śucirbhūtvāsmaredevaṃ susrātaḥ saṃyateṃdriyaḥ |
hastaupra kṣālya kṣīreṇālakta ke na tumardayet || 27 ||

gaṃdhaiḥpuṣpaiḥkaraurutvāmmṛgayecca śubhāśubham |
kaniṣṭhāmāditaḥ kṛtvāyāvadaṃguṣṭhakaṃpri ye || 28 ||

parvatrayakrameṇaivahastayorubhayorapi |
pratipadādivinyasyatithipratipadāditaḥ || 29 ||

saṃpuṭākārahastau tu pūrvā dimukhasaṃsthitaḥ |
smarennavārṇakaṃmaṃtryāvadaṣṭottaraṃśatam || 30 ||

nirīkṣayettatohastausarvaparvaṇi yatnataḥ |
tasminparvaṇita dekhādṛśyatebhṛṃgasannibhā || 31 ||

tattithaummṛtyurvijñeyaḥ kṛṣṇeśukletathāpriye |
niśvitaṃ maraṇaṃ tasya kālacakrasya kīrtitam ||32||

adhunānādajaṃvakṣyesaṃkṣepātkālalakṣaṇam |
gamāgamaṃviditvātukarmakuryācchṛṇupriye ||33||

ātmavijñānaṃsuśro ṇicāraṃ jñātvātu tatvataḥ |
kṣaṇaṃ truṭirlavacaivanimepaṃ kāṣṭhakālikam ||34||

muhūrtakatvahorātra pakṣamāsatuvatsaram |
abdayugaṃ tathākalpaṃmahākalpaṃtathaivaca || 35 ||

evaṃ saṃharatekālaḥ paripāṭyāsadāśivaḥ |
vāmadakṣiṇamadhyetupayitrayamidaṃ smṛtam || 36 ||

vāmacāragatonādaḥ pramāṇaṃkathitaṃtava || 37 ||

bhūtaraṃdhadiśaścaivadhvajaśvavarava rṇini |
vāmacāragatonādaḥ pramāṇaṃ kālavedinaḥ || 38 ||

ṛturvikāra bhūtāścaguṇastatraivabhāmini |
pramāṇaṃdakṣiṇaṃproktaṃ jñātavyaṃ prāṇavedibhiḥ || 39 ||

bhūtasaṃkhyāṃyadāprāṇovahatecaiḍādayaḥ |
varṣatrayaṃtadātasya jīvitasyanasaṃśayaḥ || 40 ||

di nādaśapravāhenadvāvabdāni sajīvati |
paṃcadaśapravāhena abdamekaṃ gatāyuṣaḥ ||41||

viṃśaddinapravāhena ṣaṇmāsaṃlakṣayettathā |
paṃcaviṃśaddinānāṃtuvahatevāmanāḍikā || 42 ||

jīvitaṃtutadātasyamāsatrayagatāyuṣaḥ |
ṣaḍviṃśaddinamānenamāsadvayagatā yuṣaḥ || 43 ||

saptaviṃśaddinānāṃ tuvahatetvativiśragā |
māsamekaṃsamākhyātaṃjīvitaṃvāmagocare || 44 ||

savyaṃtaredinānyeva catvāra anupūrvaśaḥ || 45 ||

teṣāṃ pramāṇaṃvakṣyāmi adhunāhihitārthataḥ || 46 ||

ṣar̤adinānyāditaḥ kṛtvā saṃkhyāyāścayathāvidhi |
etadaṃtargataṃ kālaṃvāmaraṃdhretra kāśitam ||47||

ṣaḍdināniyadārūḍhaṃva rṣadvayaṃsajīvati |
māsānyaṣṭauvijānīyāddinānyaṣṭādaśānitu || 48 ||

prāṇaḥ sapta dinedevivarṣadvayaṃnasaṃśayaḥ |
saptamāsānvijānīyāddinaiḥṣaḍtirgatāyuṣaḥ || 49 ||

aṣṭaudinaprabhedena varṣadvayaṃsa jīvati |
māsāṃ ścatvārivijñeyaṃcaturviṃśaddināvadhi ||50||

navadināniyadāprāṇāvahaṃtitatrimāsakam |
māsadvayaṃcadvemā sedinādvādaśakī titāḥ || 51 ||

pūrvavatkathitāyetukālaṃteṣāṃnapūrvake |
avataradināyetutesamāsenakathyate || 52 ||

ekādaśapravāhenavarṣame kaṃnajīvati |
māsānavatathāproktādinā aṣṭadināvadhi ||53||

dvādaśāhepravāhenavarṣamekaṃ gatāyuṣam |
māsāntsatavijānī yāddinaṣaṭumudāhṛtam || 54 ||

yadācavahatenāḍītrayodaśadināvadhi |
saṃvatsaraṃ bhavettasyamāsāśvatvāri kīrtitāḥ || 55 ||

prāṇavāhoyadāvāmecaturdaśadināni || 56 ||

dvādaśāpravāhenadvimāsaṃjīvitaṃkhalu |
dvitīyaṃtucaturthena aṃtarāladaśāṃtare || 57 ||

tṛtīyaṃcasamāsenakālacakraṃvadāmyaham |
ṣoḍaśāhapravāhenanavamāsairgatāyuṣam || 58 ||

dinā aṣṭādaśatatra kathitaṃsādhakeśvara |
vāmacāraṃyadādevi aṣṭādaśadināvadhi || 59 ||

jīvitaṃaṣṭamāsaṃtudinādvādaśakīrtitāḥ |
caturviṃśaddināstatraniśvayenāvadhāraya || 60 ||

prāṇavāhoyadāvāmetrayoviṃśaddināvadhi |
māsāścatvārovijñeyāṣadinānitathottare || 61 ||

caturviṃśapravāhenamāsatrayaṃsa jīvati |
dināstatradaśāṣṭaucasaṃharatyavicārataḥ || 62 ||

avāṃtaradināyetusaṃkṣepātprakaṭīkṛtāḥ |
vāmacāraṃsamākhyātaṃdakṣiṇaṃśśṛṇusāṃpratam || 63 ||

aṣṭāviṃśapravāhenatithimānena jīvati |
pravāhenadaśāhena tatsaṃkhyenavipadyate || 64 ||

triṃśavāhapravāhenapaṃcāhenavinaśyati |
ekatriṃśadyadādevivahatecaniraṃtaram || 65 ||

dakṣiṇaṃkathitaṃprāṇaṃ madhyasthaṃ kathayāmite || 66 ||

ekabhāvagatovāyupravāho mukhamaṃḍale |
dhamamānaḥ pravāhetudina me kaṃsajīvati || 67 ||

cakrametatparā sorhikālacakramudāhṛtam |
etatte kathitaṃ devi kimanyacchrotumicchasi || 68 ||


devyuvāca ||

kathitaṃtutvayādeva kālajñānaṃyathārthataḥ |
kālasyavacanaṃbrūhiyathātatvena yoginaḥ || 1 ||

kālastu saṃnikṛṣṭohivartate sarva jaṃtuṣu |
yathācāsyanamṛtyuścavaṃ catekālamāgatam || 2 ||

tathākathayama devahitārthamihayoginaḥ |
śaṃkarauvāca || śṛṇudevipravakṣyāmiyattvayāpṛcchatetyaham || 3 ||

kathayāmisamāsenamānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthataḥ |

oṃnamaḥ śivāya mṛtyordurjayatā mukkā śabdabrahmavicakṣaṇaiḥ |
sajayyaitidevena munyandhyādhyāya ucyate || 1 ||

eteṣāṃhisamāyogaḥśarīraṃpāṃcabhautikaṃ |
ākāśastuparovyāpīsarveṣāṃ sarvagaḥsthitaḥ ||5||

ākāśetuvilīyaṃte saṃbhavaṃtipuna stataḥ |
viyogetusadaikasyasvaṃdhāmapratipedire || 6 ||

tatosyasthiratācāstisaṃnipātasya suṃdari |
jñāninopitathāyatnātta pomaṃtrabalāyadi || 7 ||

devyuvāca ||

śvetaṃnayannatyatighorarūpaḥ kālaḥ karālastridiveśanātha |
dagdhastvayātvaṃ punareva tuṣṭaḥ sto traiḥ stutvātvāṃ prakṛtiṃsalebhe || 8 ||

tvayāsacoktaḥkṛpayājanānāmadṛṣṭarūpaḥ pracariṣyasīti |
dṛṣṭaṃtvayātannihitaṃmayāhiprabho varāttepunarutthitaṃca || 9 ||

tadadyabhoḥ kālaihāstikiṃcidvihanyateyenavadasvatanme |
śaṃkarauvāca || nahanyatedevavaraistu daityaiḥ sa yakṣarakṣoragamānuṣaiśca || 10||

ye yoginodhyānaparāḥ sadehābhavaṃtiteghnaṃtisukhena kālaṃ || 11||

sanatkumārauvāca ||

etacchrutvā tribhuvanaguroḥ prāhagaurīvihasya satyatvaṃmeva dakathamasauhanyate yenakālaḥ |
śaṃbhustāmāhaśaśivadane yoginoye kṣayaṃtikālaṃ jātaṃsakalamana ghāstacchṛṇuṣvaikacittā || 12 ||

śaṃkarauvāca ||

paṃcabhūtātmakodehaḥ sadāyuktastu guṇaiḥ |
utpadyate varārohe tadvilīnairhipārthivaiḥ || 13 ||

ākāśājjāyatevāyurvāyostejaśvajāyate |
tejasobuvinirdiṣṭaṃtasmāddhipṛthivībhavet 34 pṛthivyādīni bhūtānigacchaṃtikramaśovaraṃ || dharāpaṃcaguṇāproktā āpaścaiva caturguṇāḥ || 15||

triguṇaṃ catathā tejovāyurdvigu ṇaevaca |
śabdakaguṇamākāśaṃ pṛthivyādiṣukīrtitāḥ ||16||

vijahātiguṇaṃ svaṃ svaṃ tadābhūtaṃvipadyate || 17 ||

yadāguṇaṃvigṛhṇāti prādurbhūtaṃ taducyate |
tasmādviyoginānityaṃsvakāyākāśajāguṇāḥ || 18||

nityaṃcityamāciṃtanīyāḥprayatnena devi kālajigīṣuṇā |
devyuvāca || kathaṃ hijīyate kāloyogibhirmaivadeśvara || 19 ||

dhyānenajāpyamātreṇa tatsarvaṃ kathayaprabho |
śaṃkarauvāca || śṭaṇudevipravakṣyāmiyogināṃ hitakāmyayā || 20 ||

paraṃjñānamakathyaṃ canadeyaṃyasya kasyacit |
śraddadhānāya dātavyaṃbhaktiyuktāyadhīmate || 21 ||

anāstikāyaśuddhāyadharmanityāya bhāmini |
sukhāsanethā śayyāyāṃyogaṃyuṃjīta yogavit || 22 ||

dīpaṃvināṃdhakāretuprajāsumetudhārayet |
tarjanyāpi hitau karṇaupīḍayitvāmuhūrtakaṃ || 23||

tasmātsaṃśrūyateśabdastuṃ vanhisamudbhavaḥ |
sadhmātobhuktamevaṃ hipacatyannaṃkṣaṇādapi || 24 ||

sarvarogaṃnihaṃtyāśujvarāyupadravānbahūn |
yaśvopalakṣayennityamekāṃteghaṭikādvayaṃ || 25||

jitvāmṭatyuṃyathākāmaṃsvecchayāparyaṭediha |
sarvataḥ sarvadarśīcasarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt || 26 ||

yathābhnadatenādaṃprāvṛṇnabhasi saṃsthitaṃ |

tataḥsayogibhirnityaṃsūkṣmaḥ sūkṣmatarobhavet |
etatte kathitaṃdeviśabdabrahmavidhikramaṃ || 28 ||

śabdabrahmaidaṃprāpya ye kecidanyakāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 29 ||

pratite muṣṭinā kāśaṃkaṃḍayaṃtikṣudhātuṣān |
jñātvāparamidaṃ brahmasukhadaṃmuktikāraṇaṃ || 30 ||

avācyamakṣaraṃcaiva sarvopādhivivarjitaṃ |
māṃhitāḥ kālapāśena mṛtyupāśavaśaṃgatāḥ || 31 ||

śabdabrahmanajānaṃtipāpinastekubuddhayaḥ |
tāvadbha matisaṃsāreyāvadbrahmana viṃdati || 32 ||

vidite tu pare tattvamucyatejanmabaṃdhanāt |
nidrālasyaṃmahāvighnaṃ jitvāśacaṃprayatnataḥ || 33 ||

sukhāsanesthitānityaṃśabdabrahmābhyasenniśi |
śatavṛddhaḥpumāṃlabdhvāyāvadāyuṣyamabhyaset || 34 ||

gṛtyuṃjayavapustaṃbha ārogyaṃcāyurvardhanaṃ |
pratyayodṛśyatevaddhe kiṃpunastaruṇe jane || 35||

nacoṃkārona maṃtropinaivabījanacākṣaraṃ |
anāhaṃtamanuccāryaśabdabrahmaparaṃśivaṃ || 36 ||

tasmācchabdānavaproktāḥ prāṇavidbhistulakṣitāḥ |
tānpravakṣyāmiyatnenanādasiddhimanukramāt || 37 ||

ghoṣaṃkāṃsyaṃ tathābhṛṃ gaṃghaṃṭāvīṇādivaṃśakam |
duṃdubhiḥśaṃkhaśabdaścana va maṃmeghagarjitam || 38 ||

navaśabdaṃ parityajya oṃ kāraṃtusamāśrayet |
dhyāya navaṃ sadāyogīpuṇyapāpairnalipyate || 39 ||

naśṛṇotiyadāśṛṇvan ṣaṇmāsecatadaṃvi ke |
mriyatebhyasyamānastaṃyogītiṣṭhe ddivāniśam || 40 ||

tasmādutpadyate śabdomṛtyu jitsaptabhirdinaiḥ |
prathamaṃnadateghoṣamātmaśuddhikaraṃ param || 41 ||

sarvavyā dhiharaṃ nādaṃ vaśyākarṣaṇamuttamam |
dvitīyaṃnadate kāṃsyaṃ staṃbhayetyāṇināṃgatim ||42||

viṣaṃbhūtagrahāntsarvānbaṃdhayennātra saṃśa yaḥ |
tṛtīyaṃnadateśṛṃgamabhicāreniyojayet || 43 ||

vidveṣoccāṭaneśatromaraṇecaprayojayet |
ghaṃṭānādaṃ caturthaṃtuvadatepara me śvaraḥ || 44 ||

ākarṣasarvadevānāṃ kiṃpunarmānuṣābhuvi |
yakṣagaṃdharva kanyāśvatasyāṣṭādatihi || 45||

yathepsitāṃ mahāsi dviyoginaḥkāmatopivā |
vīṇātupaṃcamonādaḥ śrūyateyogibhiḥ sadā || 46 ||

tasmādutpadyate devidūrāddarśanameva hi |

duṃdubhidhyāyamānastu jarāmṛtyuvivarjitaḥ |
śaṃkhaśabdenadeveśikārūpaṃprapadyate || 48 ||

yoginomeghanādenaviyatsaṃgamatobhavet |

devyuvāca || vāyostupadamāpnotiyogākāśasamudbhavāt || 50 ||

tanmesarvasamācakṣva prasannastvaṃya diprabho |
śaṃkarauvāca || purāmesarvamākhyātaṃ yogināṃ hitakāmyayā ||51||

kālaṃjigamiṣoḥsamyagvāyoliṃgaṃyathābhavet |
tenajñātvādinaṃyogīprāṇāyāma paristhitaḥ ||52||

sajayatyāgataṃ kālaṃmā sārddhenaivasuṃdari |
hṛtsthovāyuḥ sadāva nhedīpakaḥ sonupāvakaḥ || 53 ||

sabātyābhyaṃtaravyāpīvāyuḥ sarvagatomahān |

yenehanirjito vāyustena sarvamidaṃjagat |
dhāraṇāyāṃsadātiṣṭhejjarāmṛtyujighāṃsayā ||55||

lohakāroyathābhastrāmāpūryamukhatonyataḥ |
dhārayeddāyunākarmatadvadyogīsamabhyaset || 56 ||

devaḥ sahasrako netrapādaha stasahasrakaḥ |
khaṃyopisarvamāvṛtyasogretiṣṭhaddaśāṃgulam || 57 ||



śatamabdaṃta pastamvākuśāgrāpaḥ pibeddijaḥ |
tadāpnoti phalaṃ deviprāṇānāṃdhāraṇaikayā ||60||

yodvijaḥ kalyamutthāyaprā ṇāyāmaikamācaret |
sarvapāpaṃnihatyāśubrahmalokaṃsagacchati || 61 ||

jarāṃmṛtyuṃ vinirjityavāyugaḥkhecarītisaḥ ||62||

siddhasya bhavaterūpaṃ kāṃtirmedhāparākramaḥ |
śauryaṃvāyusamogatyāsaukhyaṃ ślāghyaṃsurā dibhiḥ || 63 ||

etatkathitamaśeṣaṃvāyoḥ siddhiyadādbhutaṃ yogī |
yattejasopilabhatetattevakṣyāmideveśi || 64 ||

sthitvāsu khāsane sausudhvatijanavacanahīne tu |
śiśiraviyuktyātejaḥprakāśayenmadhya mede || 65||

vanhigataṃ bhūmadhye prakāśateyastvataṃ dritoyogī |
tāraṃ paśyeyāyenmuhūrtārddhatamekabhāvopi || 66||

tatastutamasidhyānīpaśyate jyotiraiśvaram |
śvetaṃraktaṃtathāpī taṃkṛṣṇamiṃdrāyudhaprabham ||67||

bhruvormadhye lalāṭasthaṃbālārkasamatejasaṃ |
taṃviditvātukāmāṃgīkrīḍatekāmarūpadhṛk || 68 ||

kāraṇaprasamāveśaṃparakāya praveśanaṃ |
aṇimādiguṇāvāptirmanasācāvalokanaṃ || 69 ||

dūrācchravaṇavijñānamadṛśyaṃ bahurūpadhṛk |
saṃtatābhyāsayoge nakhecaratvaṃprajāyate ||70||

jñāninopivimutyaṃtepūrva karmavaśānugāḥ || 71 ||

paśyaṃtopinapaśyaṃtiśṛṇvānābadhirāyathā |
yathāṃdhāmānuṣelokemūḍhāḥ pāpavimohitāḥ || 72 ||

tamevaviditvātimṛtyumetinānyaḥ paṃthāvidyateprāpaṇāya || 73 ||

etatte kathitaṃsamyaktejaso vidhimuttamaṃ |
kālaṃjitvāyathāyogīcāmaratvaṃprapadyate || 74 ||

punaḥparataraṃvakṣye yathāmṭatyurnajāyate |
turīyāddevi bhūtāni yoginodhyāninaḥkadā ||75||

samunnataśarīropisa badhvākarasaṃpuṭaṃ || 76 ||

caṃ cvākāreṇa vakreṇapibanvāyuṃśanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
prasravaṃtikṣaṇādāpastālusthājīvadāyakāḥ ||77||

tājighredvāyunādāyāmmṛtaṃtacchī talaṃjalaṃ |
pivannanudinaṃyogīna mṛtyuvaśagobhavet || 78 ||

divyakāyomahātejāḥ pipāsākṣudvivarjitaḥ ||79||

balenanāga sturagojavenadṛṣṭyāsupūrṇaḥ suśrutistudūrāt |
ākuṃcitākuṃḍalikṛṣṇakeśogaṃdharvavidyādharatulyavarṇaḥ || 80 ||

jīvennarova rpaśataṃ surāṇāṃ sumedhasāvākpatināsamatvaṃ ||81||

punaranyapravakṣyāmividhānaṃ yatsurairapi |
gopitaṃtuprayatnenatacchṛṇuṣvavarā nane ||82||

samākuṃcyābhyasedyogīrasanāṃtālukaṃprati |
kiṃcitkālāṃtareṇaiva kramāvyāpnotilaṃbikāṃ ||83||

|| 84 ||

rephāyamivasūkṣmaṃ laṃbakāghaṃṭikāryakuru |
tataḥ prasravatesātusaṃspṛṣṭvāśītalāṃ sudhāṃ || pivannevasadāyogī somaratvaṃhigancchati ||84||

rephāgraṃlaṃvakāryaṃ kurutala ghaṭanaṃśuṃ padmasyabiṃdu stenāruṣṭaṃ sudhaughaṃpatati parapadedevatānaṃdakāraṃ |
sāraṃsaṃsāratāraṃkṛta kaluṣataraṃ kāraṇaṃ kālatāraṃyene maṃplāvitāṃ gaṃsabhavatiamaraḥkṣutpipāsāvihīnaḥ || 85||

ebhiryukttyācaturbhiḥkṣitidharatanayeyogineyaṃdharāvai dhairyānnityaṃ kutataḥ sakala mapijagadyatsukhaṃ prāyaṇāya |
svapne dehīvidhattesakalamapisadāmāna saṃyacca duḥkhaṃsvargatyavaṃ dharidhyāḥ prabhavaticatatovāsikiṃ ciccaturṇāṃ ||86||

tasmānmaṃtraistapobhirvrataniyamayutairauṣadhairyogayuktā dhātrīraktāmanuṣyairnayavinayayutairdharmavidbhiḥ krameṇa |
bhūtānāmādidevāna hibhavaticalaḥsaṃyutovai caturṇātismāevaṃ pravakṣye vividhamanudinaṃchāyikaṃyacchivākhyaṃ || 87||


|| 65 ||

devyuvāca ||

kathitaṃtesamāsenachā yikaṃjñānamuttamaṃ |
vistareṇasamākhyāhiyoṃgināṃ hitakāmyayā || 1 ||

śaṃkarauvāca ||


sūryahipṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā somaṃvāvaravarṇini |
śuklāṃbaradharaḥ sragvīgaṃ dhadhūpādivāsitaḥ || 3 ||

saṃsmaranme mahāmaṃtraṃ sarvakāmaphalapradaṃ |
navātmakaṃpiṃḍabhūtaṃ svāṃchāyāṃsvanirīkṣayet || 4 ||

dṛṣṭvātāṃ punarākāśeśvetavarṇasvarūpiṇaṃ |
sapaṭhatyeka bhāva stuśivaṃ paramakāraṇaṃ || 5 ||

śi rohīnaṃyadāpaśyatṣatirmāsairbhavennasaḥ ||6||

samastaṃcābhayaṃtasyavidyateyoginastathā |
śukledharmavijānīyātkṛṣṇe pāpaṃvinirdiśet || 7 ||

raktebaṃdhaṃvijānīyātpīte vidveṣamādiśet |
vivāhaubaṃdhunāśaḥ syādviḍe caiva kṣudbhayaṃ ||8||

vikaṭaunaśyatebhāryāvijaṃghedhanamevahi |
pādayorvai vide śaḥsyādityetatkathitaṃmayā || 9 ||

samyaktaṃpuruṣadṛṣṭvā saṃniveśyātmanātmani |
japennavātmakaṃ maṃtraṃ hṛdayaṃ me maheśvari || 10 ||

vatsarevigatemaṃtrītannāstiyanna sādhayet |
aṇimādiguṇānyaṣṭaśaikhecaratvaṃprapadyate || 11 ||

punaranyatpravakṣyāmiśaktijñātuṃ durāsadāṃ |
pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyatelokejñānināmagrataḥ sthitaṃ || 32 ||

ajñeyālikhyate lokeyāsapakṛtakuṃḍalī |
sāmātrā'pā nasaṃsthāpidṛśyatenacapaṭhyate || 13 ||

brahmāṃḍamūrdhnigāyatrīstutāvedaistu nityaśaḥ |
jananīsarvavidyānāṃ gutyavidyetigīyate ||14||

khecarāsāvinirdiṣṭāsarvaprāṇiṣusaṃsthitā |
dṛśyādṛśyācalānityāvyaktāvyaktāsanātanī || 15 ||

avarṇāvarṇasaṃyu ktāprocyate biṃdumālinī |
tapaśyantsarvadāyogīkṛtakṛtyobhijāyate || 16 ||

sarvatīrthakṛtasnānādbhaveddānasya yatphalaṃ |
sarvayajñaphalaṃyaccamālinyādarśanātsadā || 17 ||

prāpnotitannasaṃdehaḥ satyaṃcakathitaṃmayā |
sarvatīrtheṣuyaḥ trātvādatvādā nānisarvaśaḥ || 18 ||

sarveṣadiviyajñānāṃyatphalaṃtalabhetpumān |
tasmājjñānaṃtathāyogamabhyasetsatataṃbudhaḥ || 19 ||

a bhyāsājjāyatesiddhiryogobhyāsātyavartate |
saṃvittirlabhyatebhyāsānmokṣobhyāsātsamaśnute || 20 ||

ityetatkathitaṃdevi bhuktimuktiphalapradaṃ |
kimanyatpṛcchasedevivadasatyaṃ bravīmi || 21 ||


devyuvāca || jñānaniṣṭhāstaponiṣṭhāḥ paṃcāgnibrahmacāriṇaḥ|| dṛśyatevividhāloke kācaiṣāmuttamāgatiḥ

etatsarvasamākhyā hiyadituṣṭāsi me prabho |
śaṃkaraucāca|| śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmiyāthātathyaṃ varānane ||2||

puṣkareparvate kauvātrividhovartatesadā |
uttamādhamamadhyauvānottamobahudhānṛṣu || 3 ||

ātmaśreyonajānaṃtipaśyaṃtopivicakṣuṣaḥ |
sthāvarāḥ pāpamicchaṃticatuṣpādva tumīpsavaḥ || 4 ||

mānavāḥsvargamicchaṃtimokṣamicchaṃtidevatāḥ |
yajñairdānaistapobhistusvargaḥsaṃprāpyatekila || 5 ||

tatrasthā mokṣamicchaṃtimānavāḥpatanāttataḥ |
mokṣamicchetsadādevi sarvadānaṃdadāyakam || 6 ||

brahmāviṣṇurahaṃdevibaddhāḥsmakarmaṇā sadā |
kāmakrodhādibhirdoṣaistasmātsarve anīśvarāḥ || 7 ||

mucyate karmaṇaḥsaṃgājjñāninomokṣa tatparāḥ |
prāpnuvaṃtinacettāṃ tujñānāvātiṃtuyoginaḥ || 8 ||

yāgatiḥpuṇyaśīlānāṃyajvināṃcatapasvinām |
yadinojñānāvāptiḥsyāyogaśāstrāṇiyatna taḥ || 9 ||

adhyaṃtatryānipaurāṇaṃśāstraṃtacchrayatesadā |
pāpaṃ saṃkṣīyatenityaṃ dharmaścaiva vivardhate || 10 ||

kramājjñānaphalāvā tirnasaṃsāraṃ prapadyate |
ataevapurāṇāniśrotavyāniprayatnataḥ || 11 ||

dharmārthakāmalābhāyamokṣa mārgātayetathā |
yajñairdānai stapobhistuyatphalaṃtīrthasevayā || 12 ||

nabhaveyuḥ purāṇānidharmamārgekṣaṇānitu ||13||

yadyatrapaddhatīsyātāmihapāratrikīkatham || 194 ||

vedenadṛṣṭojagatāṃhimārgaḥ paurāṇadharmohisadāvariṣṭhaḥ |
śāstravi nāsarvamidaṃ vibhātisūryeṇa hīna stvivajīva lokaḥ || 35 ||

dānāniniyamāścaiva etaduktaṃ sva yaṃbhuvā || 16 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśatipurāṇānāmataekaṃṭaṇotiyaḥ |
paṭhedvābhaktiyuktastuśṭaṇutasyāpiyatphalam || 17 ||

araṇyeyopyadhīyānaśvāturviyaṃ phalaṃlabhet |
yajñairdānaistapābhistudevitatpaṭhanādataḥ || 18 ||

etatte kathitaṃdevi sudhāvātiphalaṃhitat |
dharmaśāstraṃ hiśrotavyaṃ sarvadharmaphalapradam || 19 ||

sūta uvāca||

etadvaḥ kathitaṃ dharmaṃyathādevenabhāṣitam |
yatpṛcchadhvaṃhitadbhūyaḥkathayāmi purātanam ||20||

śaunakauvāca ||

pādādilyaṃ gaṃmṛddhatidevānāṃ pūjyate sadā |
śaṃkarasye jyate kasmāliṃgaṃśaṃsasva me sadā || 21 ||

etanme brūhi sarvahiyaṃdijānāsi tattvataḥ |
sūtauvāca || sanatkumāreṇapurāyaduktaṃmunisattamāḥ || 22||

tadvakṣyāmimataṃbhūyaḥpārāśaryasya pṛcchataḥ |

brahmāviṣṇuśrvarudraśva samuttasthurjalātkila |
avyaktābhigatisteṣāṃnajānaṃtimanīṣiṇaḥ || 24 ||

pṛthivīgatiḥpūrveṣāṃnaṣṭāsthāvarajaṃgamāḥ |
vāyustīvraḥ samuttasthauśoṣayantsaptasāgarān || 25||

saṃśuṣkāḥ sarvataścāpovinaṣṭājaṃgamāstataḥ |
sthāvarāśvatataḥ sarve saṃśuṣkākramaśaḥ kila || 26 ||

tasthau prāguditaḥ sūryaeko bhūdvitīyaḥ kila |
dakṣiṇāyāṃsamāśāyāṃtadvadāsītkarairyutaḥ || 27||

śoṣayantsarvatastoyaṃ pradahantsacarācaram |
paścimāyāṃ tatastasthauṭatīyaśvottarāṃdiśam || 28 ||

pradahan svaṃ caturthaśvauditaḥ sacarācaram |
upariṣṭāttathācāṣṭāvuditādvādaśaivate || 29 ||

yosāvihasamākhyātorudraḥ kālāgnisaṃjñitaḥ |
samuttasthausapātālātpūrayantsarvatodiśam ||30||

udāraḥ sarvato jñeyaḥskhaṃdhāmapūrvanirmitam || 31 ||

tatomeghāḥ samuttasthurvarṣataḥ sarvatodiśam |
tataste pṛthivīṃ sarvā saṃplāvyādvidiśastadā || 32 ||

nimamajjuśvatatraiva ekārṇavamabhūjjagat |
naḥdharānadiśastatratvaṃtarāpuṣkaraṃ tadā || 33 ||

jñāyate kiṃsvideve hakabaṃdhapari pūrita |
udanmadhyagatāstasthustra yodevāḥ sanātanāḥ || 34 ||

brahmāviṣṇuśvarudraśva avyaktagatayastu te |
tatastāvūcatuḥ śarvajvalitaṃtigmatejasam ||35||

raudraśaktiṃsuvibhrāṇaṃtaṃkaviṣṇūpraṇamya |
prabhutvaṃ sarvato smākaṃ kurusṛṣṭiyathecchayā || 36 ||

kariṣyāmītitāvāhaityuktvāpsumamajjasaḥ |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃtu magnaṃ samabhivīkṣyate || 37 ||

anyonyamūcaturāvāṃkiṃkari pyāvahevinā |
kathaṃtuvartatesṛṣṭistāvevaṃ vedhasaṃhariḥ || 38 ||

provāca kurumevākyaṃ nātrakālātyayo bhavet |
prajāsarganimi taṃhikuruyatnaṃpitāmaha || 39 ||

lokānāṃtvaṃsamarthohisraṣṭuṃvaivividhāḥ prajāḥ |
śaktiṃtehaṃpradāsyāmisraṣṭṛtvepitathātmanaḥ || 40 ||

evaṃ tepitarodevādevāśvapitarastathā || anyonyapitarāṃvaitedevāśvapitaraścaha || 61 ||


sanatkumārauvāca||

catvāro mūrtimaṃtovaitraya caivatyamūrtayaḥ || 1 ||


trīnyajaṃ tedevagaṇāanyānviprādayastathā |
āpyāyayaṃtiyepūrva somaṃyogabalenavai || 3 ||


vanherāpyāyanaṃkṛtvā somasyatuyamasya vai || 5 ||

udgāyanamapyagnāvagnyabhāvepsuvāpunaḥ |

svargamārogyavṛddhicayadanyadapi cepsitaṃ || 7 ||

pitṛbhaktosiviprarṣetenatvamajarāmaraḥ ||8 ||

nayogenagatiḥsātupitṛbhaktasyayāmune |
mārkaṃḍeyauvāca|| evamuktvāsadeveśodevānāmapi durlabham || 9 ||

cakṣurdatvāsavijñānaṃjagāmayogināṃgatim |
śṛṇubhī ṣmapurābhṛdyadbhāradvājātmajādvijāḥ ||1 ||

yogadharmamanuprāpyaśvaṣṭāduścaritena vai |


pitaryuparate sarve pravasaṃtastadābhavan |
viniyogādgurostasyagāṃdogdhīṃsamakālayan || 13 ||




sasarvānabravītkopātpitṛbhaktisamanvitaḥ |


pitṝnabhyarcyadharmeṇanādharmosmānbhaviṣyati |
evamukāśvate sarve mokṣayitvācagāṃtadā || 19 ||

pitṛbhyaḥ kalpayitvātuupayuṃjaṃtabhārata |
upayujyacagāṃsarve guroranyavedayan || 20 ||

ārjavātsatutaṃ vatsaṃpratijagrāhavaidvijaḥ || 21 ||

mithyopacārataḥ pāpamabhūttepāṃ cagopratāṃ |
kālena samayujyaṃta bherīta upayujya ca || 22 ||

goḥ sarvatasyahaṃtāraḥ sarve tesvāyupaḥ kṣaye |

lubdhakasya sutāstātabalavaṃtomanasvinaḥ || 24||


trāsodvegenasaṃvignāramyekālaṃjaregirau |
tamevārthamanudhyātojñānaṃ maraṇasaṃbhavam || 26 ||


tathaiva casthitābuddhau saṃsāreṣvanivartanam || 28 ||




tyaktvāsahacarīdharmamunayodharmacāriṇaḥ |
viprayonauyadāmohānmithyāpacaritaṃgurau || 32||

niṣṭattinirvatāścaivaśakunānāmataḥ smṛtāḥ || 33 ||

jātismarāḥ susaṃbaddhāḥ saptaivabrahmacāriṇaḥ || 34||

viprayonauyadāmohānmithyāpacaritaṃgurau |
tiryagyo॒natathājanmaśrāddhājjñānaṃtulebhire || 35 ||

tathātupitṛkāryārtharutaṃ śrāddhaṃvyavasthitau |
tathājñānaṃcajātaṃcakramāvyāptaṃguṇottaram || 36 ||





yadyastisukṛtaṃ kiṃcittapovāniyamopivā || 41 ||

khinnohamupavāsena tapasāniṣphalenaca |

tathetyuktasya tasyāsīttadāyogātmanomatiḥ || 43 ||

yasmātkāmaṃbuvāṇastvaṃyogadharmamavāpyatam || 44 ||

avaraṃvaraṃprārthayasetasmādvākyaṃ nibodhame |

bhaviṣyaṃtaḥsakhāyaudvāvimautesacivauvarau |

tetānūcukhayorājaṃśvaturaḥ sahacāriṇaḥ |
prasādaṃtepunaścakrustanmatesumanābravīt || 47 ||

śrātarvobhavitāśāpaḥpunaryogamavāpsyatha |
sarvasatvarutajñaśvasvatantroyaṃbhaviṣyati || 48 ||

pitṛprasādoyuṣmābhiraprāptaḥ suhṛdobhavet |




mārkaṃḍeyauvāca ||


krīḍayitvāciraṃtatra sabhāryaḥ svapuraṃyayau || 2 ||

taṃviśvājaḥ sutaṃrājye sthāpayitvāvanaṃ yayau || 3 ||


tatoviśvājitaṃtenaviśvājaṃnāmataddanaṃ |

yogaśraṣṭāstrayaścaiva dehatyāgarutobhavan |
kāṃpilyena gare tetubrahmadattapurogamāḥ ||6||

smṛtimaṃtotracatvārastrayastuparimohitāḥ || 7 ||

chidradarśīsunetrastu vedavedāṃgapāragau || 8 ||

jātau śrotriyadāyādau pūrvajātisahoṣitau |

dvivedaṃpuṃḍarī kaśvacchaṃdogādhvaryurevaca |
tatonūhaḥ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvābrahmadattamakalmaṣaṃ || 10 ||

pāṃcālaḥpuṃḍarīkastuputrausaṃsthāpya maṃdire || 11 ||

viviśaturvanaṃ tatra gatauparamikāṃgatiṃ |
brahmadattasya bhāryātusannatirnāmabhārata || 12 ||

sātvekabhāvasaṃyuktāreme bhāryāsahaivatu |

jātāśrotriyadāyādādaridrasya kulennṛpa |


āmaṃtrayitvāsminviṣṇoḥ padaṃparipraṇamyatu |
śūrāyesaṃprapadyaṃte apunarbhavakāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 16 ||


kṛte samyagidaṃ bhaktyāpaṭhaṃcchraddhāsamanvitaḥ |

uccāryamāṇaetasmindevadevasya saṃstave |

tasmātsaṃcititepāpesamanaṃta ramevaca |
japtavyametatpāpasyapraśamāyamahāmune || 20 ||

yosyacārthamimaṃdhyāyanpaṭheccainaṃ śṛṇotivā |


vyāsapūjanam |
ācāryatvaṃmahāviṣṇuvyāsarūpanamostute || prasannetvayivipreṃdraprasannomesadāśivaḥ || 1||

graṃthāṃtevidhivada yādvidvadbhyo bhūridakṣiṇām |
tatovaktāramānamya saṃpūjyacayathāvidhi || 2||

bhūṣaṇairhasta karṇānāṃva trairhemādibhiḥ sudhīḥ |
śiva pūjāsamāptautudadyāddhenuṃsavatsa kām ||3||

śrutesiṃhaṃ suvarṇasya palamānasya soṃbare |
ācāryāyasudhīrdayādbhaktiḥsyādbhavabaṃdha naiḥ ||4||

vidhānasahitaṃsamyakpurāṇaṃ phaladaṃśrutam |
tasmādvidhānayuktaṃtupurāṇaṃ phalamuttamam || 5 ||


itidharmasaṃhitāsamāptā
